# Recursive Harness Self-Improvement：中英逐页对照

> 本文件用于译文校对；每一页先列英文提取文本，再列中文译文。图像/矢量图中的坐标标签可能只在原 PDF 中可见。

## 第 1 页

### English (extracted)

2026-7-20
Recursive Harness Self-Improvement
Hyunin Lee1,2, Jinglue Xu1, Jeffrey Seely1, Donghyun Lee2, Matei Zaharia2 and Yujin Tang1 1Sakana AI, 2UC Berkeley Under model–harness co-evolution, harnesses are not merely inference-time scaffolds but datagenerating components whose execution traces can shape future foundation models. This motivates harness-in-the-loop learning: optimizing harnesses for both immediate agent performance and the quality of traces used for future model training. However, continually updating provider-built scaffolds is costly and labor-intensive. We therefore investigate whether optimizing user-constructed harnesses in a task-specific manner can improve execution-trace quality while remaining computationally lightweight and requiring only a few update iterations. To this end, we introduce Recursive Harness Self-Improvement (RHI), which represents the harness as a prompt-level specification of the agent loop and iteratively refines it using pairwise feedback over its own revision history. Across 30 synthetic machine-learning research tasks spanning quantitative finance, robotics, and pharmacy, a few RHI iterations suffice to substantially raise the performance ceiling of low-reasoning-effort agents, exceeding the corresponding maximum-reasoning-effort setting while reducing inference cost by up to 60%. We show that these gains arise primarily from improved task-specific context management through more effective inter-agent information flow rather than longer reasoning traces. Finally, we formalize this behavior as an information-theoretic hypothesis for RHI’s implicit optimization objective, suggesting RHI as a practical algorithm for continual learning within the
paradigm of model–harness co-evolution.

### 中文（翻译）

2026-7-20
递归式 Harness 自改进(Recursive Harness Self-Improvement)
Hyunin Lee1,2, Jinglue Xu1, Jeffrey Seely1, Donghyun Lee2, Matei Zaharia2 与 Yujin Tang1 1Sakana AI, 2UC Berkeley 在模型–harness 协同演化的范式下,harness 不仅是推理时的脚手架,更是生成数据的组件——其执行轨迹(execution trace)可塑造未来的基础模型。这促使我们提出"harness 在环"学习:同时针对即时的智能体(agent)性能以及用于未来模型训练的轨迹质量来优化 harness。然而,持续更新由服务提供商构建的脚手架代价高昂且劳动密集。为此,我们探究:以任务特定方式优化用户自建的 harness,能否在提升执行轨迹质量的同时保持计算轻量化,且仅需少量更新迭代。为实现这一目标,我们提出递归式 Harness 自改进(Recursive Harness Self-Improvement,RHI):将 harness 表示为智能体循环的提示词(prompt)级规范,并利用其自身修订历史上的成对反馈对其进行迭代精化。在横跨量化金融、机器人和药学领域的 30 项合成机器学习研究任务上,仅需少量 RHI 迭代即可大幅提升低推理开销智能体的性能上限,超越相应的最大推理开销设置,同时将推理成本降低高达 60%。我们表明,这些性能增益主要来源于通过更有效的智能体间信息流动实现的任务特定上下文管理的改进,而非更长的推理轨迹。最后,我们将这一行为形式化为 RHI 隐式优化目标的信息论假设,表明 RHI 是模型–harness 协同演化范式下持续学习的一种实用算法。

## 第 3 页

### English (extracted)

1. Introduction
Scaling progress in AI is typically attributed to larger models (Kaplan et al., 2020), increased training compute (Hoffmann et al., 2022), and improved post-training (Guo et al., 2025). Increasingly, however, advances in deployed AI systems arise from the co-evolution of foundation models and harnesses, rather than from foundation models alone (Anthropic, 2026b; OpenAI, 2026a). This co-evolution requires a recursive feedback loop: for a fixed foundation model, stronger harnesses organize more effective agent workflows that generate substantially higher-quality execution traces than the standalone model can produce, which can subsequently serve as training data for future foundation models (Lin et al., 2026). Modern coding agents generate such high-quality execution traces by leveraging harnesses from two complementary sources: providers ship built-in, general-purpose harnesses for coding and multi-agent workflows (Anthropic, 2025b; Google, 2025; LangChain, 2024; OpenAI, 2024b, 2025b), while users further specialize these systems with task-specific harnesses, including reusable skills, subagents, and automation workflows (Anthropic, 2026c,d,e; n8n, 2026; OpenAI, 2026b). Consequently, a key bottleneck in this harness–model co-evolutionary loop is the quality of the execution traces produced by the harness. This work focuses on the first half of this recursive loop: for a fixed foundation model, how to
improve the harness to generate higher-quality execution traces.
Improving harnesses, however, is challenging in practice. Provider-built harnesses must generalize across diverse users and tasks, making continual updates prohibitively labor- and cost-intensive (Anthropic, 2026b; OpenAI, 2026a; Shang et al., 2025; Zhang et al., 2026, 2025c). By contrast, user-constructed harnesses can be optimized for individual tasks, making them a practical target for improving execution-trace quality (Anthropic, 2026d,e; n8n, 2026; OpenAI, 2026b). Moreover, for such optimization to be practical, each update should be computationally lightweight, and the optimization process should converge within only a few update iterations, enabling users to specialize agents to many open-ended tasks under limited per-task budgets. Yet how to achieve such lightweight, iterative harness optimization remains largely unexplored. To address this execution-trace quality bottleneck, we investigate whether a few iterations of lightweight optimization of a user-constructed harness can raise the performance plateaus of agent test-time scaling (Wang et al., 2025a). To this end, we introduce Recursive Harness Self-Improvement (RHI), which defines the harness as the agent loop itself, including the roles assigned to agents, the instructions they follow, the information exchanged between agents (contracts), and the workflow structure governing when reasoning is invoked (hops). Roles and instructions define task allocation, contracts specify interagent communication, and hops determine the control flow of the orchestrator–subagent workflow. RHI then iteratively refines the harness using pairwise feedback over its own revision history (Section 3). First, to enable efficient optimization of user-constructed harnesses, our key design choice is to treat the harness as a prompt-level object rather than executable code. Second, RHI is lightweight because it replaces expensive population-level harness search with trajectory-local self-comparison: at each iteration, the current harness is compared with its immediate predecessor, and the resulting preference history guides the next revision. Third, RHI requires only a few update iterations to substantially raise the performance plateaus of a low-reasoning-effort agent,
often exceeding the corresponding maximum-reasoning-effort setting.
We evaluate RHI on 30 synthetic, open-ended machine-learning research tasks spanning quantitative finance, robotics, and pharmacy. Each task requires generating a complete code repository with standardized deliverables, and outputs are evaluated using pairwise LLM-as-a-judge comparisons. Across sonnet-4.6, opus-4.7, and opus-4.8, few-shot RHI applied to highreasoning agents achieves higher win rates than all stronger test-time-scaling settings, including xhigh, max, and ultracode. These gains persist across progressively stronger foundation models. For opus-4.8, RHI also reduces inference cost by up to 60% relative to the ultracode

### 中文（翻译）

# 1. 引言

AI 的规模化进展通常归因于更大的模型(Kaplan et al., 2020)、更多的训练计算量(Hoffmann et al., 2022)以及改进的后训练方法(Guo et al., 2025)。然而,越来越多的迹象表明,已部署 AI 系统的进步源于基础模型与工作流框架(harness)的协同演化,而非单纯依赖基础模型本身(Anthropic, 2026b; OpenAI, 2026a)。这种协同演化需要一个递归反馈循环:在固定基础模型的前提下,更强的工作流框架能够组织更高效的智能体(agent)工作流(workflow),从而生成质量远高于独立模型的执行轨迹;这些执行轨迹随后可作为未来基础模型的训练数据(Lin et al., 2026)。现代编程智能体通过利用两类互补来源的工作流框架来生成此类高质量执行轨迹:服务提供商内置通用的编程与多智能体工作流框架(Anthropic, 2025b; Google, 2025; LangChain, 2024; OpenAI, 2024b, 2025b),而用户则进一步以特定任务的工作流框架对这些系统进行专化,包括可复用技能、子智能体和自动化工作流(Anthropic, 2026c,d,e; n8n, 2026; OpenAI, 2026b)。因此,这一工作流框架–模型协同演化循环的关键瓶颈在于工作流框架所产生执行轨迹的质量。本文聚焦于该递归循环的前半段:在固定基础模型的前提下,如何改进工作流框架以生成更高质量的执行轨迹。

然而,在实践中改进工作流框架颇具挑战。服务提供商构建的工作流框架需要泛化到多样化的用户与任务,使得持续更新在人力和成本上代价高昂(Anthropic, 2026b; OpenAI, 2026a; Shang et al., 2025; Zhang et al., 2026, 2025c)。相比之下,用户构建的工作流框架可针对特定任务进行优化,成为提升执行轨迹质量的实际目标(Anthropic, 2026d,e; n8n, 2026; OpenAI, 2026b)。此外,为使优化具有实用性,每次更新应在计算上保持轻量,且优化过程应在少量更新迭代内收敛,从而使用户能够在有限的单任务预算下将智能体专化到众多开放式任务。然而,如何实现此类轻量化、迭代式的工作流框架优化在很大程度上仍未被探索。

为解决这一执行轨迹质量瓶颈,我们研究了对用户构建的工作流框架进行少量轻量化优化迭代,是否能够提升智能体测试时扩展(test-time scaling)的性能上限(Wang et al., 2025a)。为此,我们提出递归工作流框架自改进(Recursive Harness Self-Improvement,RHI),将工作流框架定义为智能体循环本身,包括分配给各智能体的角色、其遵循的指令、智能体间交换的信息(契约,contract),以及控制何时调用推理的工作流结构(跳转,hop)。角色与指令定义任务分配,contract 规定智能体间通信,hop 决定编排器–子智能体工作流的控制流。RHI 随后利用基于自身修订历史的成对反馈对工作流框架进行迭代精化(第 3 节)。首先,为实现用户构建工作流框架的高效优化,我们的核心设计选择是将工作流框架视为提示词(prompt)层面的对象,而非可执行代码。其次,RHI 轻量化的原因在于:它以轨迹局部自比较取代昂贵的种群级工作流框架搜索——在每次迭代中,当前工作流框架与其直接前驱进行比较,所得偏好历史引导下一次修订。第三,RHI 仅需少量更新迭代便可大幅提升低推理力度智能体的性能上限,往往超越相应的最大推理力度设置。

我们在 30 个涵盖量化金融、机器人技术和药学的合成开放式机器学习研究任务上对 RHI 进行评估。每项任务要求生成包含标准化交付物的完整代码仓库,输出使用成对 LLM-as-a-judge 比较进行评估。在 sonnet-4.6、opus-4.7 和 opus-4.8 上,应用于高推理力度智能体的少样本 RHI 比所有更强的测试时扩展设置(包括 xhigh、max 和 ultracode)取得了更高的胜率。这些增益在逐步更强的基础模型上持续存在。对于 opus-4.8,RHI 相对于 ultracode

## 第 4 页

### English (extracted)

baseline (Figure 1; Section 5). Our ablation studies show that these gains do not arise from generating longer outputs: across RHI iterations, output-token usage remains nearly constant while performance improves, and cache read/write usage and inference cost often decrease. Instead, the results suggest that RHI improves task-specific context management: communication contracts and workflow hops become more task-specific, task-relevant information encoded in these components increases, and redundancy across harness components decreases (Section 6). Furthermore, we observe that RHI’s learning dynamics are consistent with an implicit objective that reorganizes information flow across harness components. We formalize this observation as an information-theoretic hypothesis for RHI’s implicit update objective–increasing the mutual information between each component and the task while penalizing redundancy across components–yielding a testable account of RHI’s implicit updates. Overall, this paper argues that future progress in harness–model co-evolution should focus on improving the data flywheel. To advance the first half of this loop, which optimizes userconstructed harnesses for task-specific execution trace generation. We envision RHI as a practical framework that enables users to continually specialize coding agents for diverse open-ended tasks
encountered in everyday use.
2. Preliminary
We first define the harness optimization problem in Section 2.1 and then review existing methods
in Section 2.2.
2.1. Problem definition
Formally, let V denote the vocabulary (token) space, and let V∗ := V ×V × ··· denote the set of all finite-length token sequences (i.e., sentences). Let A be a coding agent with a fixed language model L, and let 𝑥 ∈ X ⊂ V∗ denote a task prompt. Let H denote the space of harnesses, and let Y denote the space of code repositories, where each repository is represented as a collection of file-path/content pairs. Given a harness 𝐻 ∈ H, the corresponding agent produces an output 𝑦 ∼ A(𝐻, 𝑥), where 𝑦 ∈ Y is the repository generated by the agentic execution procedure induced by the language model L, the harness 𝐻, and the task 𝑥. Our evaluation protocol is motivated by open-ended coding tasks encountered in everyday use, where the output is a complete code repository rather than a single, scalar verifiable answer. Such repositories cannot be reliably evaluated using a single metric because they must be assessed across multiple criteria, including functional correctness, task alignment, reproducibility, and code quality. Moreover, these criteria are often difficult to aggregate into a single numerical score but are naturally expressed through pairwise preferences between outputs. We therefore adopt a pairwise evaluation protocol conditioned on an evaluation prompt 𝑥eval ∈ V∗ that multiple criteria. Given two outputs 𝑦1, 𝑦2 ∈ Y, the evaluator returns one of three possible judgments: Leval(𝑦1, 𝑦2, 𝑥eval) ∈ {𝑦1 ≻ 𝑦2, 𝑦1 ∼ 𝑦2, 𝑦2 ≻ 𝑦1}, where 𝑦1 ≻ 𝑦2 denotes that 𝑦1 is preferred to 𝑦2 under the evaluation criteria specified by 𝑥eval, and 𝑦1 ∼ 𝑦2 denotes that the two outputs are judged equivalent. Here, Leval denotes an LLM-based evaluator, which may be different from the coding agent’s underlying language model L. Given a task 𝑥 ∈ X and an evaluation prompt 𝑥eval, our ideal objective is to identify a task-specific harness whose outputs are preferred to those generated by competing harnesses. A natural
population objective is

### 中文（翻译）

基准还将推理成本降低了最多 60%(图 1;第 5 节)。我们的消融研究表明,这些增益并非源于生成更长的输出:在 RHI 各迭代轮次中,输出 token 用量几乎保持不变,而性能持续提升,缓存读写用量和推理成本往往也有所下降。相反,结果表明 RHI 改善了特定任务的上下文管理:通信 contract 和工作流 hop 变得更加任务专属,编码在这些组件中的任务相关信息增加,工作流框架各组件间的冗余减少(第 6 节)。此外,我们观察到 RHI 的学习动态与一个隐式目标一致,该目标对工作流框架各组件间的信息流进行重组。我们将这一观察形式化为 RHI 隐式更新目标的信息论假说——增大每个组件与任务之间的互信息,同时惩罚各组件间的冗余——从而为 RHI 的隐式更新提供了可检验的理论解释。

总体而言,本文认为工作流框架–模型协同演化的未来进展应聚焦于改进数据飞轮。为推进该循环的前半段——针对特定任务执行轨迹生成优化用户构建的工作流框架——我们将 RHI 定位为一个实用框架,使用户能够持续将编程智能体专化到日常使用中遇到的各类开放式任务。

# 2. 预备知识

我们首先在第 2.1 节中定义工作流框架优化问题,然后在第 2.2 节中回顾现有方法。

## 2.1. 问题定义

形式上,令 $V$ 表示词汇(token)空间,令 $V^* := V \times V \times \cdots$ 表示所有有限长度 token 序列(即句子)的集合。令 $A$ 为一个具有固定语言模型 $L$ 的编程智能体,令 $x \in \mathcal{X} \subset V^*$ 表示任务提示词。令 $\mathcal{H}$ 表示工作流框架空间,令 $\mathcal{Y}$ 表示代码仓库空间,其中每个仓库表示为文件路径/内容对的集合。给定工作流框架 $H \in \mathcal{H}$,相应的智能体生成输出 $y \sim A(H, x)$,其中 $y \in \mathcal{Y}$ 是由语言模型 $L$、工作流框架 $H$ 和任务 $x$ 所诱导的智能体执行过程生成的仓库。

我们的评估协议面向日常使用中遇到的开放式编程任务,其中输出是完整的代码仓库,而非单一的标量可验证答案。此类仓库无法使用单一指标进行可靠评估,因为需要从多个维度进行评估,包括功能正确性、任务对齐性、可复现性和代码质量。此外,这些标准往往难以聚合为单一数值分数,但通过输出间的成对偏好来表达则更为自然。因此,我们采用以评估提示词 $x_\text{eval} \in V^*$ 为条件、覆盖多项标准的成对评估协议。给定两个输出 $y_1, y_2 \in \mathcal{Y}$,评估器返回三种可能判断之一:

$$\mathcal{L}_\text{eval}(y_1, y_2, x_\text{eval}) \in \{y_1 \succ y_2,\ y_1 \sim y_2,\ y_2 \succ y_1\}$$

其中 $y_1 \succ y_2$ 表示在 $x_\text{eval}$ 所指定的评估标准下 $y_1$ 优于 $y_2$,$y_1 \sim y_2$ 表示两个输出被判定为等价。此处 $\mathcal{L}_\text{eval}$ 表示基于 LLM 的评估器,其可以不同于编程智能体底层所用的语言模型 $L$。

给定任务 $x \in \mathcal{X}$ 和评估提示词 $x_\text{eval}$,我们的理想目标是识别出一个特定任务的工作流框架,使其输出优于竞争工作流框架所生成的输出。一个自然的种群目标为

$$H^*_x \in \operatorname*{argmax}_{H \in \mathcal{H}} f_x(H), \quad f_x(H) = \mathbb{E}_{H' \sim \mu(\cdot | H' \neq H),\ y \sim A(H,x),\ y' \sim A(H',x)} \!\left[\mathbf{1}\!\left\{\mathcal{L}_\text{eval}(y, y'; x_\text{eval}) = y \succ y'\right\}\right], \tag{1}$$

其中 $\mu$ 是竞争工作流框架上的参考分布,$\mathbf{1}{\cdot}$ 表示示性函数。

which equals 1 if the enclosed predicate is true and 0 otherwise. The function 𝑓𝑥 (𝐻) denotes the expected pairwise win rate of harness 𝐻 against competing harnesses under the evaluation prompt 𝑥eval. Accordingly, 𝐻∗ 𝑥 is the harness that maximizes this expected win rate.
2.2. Existing methods
Exact maximization of Equation (1) would require searching over a discrete, high-dimensional harness space and estimating the expected pairwise win rate of each candidate against harnesses drawn from the reference distribution 𝜇. Prior work typically makes this optimization tractable in two ways: by restricting the search space to a concrete representation, such as prompts, workflow graphs, tool-use policies, or executable harness code, and by replacing the expectation over 𝜇
with a finite candidate set that can be evaluated.
In the notation of Equation (1), population-based harness, workflow, prompt, or program search methods can be viewed as replacing the reference distribution 𝜇 with a sampled population S𝑖 = {𝐻𝑖,1, . . . , 𝐻𝑖,𝑚} and estimating b 𝑓𝑥 (𝐻;S𝑖) = |S𝑖| − 1 ∑︁ 𝐻′∈S𝑖\{𝐻} 𝔼𝑦∼A(𝐻,𝑥), 𝑦′∼A(𝐻′,𝑥) [1{Leval(𝑦, 𝑦′ ;𝑥eval) = 𝑦 ≻ 𝑦′ }] . (2) Different algorithms instantiate the population S𝑖 at different levels of abstraction. At the harness level, Meta-Harness treats the harness code itself as the candidate object and proposes new harnesses from the source, scores, and execution traces of prior candidates (Lee et al., 2026), while AutoHarness automatically synthesizes a code harness and refines it from execution feedback (Lou et al., 2026). TTHE applies the same harness-level pattern at test time: on each unlabeled test batch, it instantiates S𝑖 as a population of parallel harness branches evolved over multiple rounds, and an agentic judge commits one branch to the next batch using execution-derived proxy signals as the scoring rule (Nie et al., 2026). At the agent and workflow level, ADAS, through Meta Agent Search, maintains an archive of code-defined agents and uses a meta-agent to program new agents from that archive (Hu et al., 2025); GPTSwarm represents agents as optimizable graphs and updates node prompts and graph connectivity (Zhuge et al., 2024); and AFlow searches over code-represented workflows using Monte Carlo tree search with code modification and execution feedback (Zhang et al., 2025c). At the program level, AlphaEvolve and ShinkaEvolve instantiate the same pattern over executable code: an LLM mutates or rewrites candidates, an evaluator scores them, and selection or parent sampling decides which candidates seed the next generation (Lange et al., 2025; Novikov et al., 2025). In each case, the method does not optimize Equation (1) exactly. Instead, it constructs a finite candidate set S𝑖 and ranks its members with a task-specific score, evaluator, or pairwise judgment; when the scoring rule is order-consistent with the preference ≻, this ranking recovers the within-population win rate b 𝑓𝑥 (·;S𝑖) of Equation (2) up to a monotone transformation, so selecting or evolving the top candidates approximately maximizes the finite-population approximation before reseeding S𝑖+1. Prompt and pipeline optimizers can be interpreted as the same finite-population approximation with a more restricted feasible set S𝑖 of textual prompts or modular LLM programs. OPRO treats the prompt as the optimization variable and asks an LLM optimizer to propose improved prompts from previous prompt–score pairs (Yang et al., 2024). TextGrad converts textual feedback into natural-language gradients that revise text variables (Yuksekgonul et al., 2024). DSPy compiles modular LM programs by searching over prompts and few-shot demonstrations (Khattab et al., 2023). GEPA maintains a Pareto frontier of prompt candidates, reflects on execution trajectories, and proposes, tests, and recombines prompt edits (Agrawal et al., 2025), and optimize_anything generalizes this reflective text-optimization view to arbitrary text parameters (Agrawal et al., 2026). All of these are natural approximations to Equation (1) when many candidates can be instantiated and evaluated. Their common limitation in our setting is that the fidelity of b 𝑓𝑥 (·;S𝑖)

## 2.2 现有方法

精确最大化式(1)需要在离散、高维的 harness 空间中搜索,并估计每个候选项对参考分布 $\mu$ 中抽取的 harness 的期望成对胜率。先前工作通常通过两种方式使该优化可行:将搜索空间限定为具体表示形式(如提示词、工作流(workflow)图、工具使用策略或可执行的 harness 代码),以及用一个有限候选集替换对 $\mu$ 的期望以便求值。

在式(1)的符号体系中,基于种群的 harness、workflow、prompt 或程序搜索方法,可视为将参考分布 $\mu$ 替换为采样种群 $S_i = \{H_{i,1}, \ldots, H_{i,m}\}$,并估计

$$\hat{f}_x(H; S_i) = |S_i|^{-1} \sum_{H' \in S_i \setminus \{H\}} \mathbb{E}_{y \sim A(H,x),\, y' \sim A(H',x)} \bigl[1\{L_{\text{eval}}(y, y'; x_{\text{eval}}) = y \succ y'\}\bigr]. \tag{2}$$

不同算法在不同抽象层次上实例化种群 $S_i$。

在 harness 层面,Meta-Harness 将 harness 代码本身作为候选对象,并从先前候选项的源码、分数和执行轨迹中提出新 harness(Lee et al., 2026);AutoHarness 自动合成一套代码 harness,并根据执行反馈加以精化(Lou et al., 2026)。TTHE 在测试时应用相同的 harness 级模式:对每个无标签测试批次,它将 $S_i$ 实例化为经多轮演化的并行 harness 分支种群,并由一个智能体(agent)评判者利用执行衍生的代理信号作为评分规则,将某一分支提交至下一批次(Nie et al., 2026)。

在 agent 与 workflow 层面,ADAS 通过 Meta Agent Search 维护一个由代码定义的 agent 档案库,并使用元智能体(meta-agent)从该档案库中编程出新 agent(Hu et al., 2025);GPTSwarm 将 agent 表示为可优化图,并更新节点提示词与图的连通性(Zhuge et al., 2024);AFlow 使用蒙特卡洛树搜索结合代码修改和执行反馈,对以代码表示的 workflow 进行搜索(Zhang et al., 2025c)。

在程序层面,AlphaEvolve 与 ShinkaEvolve 在可执行代码上实例化相同模式:大语言模型(LLM)对候选项进行变异或重写,评估器为其打分,再由选择或父代采样决定哪些候选项作为下一代的种子(Lange et al., 2025; Novikov et al., 2025)。

在上述每种情形中,方法均未精确优化式(1)。它们构建有限候选集 $S_i$,并以任务特定的分数、评估器或成对判断对其成员排序;当评分规则与偏序 $\succ$ 保序时,该排序在单调变换意义下恢复了式(2)中种群内胜率 $\hat{f}_x(\cdot; S_i)$,从而在重新播种 $S_{i+1}$ 之前,对顶部候选项的选择或演化近似最大化了有限种群近似。提示词与流水线优化器可解释为相同的有限种群近似,只是将可行集 $S_i$ 限定为文本提示词或模块化 LLM 程序。OPRO 将提示词视为优化变量,并请 LLM 优化器根据先前的提示词–分数对提出改进提示词(Yang et al., 2024)。TextGrad 将文本反馈转化为自然语言梯度以修订文本变量(Yuksekgonul et al., 2024)。DSPy 通过搜索提示词和少样本示例来编译模块化语言模型程序(Khattab et al., 2023)。GEPA 维护一个提示词候选项的帕累托前沿,反思执行轨迹,并提出、测试和重组提示词编辑(Agrawal et al., 2025);optimize_anything 将这种反思式文本优化视角推广至任意文本参数(Agrawal et al., 2026)。当可以实例化和评估大量候选项时,以上方法均为式(1)的自然近似。它们在本文场景下的共同局限在于:$\hat{f}_x(\cdot; S_i)$ 的保真度

grows with the size and diversity of S𝑖, yet every additional candidate requires a fresh, expensive
black-box agent execution and evaluation.
Such population-based search is ill-suited to user-constructed harness optimization because every additional candidate requires a full black-box agent execution and evaluation. For users continually specializing agents to many open-ended tasks, this cost is prohibitive. Indeed, Wang et al. (2026) show that once this search cost is properly accounted for, automatic harness evolution fails to consistently outperform simple test-time scaling baselines such as parallel sampling and sequentialrefinementunderfixedinferencebudgets. Harnessoptimizationisthereforeworthwhile only when its search overhead is far smaller than that of population-based methods. Consequently, our setting calls for an optimization method that is computationally lightweight, converges within only a few update iterations, and nevertheless yields substantial performance improvements.
3. Recursive Harness Self-Improvement
This section introduces our proposed algorithm, Recursive Harness Self-Improvement (RHI). Section 3.1 presents a computationally lightweight relaxation of the RHI objective, Section 3.2 describes the RHI algorithm, and Section 3.3 defines the harness representation used by RHI. Figure 2 provides a high-level overview of RHI, and Algorithm 1 presents the complete proce-

3.1. RHI objective function
Unlike the methods discussed in Section 2.2, RHI adopts a trajectory-local relaxation of Equation (1). At iteration 𝑖, it replaces the broad competitor distribution 𝜇 with a local competitor distribution concentrated on the previous harness, 𝜈𝑖 = 𝛿𝐻 (𝑖−1) 𝑥
. The corresponding local preference
objective is

(3) This trajectory-local objective is not an unbiased estimator of 𝑓𝑥 (𝐻) under the original 𝜇; rather, it deliberately changes the problem from global comparison against arbitrary harnesses to local improvement over the system’s own previous harness. This distinction separates Meta-Harness (Lee et al., 2026) from Self-Harness (Zhang et al., 2026). Meta-Harness is better viewed as optimizing the finite-candidate approximation in Equation (2): it maintains a population and Pareto frontier of evaluated harnesses, stores each candidate’s source code, scores, and execution

这种基于种群的搜索不适合用户构建的 harness 优化,因为每新增一个候选项都需要完整的黑盒 agent 执行与评估。对于持续将 agent 特化到众多开放式任务的用户而言,这一代价是难以承受的。Wang et al.(2026)确实表明,一旦将搜索成本纳入合理核算,自动 harness 演化在固定推理预算下未能持续超越并行采样、顺序精化等简单测试时缩放基线。因此,harness 优化只有在其搜索开销远低于基于种群方法时才值得进行。由此,本文场景需要一种计算轻量、在少数更新迭代内即可收敛、且仍能带来显著性能提升的优化方法。

## 3. 递归 Harness 自改进

本节介绍所提算法——递归 Harness 自改进(Recursive Harness Self-Improvement,RHI)。第 3.1 节给出 RHI 目标函数的计算轻量松弛形式,第 3.2 节描述 RHI 算法,第 3.3 节定义 RHI 使用的 harness 表示。图 2 提供 RHI 的高层概览,算法 1 给出完整流程。

$$
\boxed{
\text{TASK} \xrightarrow{\text{12}} \text{Harness}[i] \to \text{output}[i] \xrightarrow{\text{3 自比较}} \text{output}[i] \text{ vs } \text{output}[i{-}1] \xrightarrow{\text{4}} \text{P.F}[i] \xrightarrow{\text{5}} \text{更新 Harness}
}
$$

**图 2 | 递归 Harness 自改进(RHI)。** 12:在第 $i$ 次迭代中,编码 agent 接收任务 $x$ 和当前 harness $H^{(i)}$,求解任务并产生输出 output[$i$]。3:LLM 评估器随后将 output[$i$] 与前一轮输出 output[$i{-}1$] 进行比较,返回偏好反馈(Preference Feedback)P.F[$i$]。4:该反馈存入自比较(self-comparison)历史记录。5:最后,LLM harness 优化器利用该历史记录将 harness 从 $H^{(i)}$ 更新为 $H^{(i+1)}$。

### 3.1 RHI 目标函数

与第 2.2 节讨论的方法不同,RHI 对式(1)采用轨迹局部(trajectory-local)松弛。在第 $i$ 次迭代中,它将宽泛的竞争者分布 $\mu$ 替换为集中于前一 harness 的局部竞争者分布 $\nu_i = \delta_{H^{(i-1)}_x}$。对应的局部偏好目标为

$$\tilde{f}^{(i)}_x(H) = \mathbb{E}_{y \sim A(H,x),\, y^- \sim A(H^{(i-1)}_x, x)} \bigl[1\{L_{\text{eval}}(y, y^-; x_{\text{eval}}) = y \succ y^-\}\bigr]. \tag{3}$$

该轨迹局部目标并非原始 $\mu$ 下 $f_x(H)$ 的无偏估计量;它有意将问题从与任意 harness 的全局比较转变为相对于系统自身前一 harness 的局部改进。这一区别将 Meta-Harness(Lee et al., 2026)与 Self-Harness(Zhang et al., 2026)区分开来。Meta-Harness 更宜被视为优化式(2)中的有限候选近似:它维护一个已评估 harness 的种群和帕累托前沿,存储每个候选项的源码、分数与执行

## 第 7 页

### English (extracted)

traces in a filesystem, and imposes no parent-selection rule, so the proposer may inspect any prior harness and trace when proposing a new harness (Lee et al., 2026). It therefore does not instantiate Equation (3), whose comparator is only the immediately previous harness 𝐻 (𝑖−1) 𝑥 . Self-Harness is closer to Equation (3): in each round, the current harness is evaluated, failures are mined from its execution traces, candidate edits are proposed, and each candidate is regressiontested against the current harness on held-in and held-out splits (Zhang et al., 2026). However, Self-Harness is not identical to RHI’s trajectory-local objective. It may evaluate multiple candidate branches and merge compatible accepted edits, and its comparison signal is a verifier-based pass-count rule—improve at least one split without degrading the other—rather than an LLM pairwise preference between two generated outputs. Thus, Self-Harness can be interpreted as a deterministic, verifier-based analogue of Equation (3) only after replacing the preference relation ≻ with the regression-test acceptance relation; without that substitution, it is local in structure
but not a direct solution of the RHI objective.
RHI is computationally lightweight. Equation (3) relaxation is computationally lightweight because it substantially reduces the cost of both agent execution and pairwise evaluation (Table 1). Let the computational cost of one optimization step be 𝐶 = 𝑁trace + 𝑁pair, where 𝑁trace is the number of coding-agent executions (calls to A) and 𝑁pair is the number of pairwise evaluations (calls to Leval). We assume that each harness output is cached after generation and reused for
subsequent comparisons.
Table 1 | Per-iteration computational cost of three harness-search objectives. Here 𝑀 denotes the

Θ(𝑀2)
Finite-population search (Eq. 2) 𝑚 𝑚  Θ(𝑚2) Trajectory-local RHI (Eq. 3) 1 1 𝚯(1) The ideal objective compares each candidate harness against the entire reference population, requiring one execution trace per harness and pairwise comparisons between all competing pairs. Finite-population methods reduce this cost by restricting the comparison to a sampled population of size 𝑚, but still require quadratic pairwise evaluations. In contrast, RHI compares the current harness only with the cached output of its immediate predecessor, requiring exactly one new execution trace and one pairwise evaluation per iteration. Consequently, 𝐶ideal > 𝐶pop > 𝐶RHI, whenever 𝑀 ≫ 𝑚 ≫ 1. Scaling to 𝑛 independent tasks multiplies all three costs by 𝑛 without
changing their ordering.
RHI performs noisy local ascent. One may wonder whether the relaxation in Equation (3) is too aggressive. Under a standard pairwise-preference model, however, the trajectory-local objective preserves the same latent utility ordering as the ideal population objective. Specifically, suppose there exists a task utility 𝑢𝑥 : H → ℝ and a strictly increasing link function 𝜎 satisfying 𝜎(0) = 1
2 such that Pr(𝐻 ≻ 𝐻′) = 𝜎(𝑢𝑥 (𝐻) − 𝑢𝑥 (𝐻′)). If the competitor distribution is independent
of the candidate harness (or if the conditioning 𝐻′ ≠ 𝐻 is treated as a negligible no-self-comparison correction), then both objectives are monotone functions of the same latent utility: 𝑓𝑥 (𝐻) = 𝔼𝐻′∼𝜇 [𝜎(𝑢𝑥 (𝐻) − 𝑢𝑥 (𝐻′ ))] , e 𝑓 (𝑖) 𝑥 (𝐻) = 𝜎  𝑢𝑥 (𝐻) − 𝑢𝑥 (𝐻 (𝑖−1) 𝑥 ) 
.

### 中文（翻译）

文件系统中的执行轨迹(trace),且不施加任何父节点选择规则,因此提议者在提出新测试框架(harness)时可以检查任意先前的 harness 及其 trace(Lee et al., 2026)。因此,该方法并不实例化公式(3)——公式(3)的比较对象仅为紧邻的前一个 harness $H_x^{(i-1)}$。Self-Harness 更接近公式(3):在每一轮中,当前 harness 被评估,失败案例从其执行 trace 中挖掘出来,候选编辑被提出,每个候选在保留内集和保留外集上对当前 harness 进行回归测试(Zhang et al., 2026)。然而,Self-Harness 与 RHI 的轨迹局部目标并不完全相同。它可能评估多个候选分支并合并兼容的已接受编辑,其比较信号是基于验证器的通过计数规则——在至少一个分割上提升而不降低另一个分割——而非两个生成输出之间的 LLM 成对偏好。因此,Self-Harness 只有在将偏好关系 $\succ$ 替换为回归测试接受关系之后,才能被解释为公式(3)的确定性、基于验证器的类比;若不做此替换,它在结构上是局部的,但并非 RHI 目标的直接解。

**RHI 计算开销轻量。** 公式(3)的松弛在计算上是轻量级的,因为它大幅降低了智能体(agent)执行和成对评估的代价(表 1)。设每次优化步骤的计算代价为 $C = N_{\text{trace}} + N_{\text{pair}}$,其中 $N_{\text{trace}}$ 为编码 agent 的执行次数(对 $\mathcal{A}$ 的调用次数),$N_{\text{pair}}$ 为成对评估的次数(对 $L_{\text{eval}}$ 的调用次数)。我们假设每个 harness 的输出在生成后被缓存,并在后续比较中复用。

表 1 | 三种 harness 搜索目标每次迭代的计算代价。其中 $M$ 表示近似理想目标所需的有效 harness 种群大小,$m \ll M$ 为有限种群搜索所使用的采样种群大小。

| 目标 | $N_{\text{trace}}$ | $N_{\text{pair}}$ | 总代价 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 理想目标(公式 1) | $M$ | $M^2$ | $\Theta(M^2)$ |
| 有限种群搜索(公式 2) | $m$ | $m^2$ | $\Theta(m^2)$ |
| 轨迹局部 RHI(公式 3) | $1$ | $1$ | $\mathbf{\Theta(1)}$ |

理想目标将每个候选 harness 与整个参考种群进行比较,每个 harness 需要一次执行 trace,且所有竞争对之间需要成对比较。有限种群方法通过将比较限制在大小为 $m$ 的采样种群内来降低代价,但仍需要二次级别的成对评估。相比之下,RHI 仅将当前 harness 与其直接前驱的缓存输出进行比较,每次迭代恰好需要一次新的执行 trace 和一次成对评估。因此,当 $M \gg m \gg 1$ 时,$C_{\text{ideal}} > C_{\text{pop}} > C_{\text{RHI}}$。将规模扩展至 $n$ 个独立任务会将三种代价均乘以 $n$,但不改变其排序关系。

**RHI 执行带噪声的局部上升。** 人们可能会质疑公式(3)中的松弛是否过于激进。然而,在标准成对偏好模型下,轨迹局部目标与理想种群目标保持相同的潜在效用排序。具体而言,假设存在任务效用函数 $u_x : \mathcal{H} \to \mathbb{R}$ 和严格单调递增的连接函数 $\sigma$,满足 $\sigma(0) = \frac{1}{2}$,使得 $\Pr(H \succ H') = \sigma(u_x(H) - u_x(H'))$。若竞争者分布独立于候选 harness(或将条件 $H' \neq H$ 视为可忽略的非自比较修正),则两个目标均为同一潜在效用的单调函数:

$$f_x(H) = \mathbb{E}_{H' \sim \mu}\left[\sigma\left(u_x(H) - u_x(H')\right)\right], \quad \tilde{f}_x^{(i)}(H) = \sigma\left(u_x(H) - u_x\left(H_x^{(i-1)}\right)\right).$$

### 中文（翻译）

因此,最大化轨迹局部目标所针对的效用排序与最大化理想种群目标相同。此外,由于 $H_x^{(i-1)}$ 在第 $i$ 次迭代中固定,任何真实对 $H_x^{(i-1)}$ 的胜率超过 $\frac{1}{2}$ 的修订均满足 $u_x(H) > u_x(H_x^{(i-1)})$,从而提升了理想目标。因此,观测到的成对比较提供了一个带噪声的局部上升信号:赢得比较的修订被鼓励,而失败的修订则被丢弃或重新修订。

**少量迭代更新中自历史的必要性。** 由于每次成对比较仅提供带噪声的局部上升信号,RHI 在有限更新预算下跨迭代累积偏好反馈,以提高 harness 优化的可靠性。具体而言,历史记录为:

$$\mathcal{D}_x^{(i)} = \left\{ L_{\text{eval}}\left(y_x^{(k)}, y_x^{(k-1)}; x_{\text{eval}}\right) \right\}_{k=1}^{i}, \tag{4}$$

其中 $y_x^{(k)} \sim \mathcal{A}(H_x^{(k)}, x)$ 是由第 $k$ 个 harness 生成的代码仓库,每个元素记录连续 harness 修订之间的成对偏好。这里,$H_x^{(k)}$ 表示任务 $x$ 经过 $k$ 次 RHI 迭代后的特定任务 harness。在全文中,当任务由上下文明确时,我们简写为 $H^{(k)}$。RHI 不依赖单次带噪声的比较,而是以累积的偏好历史为条件进行每次 harness 更新,从而在每次迭代仅需一次新 agent 执行和一次成对评估的情况下提升鲁棒性。由于 harness 空间是离散的文本空间,这些成对偏好不定义传统意义上的梯度。取而代之,历史 $\mathcal{D}_x^{(i)}$ 充当一种**动量语义信号**,引导未来的 harness 修订。

上述见解推动了下一节所介绍的 RHI 学习算法的设计。

### 3.2. RHI 算法

**算法 1:递归 Harness 自改进(Recursive Harness Self-Improvement)**

**输入:** 任务 $\{x_j\}_{j=1}^n \in \mathcal{X}$,agent $\mathcal{A}$,评估器 $L_{\text{eval}}$,harness 优化器 $L_{\text{harness}}$,评估提示(prompt)$x_{\text{eval}}$,停止阈值 $\epsilon$

**初始化:** 对 $\forall x \in \mathcal{X}$,初始化 harness $H_x^{(0)} \in \mathcal{V}^*$,harness 历史 $\mathcal{D}_x \leftarrow \emptyset$。Agent $\mathcal{A}$ 使用初始 harness $H_x^{(0)}$ 求解任务 $x$,并为 $\forall x \in \mathcal{X}$ 获得输出。

**for** $i = 1, 2, \ldots$ **do**

&emsp;**for** $j = 1, 2, \ldots, n$ **do**

&emsp;&emsp;Agent $\mathcal{A}$ 使用 harness $H_j^{(i)}$ 求解任务 $x_j$,获得输出 $y_j^i$。

&emsp;&emsp;评估器 $L_{\text{eval}}$ 通过成对判断比较 $y_j^i$ 与 $y_j^{i-1}$,然后更新历史:$\mathcal{D}_j \leftarrow \mathcal{D}_j \cup \{L_{\text{eval}}(y_j^i, y_j^{i-1}; x_{\text{eval}})\}$。

&emsp;**end**

&emsp;计算所有任务的改进率 $s_i = \frac{1}{n} \sum_{j=1}^n \mathbf{1}\left[y_j^i \succ y_j^{i-1}\right]$,其中 $s_i \in [0, 1]$。

&emsp;**if** $s_i < \epsilon$ **then**

&emsp;&emsp;BREAK

&emsp;**end**

&emsp;对 $\forall x \in \mathcal{X}$,在不使用 $x_{\text{eval}}$ 的情况下更新 harness:$H_x^{(i+1)} \leftarrow L_{\text{harness}}(H_x^{(i)}, \mathcal{D}_x)$。

**end**

**return** $\forall x \in \mathcal{X}$ 对应的 $H_x^*$

基于上述见解,我们在算法 1 中给出 RHI 算法。在第 $i$ 次迭代时,RHI 按照以下公式更新特定任务的 harness:

$$H_x^{(i+1)} = L_{\text{harness}}\left(H_x^{(i)}, \mathcal{D}_x^{(i)}\right), \tag{5}$$

### English (extracted)

where Lharness is an LLM-based harness optimizer. Here, the direct update from 𝐻 (𝑖) 𝑥 to 𝐻 (𝑖+1) 𝑥 makes RHI computationally lightweight, while conditioning on D(𝑖) 𝑥 enables RHI to converge withinonlyafewupdateiterations. Importantly, 𝑥eval isusedonlybytheevaluatorwhenproducing pairwise feedback; it is not directly provided to Lharness during harness revision. RHI should therefore be viewed as optimizing an implicit preference objective rather than directly maximizing an explicit scalar reward. Although the harness optimizer does not observe 𝑥eval directly, the feedback history D(𝑖) 𝑥 is generated by an evaluator conditioned on 𝑥eval. Consequently, the update trajectory can indirectly align harness revisions with the 𝑥eval through accumulated pairwise comparisons. In this sense, RHI performs recursive self-improvement: each harness is optimized using the preference history induced by its own previous revisions without directly observing the evaluation prompt 𝑥eval. Appendix B provides examples of the iterative evolution of 𝐻 (𝑖) 𝑥 for 𝑖 ∈ {0,1,2, . . .} on a randomly selected task 𝑥. Appendix C provides the system and user prompts used by the harness optimizer
Lharness.

Figure3 | DecompositionoftheRHIharness. Weclassifytheharnessintotwoprimarycomponents: agent design and agent workflow. Agent design specifies the roles and instructions of candidate agents. Agent workflow is further decomposed into contract and hop: contract defines the information exchanged between subagents and the orchestrator, while hop defines the interaction structure; overall workflow logics. We define the harness as the agent loop itself. We decompose the harness into two primary components: agent design and agent workflow. RHI prioritizes the agent workflow for update, which is iteratively adapted to each task (see system prompt of Lharness in Appendix C). We further decompose the workflow into two components: contracts, which specify what information is passed between agents through the communication interface, and hops, which specify the interaction structure, including orchestrator-subagent workflow steps. Figure 3 presents this high-level decomposition, while Figure 4 illustrates how these components are represented as
textual specifications in the prompt.
Why does RHI prioritize workflow updates over agent design? Our focus on contracts and
hops is motivated by the following hypothesis.
We hypothesize that task-specific contracts and hops can improve both task performance and inference efficiency through more effective context management. In particular, a task-specific contract specifies which information should be transmitted between agents, rather than requiring the orchestrator or downstream agents to condition on the entire interaction history. This can reduce redundant context propagation, improve KV-cache efficiency, and lower inference cost. This perspective naturally motivates workflow designs such as multi-agent execution or multi-persona reasoning
within a single agent.
Conceptually, contract optimization is analogous to imposing a task-dependent sparsity pattern on inter-agent information flow. A fully shared interaction history resembles dense attention,

### 中文（翻译）

其中 $L_{\text{harness}}$ 是基于 LLM 的工作流框架优化器(harness optimizer)。此处,从 $H_x^{(i)}$ 到 $H_x^{(i+1)}$ 的直接更新使递归工作流框架改进(Recursive Harness Improvement,RHI)在计算上保持轻量,而对 $D_x^{(i)}$ 的条件化则使 RHI 能够在仅需少量更新迭代的情况下收敛。值得注意的是,$x_{\text{eval}}$ 仅由评估器在生成成对反馈时使用;在工作流框架修订过程中,它不会直接提供给 $L_{\text{harness}}$。因此,RHI 应被视为优化一个隐式偏好目标,而非直接最大化某个显式标量奖励。尽管工作流框架优化器无法直接观测 $x_{\text{eval}}$,但反馈历史 $D_x^{(i)}$ 是由以 $x_{\text{eval}}$ 为条件的评估器生成的。因此,更新轨迹可通过累积的成对比较,间接地使工作流框架修订与 $x_{\text{eval}}$ 保持一致。从这个意义上说,RHI 实现了递归自我改进:每个工作流框架均利用由其自身先前修订所诱导的偏好历史进行优化,而无需直接观测评估提示词(prompt)$x_{\text{eval}}$。附录 B 提供了在随机选取的任务 $x$ 上,$H_x^{(i)}$($i \in \{0,1,2,\ldots\}$)迭代演化的示例。附录 C 提供了工作流框架优化器 $L_{\text{harness}}$ 所使用的系统提示词与用户提示词。

## 3.3. RHI 工作流框架定义

<!-- 图3示意图:Harness[i] 分解为 Agent Design(角色、指令)和 Agent Workflow,后者进一步分解为 Contract(信息接口)和 Hop(交互结构) -->

**图3 | RHI 工作流框架的分解。** 我们将工作流框架划分为两个主要组成部分:智能体设计(agent design)和智能体工作流(agent workflow)。智能体设计规定候选智能体的角色与指令。智能体工作流进一步分解为契约(contract)和跳步(hop):契约定义子智能体与协调器之间交换的信息,而跳步定义交互结构,即整体工作流逻辑。

我们将工作流框架定义为智能体循环本身,并将其分解为两个主要组成部分:智能体设计和智能体工作流。RHI 优先对智能体工作流进行更新,该工作流会针对每个任务进行迭代适配(详见附录 C 中 $L_{\text{harness}}$ 的系统提示词)。我们进一步将工作流分解为两个组件:契约(contract),用于规定智能体之间通过通信接口传递哪些信息;以及跳步(hop),用于规定交互结构,包括协调器与子智能体的工作流步骤。图 3 展示了这一高层分解结构,图 4 则说明了这些组件如何以文本规范的形式在提示词中表示。

**RHI 为何优先更新工作流而非智能体设计?** 我们对契约和跳步的关注源于以下假设。

我们假设,任务特定的契约和跳步能够通过更有效的上下文管理,同时提升任务性能和推理效率。具体而言,任务特定的契约规定了智能体之间应传递哪些信息,而无需协调器或下游智能体以整个交互历史为条件进行推理。这可以减少冗余上下文的传播,提升 KV 缓存效率,并降低推理成本。这一视角自然地引导出多智能体并行执行或单一智能体内多角色推理等工作流设计。

从概念上看,契约优化类似于对智能体间信息流施加一种依赖任务的稀疏模式。完全共享的交互历史类似于稠密注意力,

the orchestrator–subagent workflow, and auxiliary control rules used for termination, fallback, recall, and inter-agent communication. Harness as a prompt The RHI harness first specifies a set of candidate agents, where each agent is described by its role, instructions, and contract, i.e., its expected output to the orchestrator; see “Agent candidates” in Figure 4. It then specifies the workflow governing interactions among these agents; see “Agent orchestrator-subagent workflow” in Figure 4. These two components are included in every RHI iteration and serve as a task-agnostic scaffold for harness optimization. Across iterations, RHI further induces task-dependent local harness components, such as acceptance gates, failure fallback rules, and evidence-motivated recall triggers; see the “auxiliary rules”
in Figure 4.
4. Benchmark & Evaluation
4.1. Synthetic open-ended machine learning research tasks
We evaluate RHI on a suite of 30 synthetic, open-ended machine learning (ML) research tasks spanning three domains: quantitative finance, robotics, and pharmaceutical machine learning, with 10 tasks per domain. To construct these tasks, we use a LLM to transform domain-relevant industry job postings into research-style task prompts that resemble assignments a researcher
might receive in the corresponding role. The source postings are:
1. quantitative ML research: https://www.citadel.com/careers/details/quanti
tative-researcher-phd-graduate-us/;
2. robotics ML research: https://www.amazon.jobs/en/jobs/10443615/member-o
f-technical-staff-far-frontier-ai-robotics
3. pharmaceutical ML research: https://careers.gene.com/us/en/job/202606-1
15542/Machine-Learning-Scientist-Synthesis-Planning-and-Optimiz
ation.
The generated tasks require a combination of coding, domain reasoning, empirical analysis,

### 中文（翻译）

即每个智能体均可访问其他智能体的所有先验信息。相比之下,任务特定的契约将通信限制在对下游决策最相关的信息上。因此,优化契约可被视为在工作流框架层面学习一种任务特定的通信稀疏结构,从而实现更高效的上下文管理。

<!-- 图4示意图:以提示词表示的工作流框架结构 -->

**候选智能体(Agent candidates)**
- Agent$_1$:$\{$role: ..., instruction: ..., contract (out-to-orchestrator): $\{c_{11}, c_{12}, \ldots\}\}$
- $\cdots$
- Agent$_N$:$\{$role: ..., instruction: ..., contract (out-to-orchestrator): $\{c_{N1}, c_{N2}, \ldots\}\}$

**智能体协调器-子智能体工作流(Agent orchestrator-subagent workflow)**
- Hop$_1$: ...
- $\cdots$
- Hop$_M$: ...

**辅助规则(Auxiliary rules)**
终止前的验收门控、失败回退规则、证据驱动的召回触发器,以及通用的智能体间通信规则等。

**图4 | 以提示词表示的工作流框架 $H^{(i)}$ 的结构。** 工作流框架规定了候选智能体、协调器-子智能体工作流,以及用于终止、回退、召回和智能体间通信的辅助控制规则。

**工作流框架即提示词** RHI 工作流框架首先规定一组候选智能体,每个智能体由其角色、指令和契约(即其向协调器输出的预期内容)来描述;详见图 4 中的"候选智能体"部分。其次规定管理这些智能体之间交互的工作流;详见图 4 中的"智能体协调器-子智能体工作流"部分。这两个组件出现在每次 RHI 迭代中,作为工作流框架优化的任务无关脚手架。在迭代过程中,RHI 进一步诱导出依赖任务的局部工作流框架组件,如验收门控、失败回退规则和证据驱动的召回触发器;详见图 4 中的"辅助规则"部分。

## 4. 基准测试与评估

### 4.1. 合成开放式机器学习研究任务

我们在一套包含 30 个合成开放式机器学习(ML)研究任务的测试集上对 RHI 进行评估,这些任务涵盖三个领域:量化金融、机器人学和制药机器学习,每个领域各 10 个任务。为构建这些任务,我们使用 LLM 将与领域相关的行业招聘信息转化为研究风格的任务提示词,使其类似于研究人员在相应职位中可能收到的任务。原始招聘信息来源如下:

1. 量化 ML 研究:https://www.citadel.com/careers/details/quantitative-researcher-phd-graduate-us/
2. 机器人学 ML 研究:https://www.amazon.jobs/en/jobs/10443615/member-of-technical-staff-far-frontier-ai-robotics
3. 制药 ML 研究:https://careers.gene.com/us/en/job/202606-115542/Machine-Learning-Scientist-Synthesis-Planning-and-Optimization

所生成的任务需要综合运用编程能力、领域推理、实证分析,

## 第 11 页

### English (extracted)

and ML experimentation, etc. They include objectives such as data analysis, model training, benchmark construction, ablation design, and empirical reporting. Each task asks the agent to construct a complete code repository and produce the deliverables specified in the prompt. This benchmark is designed to support diverse ML tasks while preserving a consistent evaluation interface. Each task prompt consists of two parts: a task description, which specifies the research objective, and a deliverables section, which specifies the required output artifacts. Across tasks, the core deliverables are standardized: (1) research_report.md, (2) visualization files in .png format, (3) metrics.json, and (4) index.json, which records the paths of all generated files. Some tasks additionally require domain-specific artifacts, such as model-comparison files, ablation summaries, or reproducible scripts. Appendix D provides an example task prompt from each domain: robotics, quantitative finance,
and pharmacy.
4.2. LLM-as-a-judge evaluation
Our evaluation protocol follows the problem formulation in Section 2.1, where outputs are compared through pairwise preference under multiple evaluation criteria. This corresponds to Step 3 of the RHI algorithm (Figure 2). Given two repositories generated for the same task, the standardized deliverable structure provides a common evaluation interface, allowing an LLM evaluator to compare the task-specific deliverables and select the preferred repository. For each comparison, we extract the task-specified deliverables from both repositories and provide their contents, together with the evaluation prompt 𝑥eval, to Leval. Since repositories may contain many artifacts (e.g., source code, logs, text files, and figures), including every generated file is often impractical. We therefore evaluate only the task-specified deliverables and keep the evaluator prompt within a practical context budget (approximately 30–40% of the evaluator’s maximum input length) to mitigate context rot (Hong et al., 2025). When necessary, long files
are truncated using the same protocol for both candidate repositories.
To evaluate robustness across evaluators, we use two configurations with different model families and reasoning settings: gpt-5.5 with maximum reasoning effort and opus-4.7 (or opus-4.8) with xhigh reasoning effort. Each configuration is evaluated using three random seeds. The evaluator Leval compares the two repositories according to the following criteria. Evaluation prompt (𝑥eval)
1. Deliverable coverage: whether the output satisfies the task’s explicit deliverable requirements, including files listed
under Deliverables, Deliverable, or inline output requirements.
2. Numerical and empirical rigor: whether the methodology, baselines, metrics, and limitations are appropriate and
internally consistent.
3. Reproducibility: whether the repository includes dependencies, entry points, seeds, and sufficient documentation
for reproducing the results.
4. Presentation: whether the report is clear, well structured, and appropriately integrates figures and empirical results.
5. Engineering quality: whether the repository is organized, modular, and readable based on the file tree and code
excerpts.
6. Task alignment: whether the solution addresses the stated objective without drifting to a different problem.
Appendix E provides the complete system and user prompts used by Leval.
5. Experiment
Section 5.1 describes the experimental settings, Section 5.2 defines the evaluation metrics, and
Sections 5.3–5.5 present our main experimental results.

### 中文（翻译）

以及机器学习实验等。任务涵盖数据分析、模型训练、基准构建、消融设计和实证报告等目标。每项任务要求智能体(agent)构建完整的代码仓库,并生成提示词(prompt)中指定的交付物。该基准旨在支持多样化的机器学习任务,同时保持统一的评估接口。每个任务提示词由两部分组成:任务描述(说明研究目标)和交付物章节(说明所需的输出产物)。各任务的核心交付物已标准化,包括:(1) research_report.md,(2) .png 格式的可视化文件,(3) metrics.json,以及 (4) index.json(记录所有生成文件的路径)。部分任务还额外要求特定领域的产物,例如模型对比文件、消融摘要或可复现脚本。附录 D 提供了来自三个领域(机器人学、量化金融和药学)的示例任务提示词。

4.2. 基于 LLM 的裁判评估

我们的评估协议遵循第 2.1 节中的问题形式化定义,通过多项评估标准下的成对偏好比较输出结果。这对应于 RHI 算法的第 3 步(图 2)。给定针对同一任务生成的两个代码仓库,标准化的交付物结构提供了统一的评估接口,使 LLM 评估器能够比较特定任务的交付物并选出更优仓库。对于每次比较,我们从两个仓库中提取任务指定的交付物,将其内容连同评估提示词 $x_\text{eval}$ 一并提供给 $L_\text{eval}$。由于仓库可能包含大量产物(如源代码、日志、文本文件和图像),将所有生成文件全部纳入往往不切实际。因此,我们仅评估任务指定的交付物,并将评估器提示词控制在合理的上下文预算内(约为评估器最大输入长度的 30%–40%),以缓解上下文衰减问题(Hong et al., 2025)。必要时,对较长文件按相同协议进行截断处理,两个候选仓库保持一致。

为评估跨评估器的鲁棒性,我们使用两种配置,采用不同的模型系列和推理设置:gpt-5.5(最高推理力度)和 opus-4.7(或 opus-4.8,xhigh 推理力度)。每种配置使用三个随机种子进行评估。评估器 $L_\text{eval}$ 依据以下标准对两个仓库进行比较。

评估提示词($x_\text{eval}$)

1. 交付物覆盖度:输出是否满足任务的显式交付物要求,包括 Deliverables、Deliverable 或内嵌输出要求中列出的文件。
2. 数值与实证严谨性:方法论、基线、指标和局限性是否恰当且内部一致。
3. 可复现性:仓库是否包含依赖项、入口点、随机种子及足够的文档以复现结果。
4. 呈现质量:报告是否清晰、结构良好,并适当整合了图表和实证结果。
5. 工程质量:基于文件树和代码摘录,仓库是否组织良好、模块化且可读性强。
6. 任务对齐性:解决方案是否切实解决了既定目标,而未偏离至不同问题。

附录 E 提供了 $L_\text{eval}$ 所使用的完整系统提示词和用户提示词。

5. 实验

第 5.1 节描述实验设置,第 5.2 节定义评估指标,第 5.3–5.5 节呈现主要实验结果。

5.1. Experiment settings
RHI is designed to be computationally lightweight and to converge within only a few update iterations. Our experimental setup is designed to test whether RHI can raise the performance
ceiling of agent test-time scaling.
To this end, we instantiate the coding agent A with three base language models: Claude Sonnet 4.6, ClaudeOpus4.7, andClaudeOpus4.8. Foreachmodel, thedefaultagentusesreasoningeffort high. We denote these base configurations by L1-high, L2-high, and L3-high, respectively. We denote the corresponding base agent by AL𝑏-high. For each task 𝑥 ∈ X and each base model 𝑏 ∈ {1,2,3}, we first run the base agent with the initial harness, AL𝑏-high(𝐻 (0) 𝑥 , 𝑥), and then apply RHI to iteratively produce a sequence of task-specific harnesses {𝐻 (0) 𝑥 , 𝐻 (1) 𝑥 , . . .}. At each iteration, we compare the output of the RHI-improved agent against that of the corresponding
higher test-time reasoning agent without RHI:
𝑦RHI ∼ AL𝑏-high(𝐻 (𝑖) 𝑥 , 𝑥) vs. 𝑦scale ∼ AL𝑏-xhigh/ultracode/max(𝑥). Here, AL𝑏-high(𝐻 (𝑖) 𝑥 , 𝑥) denotes the base coding agent equipped with the RHI-improved harness
𝐻
(𝑖) 𝑥 at iteration 𝑖. Relative to the original base agent AL𝑏-high, the only modification is the input prompt, which changes from 𝑥 to 𝑥 ∪ 𝐻 (𝑖) 𝑥 by augmenting the original task with the evolved harness represented as a textual specification. In contrast, AL𝑏-xhigh/ultracode/max(𝑥) denotes the same coding agent evaluated with a higher reasoning effort, but without an RHI-generated
harness.

Notation. For compactness, we use the bracketed form H[𝑖] interchangeably with 𝐻(𝑖) for the same iteration index, particularly in figure and table labels. Thus, H[𝑖] corresponds to 𝐻(𝑖), or to

pares RHI on opus-4.8-high against opus-4.8-xhigh/ultracode/max. For each base
model, we report four metrics: pairwise win/loss count (i.e., performance), normalized cost, output token count, and cache read/write usage. Cost, output token count, and cache read/write usage are recorded using the agent’s internal cost function of the claude coding agent.

### 中文（翻译）

5.1. 实验设置

RHI 在设计上计算开销轻量,仅需少数更新迭代即可收敛。我们的实验设置旨在验证 RHI 能否提升 agent 测试时扩展(test-time scaling)的性能上限。

为此,我们以三个基础语言模型实例化编码 agent $\mathcal{A}$:Claude Sonnet 4.6、Claude Opus 4.7 和 Claude Opus 4.8。对于每个模型,默认 agent 使用推理力度 high。我们将这三种基础配置分别记为 L1-high、L2-high 和 L3-high,对应的基础 agent 记为 $A_{L_b\text{-high}}$。对于每个任务 $x \in \mathcal{X}$ 和每个基础模型 $b \in \{1,2,3\}$,我们首先以初始执行框架(harness)运行基础 agent $A_{L_b\text{-high}}(H_x^{(0)}, x)$,然后应用 RHI 迭代生成一系列任务特定 harness $\{H_x^{(0)}, H_x^{(1)}, \ldots\}$。在每次迭代中,我们将 RHI 改进后的 agent 输出与未使用 RHI 的对应高测试时推理 agent 输出进行比较:

$$y_\text{RHI} \sim A_{L_b\text{-high}}(H_x^{(i)}, x) \quad \text{vs.} \quad y_\text{scale} \sim A_{L_b\text{-xhigh/ultracode/max}}(x).$$

其中 $A_{L_b\text{-high}}(H_x^{(i)}, x)$ 表示在第 $i$ 次迭代时配备了 RHI 改进 harness $H_x^{(i)}$ 的基础编码 agent。相较于原始基础 agent $A_{L_b\text{-high}}$,唯一的修改是输入提示词:从 $x$ 变为 $x \cup H_x^{(i)}$,即以文本规范的形式将演化后的 harness 附加到原始任务中。相比之下,$A_{L_b\text{-xhigh/ultracode/max}}(x)$ 表示同一编码 agent 在更高推理力度下进行评估,但不使用 RHI 生成的 harness。

### 5.3 命题 1:少样本 RHI 提升测试时缩放的性能上界

对于 Sonnet-4.6 基线,图 5a 显示,经过两次 RHI 迭代后,sonnet-4.6-high+H[2] 优于更强的测试时缩放基线 sonnet-4.6-max,在 30 次两两比较中赢得 20 次。此外,sonnet-4.6-high+H[$i$]($i \in \{3,4\}$)继续优于该基线。

两个更强基础模型上的趋势相同。对于 Opus-4.7 基线,图 6a 显示 opus-4.7-high+H[0] 弱于 opus-4.7-xhigh 和 opus-4.7-max 基线。然而,图 6b 显示仅需一次 RHI 迭代即可超越二者。对于 Opus-4.8 基线,图 7b 显示 opus-4.8-high+H[0] 略优于或与 opus-4.8-xhigh 持平。经过两次 RHI 迭代后,图 7d 显示 opus-4.8-high+H[2] 优于所有测试时缩放基线:opus-4.8-xhigh、opus-4.8-ultracode 和 opus-4.8-max。这些结果表明,在我们的基准测试和评估协议下,少量几次 RHI 迭代即可将性能提升至同系列测试时缩放饱和点之上。重要的是,这一提升并不局限于某一特定基础模型能力,而是在逐渐增强的模型中持续存在。

### English (extracted)

which same-family test-time scaling saturates under our benchmark and evaluation protocol. Importantly, this improvement is not confined to a single base-model capability, but persists across

Claim 1.1. Optimizing user-constructed harnesses at the prompt level can outperform a
system-level provider-built harness
Aparticularlynotableresultisthatopus-4.8-high+H[2] outperformsopus-4.8-ultracode (Figure 7d). opus-4.8-ultracode uses xhigh reasoning together with a built-in dynamic workflow that can spawn multiple subagents (Anthropic, 2026a). We interpret this result as evidence that, on our benchmark, dynamically generating multi-agent workflows through a provider-built-in harness is not always sufficient. Instead, a task-specific, user-constructed harness
supplied directly in the prompt can outperform it.
This finding suggests a limitation of relying on a single fixed, system-level harness to cover a broad and heterogeneous task distribution. By optimizing the harness at the prompt level, RHI enables the multi-agent workflow to adapt to each task. The advantage over opus-4.8-ultracode therefore not only demonstrates the effectiveness of optimizing a user-constructed harness over a provider-built-in harness, but also supports the harness-as-prompt formulation, in which harness design becomes an explicit, task-specific optimization variable rather than a fixed backend
workflow.
5.4. Claim 2. RHI performance gains are not explained by increased output-token us-
age
Here, we show that the performance gains from RHI are distinct from those of test-time scaling, which primarily relies on increased output-token usage, demonstrating that RHI is a standalone
method.
For the Sonnet-4.6 baseline, Figure 5b shows that the normalized output-token usage remains nearly constant across RHI iterations H[𝑖], 𝑖 ∈ {0,1,2,3,4}, ranging from 1.71 to 1.86. In contrast, Figures 6a and 6b shows that performance consistently improves across the same
iterations.
The same decoupling is observed for the Opus-4.8 baseline. Figure 7e shows that the normalized output-token usage remains nearly flat, ranging from 1.42 to 1.81, whereas Figure 7 shows that performance improves across H[𝑖], 𝑖 ∈ {0,1,2}. For the Opus-4.7 baseline, the evidence is inconclusive. Figure 6c shows that output-token usage increases together with performance across H[𝑖], 𝑖 ∈ {0,1}, so the two explanations cannot be disentangled. Moreover, this comparison spans only two iterations, fewer than for the other models, which further limits the conclusions that can be drawn. Overall, the gains are not primarily driven by longer generations: for two of the three models,
performance improves while output-token usage remains nearly constant.
Consistent with this observation, in the next subsection we show that RHI’s gains are more closely associated with KV-cache usage than with output-token usage, suggesting that efficient context management, rather than generation length alone, is the primary driver.
5.5. Claim 3. Few-shot RHI improves cost efficiency through reduced cache read/write
usage
This claim supports the hypothesis proposed in Section 3.3 that RHI prioritizes workflow updates over agent design. Specifically, we show that, beyond improving performance, RHI consistently
improves cost efficiency across all base models.

### 中文（翻译）

**命题 1.1:在提示词(prompt)层面优化用户构建的工作流框架(harness)可以优于系统层面由提供商内置的工作流框架**

一个尤为值得关注的结果是 opus-4.8-high+H[2] 优于 opus-4.8-ultracode(图 7d)。opus-4.8-ultracode 在 xhigh 推理能力基础上配备了内置动态工作流(workflow),可以生成多个子智能体(subagent)(Anthropic, 2026a)。我们将此结果解读为以下证据:在我们的基准上,通过提供商内置的工作流框架动态生成多智能体工作流并非总是足够的。相反,直接通过提示词提供的、针对特定任务由用户构建的工作流框架可以超越它。

这一发现揭示了依赖单一固定的系统级工作流框架来覆盖宽泛且异质任务分布的局限性。通过在提示词层面优化工作流框架,RHI 使多智能体工作流能够针对每个任务进行自适应调整。因此,相对于 opus-4.8-ultracode 的优势不仅证明了在用户构建的工作流框架上进行优化优于提供商内置工作流框架的有效性,还支持了"工作流框架即提示词(harness-as-prompt)"的表述——在该表述中,工作流框架的设计成为一个显式的、针对任务的优化变量,而非固定的后端工作流。

### 5.4 命题 2:RHI 的性能提升不能由输出词元用量的增加来解释

在此,我们表明 RHI 带来的性能提升有别于测试时缩放——后者主要依赖于输出词元用量的增加——从而证明 RHI 是一种独立的方法。

对于 Sonnet-4.6 基线,图 5b 显示归一化输出词元用量在各 RHI 迭代 H[$i$]($i \in \{0,1,2,3,4\}$)之间几乎保持不变,范围为 1.71 至 1.86。与此形成对比的是,图 6a 和 6b 显示性能在相同迭代过程中持续提升。

Opus-4.8 基线上也观察到相同的解耦现象。图 7e 显示归一化输出词元用量几乎持平,范围为 1.42 至 1.81,而图 7 显示性能在 H[$i$]($i \in \{0,1,2\}$)之间持续提升。对于 Opus-4.7 基线,证据尚无定论。图 6c 显示输出词元用量与性能在 H[$i$]($i \in \{0,1\}$)上同步增长,因此两种解释无法区分。此外,该比较仅跨两次迭代,少于其他模型,进一步限制了可得出的结论。总体而言,性能提升的主要驱动力并非更长的生成序列:在三个模型中的两个上,性能提升而输出词元用量几乎不变。

与上述观察一致,在下一小节中我们将说明,RHI 的增益与 KV 缓存用量的关联更为密切,而非与输出词元用量,这表明高效的上下文管理——而非生成长度本身——才是主要的驱动因素。

### 5.5 命题 3:少样本 RHI 通过降低缓存读写用量提升成本效率

该命题支持第 3.3 节提出的假设,即 RHI 优先更新工作流而非智能体设计。具体而言,我们表明,除性能提升外,RHI 在所有基础模型上均持续提升了成本效率。

## 第 16 页

### English (extracted)

Forthesonnet-4.6baseline, Figure5bshowsthatthenormalizedcostof sonnet-4.6-high+H[2] (2.38)is7%lowerthanthatof sonnet-4.6-max(2.56), whilecacheread/writeusagedecreases by 33% (4.91 → 3.31).
A similar trend is observed for stronger models. For opus-4.7 baseline, Figure 6c shows that the
normalized cost of opus-4.7-high+H[1] (2.11) is 18% lower than that of opus-4.7-max (2.60), while cache read/write usage decreases by 37% (3.37 → 2.11) . For opus-4.8 baseline, Figure 7e shows that the normalized cost of opus-4.8-high+H[2] (1.69)decreasesby23%relativetoopus-4.8-max(2.19)andby 60%relativetoopus-4.8-ultracode (4.15). The corresponding cache read/write usage decreases by 32% (2.51 → 1.69) and by 64% (4.74 → 1.69), respectively. We attribute these savings to more efficient context management. RHI learns task-specific communication contracts: textual interfaces that specify which information should pass from one agent to another, or from subagents to the orchestrator. These contracts allow each agent to condition on the information its downstream decisions require, rather than on the full interaction history, thereby curbing redundant context propagation. Optimizing the multi-agent workflow can therefore be viewed as learning a task-specific sparsity pattern over inter-agent communication. This is conceptually analogous to sparse self-attention, where selected entries of the attention pattern replace dense all-to-all token interactions to improve long-context efficiency (Beltagy et al., 2020; Child et al., 2019; Zaheer et al., 2020). Because inference cost in Claude-style coding agents depends heavily on cache reads and writes, this reduced context footprint can translate
directly into lower cost.
We caution against reading the larger savings for opus-4.7 and opus-4.8 (Figures 6c and 7e) as evidence that RHI inherently becomes more effective as the base model improves. A more conservative interpretation is that scaling test-time compute on an already strong model yields diminishing performance-per-cost returns; RHI offers an alternative that restructures the agent’s computation through the prompt-level harness rather than uniformly increasing reasoning ef-
fort.
Summary of Sections 3 and 5
Recursive Harness Self-Improvement (RHI) is a computationally lightweight method that requires only a few iterations yet can substantially raise the performance ceiling of test-time scaling baselines while reducing inference cost by up to 60%. This advantage persists across progressively stronger base models. The performance gains from RHI are not explained by increased output-token usage; instead, they are associated with more efficient context management, as reflected by reduced cache read/write usage and lower inference cost.
We examine what drives these gains in the next section.
6. Ablation Study
Each section addresses a different analysis question. Section 6.1 examines whether RHI provides benefits beyond train-time scaling. Section 6.2 investigates the factors driving RHI’s performance gains. Finally, Section 6.3 formulates an implicit objective for RHI based on the observations from
Section 6.2.
6.1. Can RHI also raise the performance plateau of train-time scaling?
A natural question is whether RHI also raises performance plateaus of train-time scaling by replac-
ing the base model with a stronger model. To test this, we compare sonnet-4.6-high+H[𝑖], 𝑖 ∈ {0,1,2,3,4}, against opus-4.7-high and opus-4.7-xhigh. Figure 8 shows that RHI

### 中文（翻译）

对于 sonnet-4.6 基线,图 5b 显示 sonnet-4.6-high+H[2] 的归一化成本(2.38)比 sonnet-4.6-max(2.56)低 7%,同时缓存读写用量减少了 33%(4.91 → 3.31)。

对于更强的模型也观察到类似趋势。对于 opus-4.7 基线,图 6c 显示 opus-4.7-high+H[1] 的归一化成本(2.11)比 opus-4.7-max(2.60)低 18%,缓存读写用量减少了 37%(3.37 → 2.11)。对于 opus-4.8 基线,图 7e 显示 opus-4.8-high+H[2] 的归一化成本(1.69)相比 opus-4.8-max(2.19)降低了 23%,相比 opus-4.8-ultracode(4.15)降低了 60%。对应的缓存读写用量分别减少了 32%(2.51 → 1.69)和 64%(4.74 → 1.69)。我们将这些节省归因于更高效的上下文管理。RHI 学习特定任务的通信契约(contract):这些文本接口规定了哪些信息应从一个智能体(agent)传递到另一个智能体,或从子智能体传递到编排器。这些契约使每个智能体能够仅基于其下游决策所需的信息进行条件化,而无需依赖完整的交互历史,从而抑制冗余上下文的传播。优化多智能体工作流(workflow)因此可视为学习智能体间通信的特定任务稀疏模式。这在概念上类似于稀疏自注意力机制——通过选择注意力模式中的特定条目来替代密集的全对全词元交互,以提升长上下文效率(Beltagy et al., 2020;Child et al., 2019;Zaheer et al., 2020)。由于 Claude 风格编码智能体的推理成本在很大程度上取决于缓存读写,上下文占用的减少可以直接转化为更低的成本。

我们提醒不应将 opus-4.7 和 opus-4.8 中更大的节省幅度(图 6c 和 7e)解读为 RHI 随基础模型增强而固有地变得更有效的证据。更为保守的解释是:对一个已经很强的模型进行测试时计算扩展(test-time compute scaling)会产生递减的性价比回报;RHI 提供了一种替代方案,通过提示词级别的框架(prompt-level harness)对智能体的计算进行重构,而非均匀地增加推理算力。

**第 3 节与第 5 节小结**

递归框架自我改进(Recursive Harness Self-Improvement,RHI)是一种计算轻量级方法,只需少量迭代即可大幅提升测试时计算扩展基线的性能上限,同时将推理成本降低多达 60%。这一优势在逐步增强的基础模型上持续存在。RHI 带来的性能提升并不能用输出词元用量的增加来解释;相反,它与更高效的上下文管理相关,具体体现在缓存读写用量的减少和推理成本的降低。

我们将在下一节中探讨这些收益背后的驱动因素。

## 6. 消融研究

各小节分别针对不同的分析问题。第 6.1 节探讨 RHI 是否能在训练时扩展之外提供额外收益。第 6.2 节研究驱动 RHI 性能提升的因素。最后,第 6.3 节基于第 6.2 节的观察,为 RHI 构建一个隐式目标函数。

### 6.1. RHI 能否同样提升训练时扩展的性能平台?

一个自然的问题是,RHI 是否也能通过替换更强的基础模型来提升训练时扩展的性能平台。为此,我们将 sonnet-4.6-high+H[$i$]($i \in \{0,1,2,3,4\}$)与 opus-4.7-high 及 opus-4.7-xhigh 进行比较。图 8 显示,RHI

6.2. Which harness factors drive RHI performance gains?
The experiments in Section 5 suggest that RHI’s performance gains are not primarily explained by longer generations. We therefore examine how the harness evolves across RHI iterations. Section 6.2.1 analyzes the evolution of the entire harness, while Section 6.2.2 investigates which harness components drive RHI’s performance gains.
6.2.1. Full-harness evolution
Visualization. Wefirstanalyzetheevolutionofthefullprompt-representedharnessforsonnet-4.6-high. For each H(𝑖) 𝑥 , 𝑖 ∈ {0,1,2,3,4}, we embed the full textual specification and project the embeddings to two dimensions using t-SNE and UMAP. We assess robustness using two embedding models, OpenAI text-embedding-3-large (OpenAI, 2024a) and all-mpnet-base-v2 (Reimers and Gurevych, 2019; Song et al., 2020). This embedding analysis is a diagnostic rather than a mechanistic explanation. Because the coding agent is black-box, we cannot directly inspect how a textual harness changes the model’s internal computation or output distribution. We therefore use text embeddings as an external proxy for semantic change and ask whether the resulting harness trajectories are systematic across iterations, embedding models, and projection methods. Figures 9a and 9b show the same low-dimensional representations of H[𝑖] with different annotations. In Figure 9a, colors indicate domains and black outlines denote the initial harness H(0). In Figure 9b, colors indicate RHI iterations and marker shapes indicate domains. Figure 9a shows a clear separation between the initial harness H(0) and the RHI-improved harnesses H(1), . . . ,H(4). This separation is consistent across embedding models and projection methods. Together with the performance improvements in Figure 5a, thses results suggest that adapting the initial domain-level harness, H(0), into task-specific harnesses is one of key factors

因此,我们将 RHI 理解为训练时扩展的补充,而非替代。RHI 能够改善固定模型的使用方式,并提升同系列测试时扩展可达到的性能上限,但它并不能消除更强基础模型所带来的优势。这一观察也为 RHI 的进一步算法改进留下了空间。

### 6.2. 哪些框架(harness)因素驱动了 RHI 的性能提升?

第 5 节的实验表明,RHI 的性能提升主要不能用更长的生成来解释。因此,我们考察框架在 RHI 各迭代轮次中的演化过程。第 6.2.1 节分析完整框架的演化,第 6.2.2 节则探究哪些框架组件驱动了 RHI 的性能提升。

#### 6.2.1. 完整框架的演化

**可视化。** 我们首先分析 sonnet-4.6-high 的完整提示词表示框架的演化过程。对于每个 $H^{(i)}_x$($i \in \{0,1,2,3,4\}$),我们对完整文本规范进行嵌入,并使用 t-SNE 和 UMAP 将嵌入投影到二维空间。我们使用两种嵌入模型评估鲁棒性:OpenAI text-embedding-3-large(OpenAI, 2024a)和 all-mpnet-base-v2(Reimers and Gurevych, 2019;Song et al., 2020)。该嵌入分析是一种诊断手段,而非机制性解释。由于编码智能体是黑盒系统,我们无法直接检查文本框架如何改变模型的内部计算或输出分布。因此,我们使用文本嵌入作为语义变化的外部代理,并考察所得框架轨迹是否在迭代轮次、嵌入模型和投影方法之间具有一致性。图 9a 和图 9b 展示了 H[$i$] 的相同低维表示,但标注方式不同:图 9a 中颜色表示领域,黑色轮廓标记初始框架 H(0);图 9b 中颜色表示 RHI 迭代轮次,标记形状表示领域。图 9a 清晰地显示了初始框架 H(0) 与经 RHI 改进的框架 H(1), ..., H(4) 之间的分离,这一分离在不同嵌入模型和投影方法下保持一致。结合图 5a 中的性能提升,这些结果表明,将初始领域级框架 H(0) 适配为特定任务框架是关键因素之一。

6.2.2. Harness-component evolution
Visualization. We decompose each harness into the four components used throughout the paper: roles, instructions, contracts, and hops (see Figure 3). Roles and instructions specify agent design; contracts specify what information is passed between subagents and the orchestrator;
and hops specify the multi-agent workflow.
Formally, suppose H(𝑖) 𝑥 specifies 𝑁𝑥𝑖 agents and 𝑀𝑥𝑖 workflow hops. For each agent 𝑛 ∈ [𝑁𝑥𝑖],
let
ℎ role,(𝑖) 𝑥𝑛 , ℎ instr,(𝑖) 𝑥𝑛 , ℎ cont,(𝑖) 𝑥𝑛 ∈ V∗ denote its role, instruction, and contract. For each hop 𝑚 ∈ [𝑀𝑥𝑖], let ℎ hop,(𝑖) 𝑥𝑚 ∈ V∗ denote the corresponding workflow step (see Figure 4). We embed each textual component using text-embedding-3-large and all-mpnet-base-v2, followed by ℓ2 normalization. If a component exceeds the embedding model’s input length, we split it into chunks, embed each chunk separately, and average the normalized chunk embeddings. Figure 10 shows t-SNE and UMAP projections of these component embeddings. Each row corresponds to a component type: role, instruction, contract, or hop. Marker shape indicates the RHI iteration 𝑖 ∈ {1,2,3,4}, and color indicates the task 𝑥. For a fixed task and iteration, the role, instruction, and contract rows contain 𝑁𝑥𝑖 points, while the hop row contains 𝑀𝑥𝑖 points. Across all three domains, Figures 10a, 10b, and 10c show that contracts exhibit the clearest task-dependent clustering. This pattern is consistent across embedding models and projection methods, suggesting that the largest semantic changes occur at the information interface between the orchestrator and subagents. Hops and instructions also exhibit task-dependent clustering, but less distinctly than contracts. In contrast, roles are less separable, likely because many ML research tasks in our benchmark share common high-level roles, such as data preparation, model training, validation, and report writing. Together with the performance results in Section 5, these findings suggest that RHI’s gains arise primarily from task-specific coordination rather than longer reasoning traces alone. In particular, contracts and hops determine what information is communicated and how the multi-agent workflow proceeds. Their prominence is expected because the harness optimizer’s system prompt explicitly emphasizes multi-agent workflow refinement, particularly contracts and hops (see Section C). We therefore interpret the observed component-level separation as evidence consistent with RHI learning task-specific coordination interfaces, while emphasizing that the embedding
analysis is correlational rather than causal.

be the

$$\frac{1}{|P_D|} \sum_{(x,x') \in P_D} \cos\!\left(\mathrm{emb}(H^{(i)}_x),\, \mathrm{emb}(H^{(i)}_{x'})\right), \quad \text{其中 } P_D = \{(x,x') : x, x' \in D,\, x \neq x'\}.$$

### 6.2.2 框架组件演化

**可视化。** 我们将每个框架分解为贯穿全文的四类组件:角色、指令、契约和跳转(见图 3)。角色和指令用于规范智能体(agent)设计;契约规定子智能体与编排器之间传递的信息;跳转规定多智能体工作流(workflow)的执行流程。

形式上,设 H(i)x 指定了 Nxi 个智能体和 Mxi 个工作流跳转步骤。对每个智能体 n ∈ [Nxi],令

$$h^{\mathrm{role},(i)}_{xn},\; h^{\mathrm{instr},(i)}_{xn},\; h^{\mathrm{cont},(i)}_{xn} \in \mathcal{V}^*$$

分别表示其角色、指令和契约文本。对每个跳转 m ∈ [Mxi],令 $h^{\mathrm{hop},(i)}_{xm} \in \mathcal{V}^*$ 表示对应的工作流步骤(见图 4)。我们使用 text-embedding-3-large 和 all-mpnet-base-v2 对每个文本组件进行嵌入,随后进行 $\ell_2$ 归一化。若某组件超出嵌入模型的输入长度限制,则将其分块,分别嵌入各块后对归一化块嵌入取平均。图 10 展示了这些组件嵌入的 t-SNE 和 UMAP 投影。每行对应一种组件类型:角色、指令、契约或跳转。标记形状表示 RHI 迭代轮次 i ∈ {1,2,3,4},颜色表示任务 x。对于固定的任务和迭代轮次,角色、指令和契约行各包含 Nxi 个点,跳转行包含 Mxi 个点。

在所有三个领域中,图 10a、10b 和 10c 均显示契约呈现出最为清晰的任务相关聚类。该规律在不同嵌入模型和投影方法下均保持一致,表明最大的语义变化发生在编排器与子智能体之间的信息接口处。跳转和指令也表现出任务相关聚类,但不如契约显著。相比之下,角色的可分性较低,这可能是因为我们基准测试中许多机器学习研究任务共享相同的高层角色,例如数据准备、模型训练、验证和报告撰写。

结合第 5 节的性能结果,上述发现表明 RHI 的性能提升主要源于任务特定的协调机制,而非单纯依赖更长的推理链。具体而言,契约和跳转决定了信息的传递内容以及多智能体工作流的推进方式。它们的突出地位在意料之中,因为框架优化器的系统提示(system prompt)明确强调了多智能体工作流的精化,尤其是契约和跳转(见附录 C)。因此,我们将观察到的组件级分离解读为 RHI 学习到任务特定协调接口的相关证据,同时强调此嵌入分析属于相关性分析而非因果分析。

**余弦相似度分析。** 我们接下来用余弦相似度定量刻画组件演化。令 hc ∈ {role, instr, cont, hop}。对于任务 x、迭代轮次 i 和组件类型 hc,令 $K^{\mathrm{hc}}_{xi}$ 为该类型的组件数量:当 hc 为角色、指令或契约时 $K^{\mathrm{hc}}_{xi} = N_{xi}$,当 hc 为跳转时 $K^{\mathrm{hc}}_{xi} = M_{xi}$。令 $z^{\mathrm{hc},(i)}_{xk}$ 表示第 k 个 hc 类型组件的归一化嵌入。我们

Figure 11c reports this quantity.
Figure 11a shows that contracts reach high consecutive similarity (0.48 → 0.66 → 0.69 → 0.72) earlier than the other components, indicating that contract updates stabilize relatively quickly. Hops and instructions exhibit intermediate stabilization, whereas roles change more gradually (0.28 → 0.31 → 0.33 → 0.31). Together with the low-dimensional visualizations in Figure 11, these results suggest that RHI specializes contracts to the task within a few iterations, while roles, instructions, and hops continue to evolve more gradually. One possible explanation is that the pairwise feedback from Leval provides a stronger learning signal for contract refinement than for the other components. Alternatively, it may indicate that our current feedback loop design is sufficient to drive effective contract updates but not the refinement of other components. This suggests that improving the quality and specificity of evaluator feedback is a promising direction
for future work.

我们通过对两轮迭代中所有同类组件 $h_c$ 之间的两两余弦相似度取平均,再对所有任务取平均,来度量 $h_c^{(i)}$ 与 $h_c^{(j)}$ 之间的相似度。我们将该量定义为 cross_cos 相似度:

$$
\text{cross\_cos}(h_c^{(i)}, h_c^{(j)}) = \frac{1}{|X|} \sum_{x \in X} \frac{1}{K_{xi}^{h_c} K_{xj}^{h_c}} \sum_{k=1}^{K_{xi}^{h_c}} \sum_{k'=1}^{K_{xj}^{h_c}} \cos\!\left(z_{xk}^{h_c,(i)},\, z_{xk'}^{h_c,(j)}\right).
$$

图 11a 报告了 $\text{cross\_cos}(h_c^{(i)}, h_c^{(i+1)})$,图 11b 报告了与初始测试框架的相似度 $\text{cross\_cos}(h_c^{(0)}, h_c^{(i)})$。对于固定迭代轮次下的跨任务相似度,令 $P = \{(x, x') : x, x' \in X,\, x \neq x'\}$,定义

$$
\text{pair\_cos}(h_c^{(i)}) = \frac{1}{|P|} \sum_{(x,x') \in P} \frac{1}{K_{xi}^{h_c} K_{x'i}^{h_c}} \sum_{k=1}^{K_{xi}^{h_c}} \sum_{k'=1}^{K_{x'i}^{h_c}} \cos\!\left(z_{xk}^{h_c,(i)},\, z_{x'k'}^{h_c,(i)}\right).
$$

图 11c 报告了该量。

图 11a 显示,contract(合约)的相邻相似度(0.48 → 0.66 → 0.69 → 0.72)比其他组件更早达到较高水平,说明 contract 的更新相对较快趋于稳定。hop(跳转)和 instruction(指令)呈现中等程度的稳定化,而 role(角色)的变化更为缓慢(0.28 → 0.31 → 0.33 → 0.31)。结合图 11 中的低维可视化结果,这些发现表明:RHI 能在少数几轮迭代内将 contract 专化为特定任务,而 role、instruction 和 hop 则以更缓慢的速度持续演化。一种可能的解释是,Leval 的两两对比反馈为 contract 细化提供了比其他组件更强的学习信号。另一种解释则是,当前的反馈回路设计足以驱动有效的 contract 更新,但尚不足以推动其他组件的细化。这提示我们,提升评估器反馈的质量与针对性是未来工作的一个有前景的方向。

6.3. What objective function does RHI implicitly optimize?
RHI is not trained against an explicit scalar reward or explicit loss function. At iteration 𝑖, the harness optimizer Lharness receives the current harness 𝐻 (𝑖) 𝑥 and the accumulated self-comparison history D(𝑖) 𝑥 , where the history is produced by pairwise judgments from Leval. It then proposes the next textual harness 𝐻 (𝑖+1) 𝑥 ; see Step 5 of Figure 2. Thus, RHI is best understood as black-box search over prompt-represented harnesses under an implicit preference induced by the Lharness prompt, the base model underlying Lharness, and addtionally feedback from Leval. Therefore, the goal of this section is not to identify the true latent objective of Lharness or to test whether such an objective improves the harness. Instead, we propose a plausible objective function that explains the harness update trajectory observed in our experiments, motivated by the analysis in Section 6.2. Based on these observations, we formulate an information-theoretic
objective as a hypothesis for future harness optimization methods.

and encourages harness components to special-

Figure 12 | Schematic illustration of the objective implicitly induced by RHI. The external factor
𝑓ext is controlled by the Lharness prompt, while the internal factor 𝑓int is determined by how the
base model underlying Lharness interprets feedback and generates next harness.
6.3.1. Evidence for an implicit objective function
Before presenting our hypothesis, we first provide empirical evidence that RHI exhibits an implicit objective. The harness component analysis in Section 6.2.2 provides two observations. First, Figure 11a shows systematic stabilization across iterations, most clearly for contracts. Second, Figure 10 shows that the evolved component representations remain task-dependent, especially for contracts and, to a lesser extent, hops and instructions. Thus, the update trajectory is neither arbitrary nor collapsing to a generic harness template. Instead, it is consistent with movement
toward task-specific harness components.
These observations do not prove that RHI optimizes a unique scalar objective. They do, however, motivate modeling its update trajectory as being guided by an implicit preference over task-specific
harness components.
6.3.2. Information-theoretic hypothesis for the implicit objective
We now state the information-theoretic hypothesis suggested by these observations using the same component notation as above. Let 𝑋 ∼ 𝑝X denote a random task sampled from the benchmark distribution, and let 𝑥 denote a realized task. Let 𝑔𝑖 be the task-to-harness mapping induced by RHI at iteration 𝑖, so that H(𝑖) 𝑥 = 𝑔𝑖(𝑥). Let C denote a general set of harness-component types. For a task 𝑥, iteration 𝑖, and component type hc ∈ C, let 𝐾hc 𝑥𝑖
be the number of components of

## 6.3. RHI 隐式优化的目标函数是什么?

RHI 并非针对显式标量奖励或显式损失函数进行训练。在第 $i$ 轮迭代时,测试框架优化器 $\mathcal{L}_\text{harness}$ 接收当前测试框架 $H_x^{(i)}$ 和累积的自比较历史 $D_x^{(i)}$(该历史由 $\mathcal{L}_\text{eval}$ 的两两判断生成),随后提出下一个文本测试框架 $H_x^{(i+1)}$(见图 2 中的第 5 步)。因此,RHI 最适合被理解为在由 $\mathcal{L}_\text{harness}$ 提示词(prompt)、$\mathcal{L}_\text{harness}$ 所依赖的基础模型以及 $\mathcal{L}_\text{eval}$ 的反馈共同诱导的隐式偏好下,对以提示词表示的测试框架进行黑盒搜索。因此,本节的目标不在于识别 $\mathcal{L}_\text{harness}$ 的真实潜在目标,也不在于验证该目标是否能改进测试框架,而是提出一个合理的目标函数假设,以解释我们在实验中观察到的测试框架更新轨迹。该假设以第 6.2 节的分析为出发点,并将其形式化为一个信息论目标函数,以供未来测试框架优化方法参考。

Harness[i]

(a) 测试框架的更新由一个可控的外部因子 $f_\text{ext}$ 和一个依赖模型的内部因子 $f_\text{int}$ 共同塑造。 (b) 我们将 $f_\text{int}$ 解释为功能专化引导,它抑制冗余重叠,并促使测试框架组件专化为各自独立的功能模块。

图 12 | RHI 隐式诱导目标函数的示意图。外部因子 $f_\text{ext}$ 由 $\mathcal{L}_\text{harness}$ 的提示词控制,内部因子 $f_\text{int}$ 则由 $\mathcal{L}_\text{harness}$ 所依赖的基础模型如何解读反馈并生成下一个测试框架所决定。

### 6.3.1. 隐式目标函数的实证依据

在提出假设之前,我们首先提供 RHI 存在隐式目标的实证证据。第 6.2.2 节的测试框架组件分析给出了两条观察。第一,图 11a 显示各轮迭代间存在系统性的稳定化趋势,在 contract 上尤为明显。第二,图 10 显示演化后的组件表示仍依赖于具体任务,尤其是 contract,以及在较小程度上的 hop 和 instruction。因此,更新轨迹既非随机,也未退化为通用测试框架模板,而是与向任务特定测试框架组件演进的方向相一致。

上述观察并不能证明 RHI 优化了某个唯一的标量目标。然而,它们确实为将 RHI 的更新轨迹建模为受任务特定测试框架组件隐式偏好引导这一假设提供了动机。

### 6.3.2. 隐式目标函数的信息论假设

我们现在利用与上文相同的组件记号,阐述由上述观察所启发的信息论假设。令 $X \sim p_X$ 表示从基准分布中随机采样的任务,$x$ 为一个具体实现的任务。令 $g_i$ 为 RHI 在第 $i$ 轮迭代时诱导的任务到测试框架的映射,使得 $H_x^{(i)} = g_i(x)$。令 $C$ 表示测试框架组件类型的一般集合。对于任务 $x$、迭代轮次 $i$ 以及组件类型 $h_c \in C$,令 $K_{xi}^{h_c}$ 为

Hypothesis: information-theoretic implicit objective of RHI
We hypothesize that the observed RHI trajectory is consistent with increasing the following
information-theoretic functional over task-specific harness mappings:
𝐽(𝑔𝑖) = ∑︁ hc∈Cext 𝐾hc 𝑋𝑖 𝐾hc 𝑋𝑖 ∑︁ 𝑘=1

 | {z } 𝑓int , 𝛽 > 0. (6) Here, 𝑓ext measures task information in the externally emphasized component types, while 𝑓int measures task-conditional dependence among all harness-component representations. Averaging over 𝐾hc 𝑋𝑖 in 𝑓ext prevents component types with more instances from dominating
the objective solely because they contain more entries.
We hypothesize that the implicit objective consists of two complementary factors (Figure 12a). The first is an external, controllable factor, 𝑓ext, specified by the system prompt of Lharness. In our implementation, the prompt emphasizes improving the multi-agent workflow, particularly communication contracts and hops (Figure 3; see system prompt of Lharness in Appendix C). Consequently, 𝑓ext encourages RHI to optimize harness components related to information flow and orchestration. The second is an internal, model-dependent factor, 𝑓int, arising from the base LLMunderlying Lharness. Thisfactordetermineshowtextualfeedbackisinterpretedandtranslated into edits of roles, instructions, contracts, and hops. We interpret 𝑓int as providing functional specialization guidance: while 𝑓ext promotes task-specific workflow refinement, 𝑓int encourages harness components to specialize into distinct functional roles by reducing task-conditioned redundancy (Figure 12b). The overall intuition is that maximizing task information alone admits degenerate solutions in which multiple harness components repeat the same task description or coordination rule. Functional specialization guidance instead encourages a modular decomposition: roles define expertise, instructions define agent behavior, contracts define the information to communicate,
and hops define the control flow.
The objective in Equation (6) formalizes this intuition. Specifically, 𝑓ext measures how well the prompt-emphasized harness components encode task information useful for decomposition, communication, and orchestration. In our implementation, these components are contracts and hops. Meanwhile, 𝑓int does not act as a classical regularizer that trades off the primary objective against model complexity.Instead, we interpret it as functional specialization guidance1: 1This terminology is loosely inspired by classifier-free diffusion guidance, where conditional and unconditional score estimates are combined to steer generation toward condition-relevant structure (Ho and Salimans, 2022). The

### 中文（翻译）

对其进行类型标注并记作 $h_{\mathrm{hc},(i)}^{x_k} \in V^*$,$k \in [K_{\mathrm{hc}}^{x_i}]$,表示类型 hc 的第 $k$ 个分量。因此,在我们当前的工作流框架(harness)中,$h_{\mathrm{role},(i)}^{x_n}$、$h_{\mathrm{instr},(i)}^{x_n}$ 和 $h_{\mathrm{cont},(i)}^{x_n}$ 分别表示智能体(agent)$n$ 的角色(role)、指令(instruction)和契约(contract),而 $h_{\mathrm{hop},(i)}^{x_m}$ 表示工作流(workflow)的跳转步骤(hop)$m$。设 $z_{\mathrm{hc},(i)}^{x_k}$ 为 $h_{\mathrm{hc},(i)}^{x_k}$ 的归一化嵌入,与上文分量相似性分析中的定义一致。对应的随机嵌入为 $z_{\mathrm{hc},(i)}^{X_k}$。最后,设 $C_{\mathrm{ext}} \subseteq C$ 表示 $L_{\mathrm{harness}}$ 提示词(prompt)所强调的分量类型;在我们当前的 RHI 实现中,

$$C = \{\text{role}, \text{instr}, \text{cont}, \text{hop}\}, \quad C_{\mathrm{ext}} = \{\text{cont}, \text{hop}\}.$$

**假设:RHI 的信息论隐式目标**

我们假设观测到的 RHI 轨迹与在任务特定工作流框架映射上最大化以下信息论泛函一致:

$$J(g_i) = \underbrace{\sum_{\mathrm{hc} \in C_{\mathrm{ext}}} \frac{1}{K_{\mathrm{hc}}^{X_i}} \sum_{k=1}^{K_{\mathrm{hc}}^{X_i}} I\!\left(z_{\mathrm{hc},(i)}^{X_k}; X\right)}_{f_{\mathrm{ext}}} - \beta \underbrace{\mathrm{TC}\!\left(z_{\mathrm{hc},(i)}^{X_k} : \mathrm{hc} \in C,\, k \in [K_{\mathrm{hc}}^{X_i}] \,\middle|\, X\right)}_{f_{\mathrm{int}}}, \quad \beta > 0. \tag{6}$$

其中,$f_{\mathrm{ext}}$ 度量外部强调分量类型中的任务信息,而 $f_{\mathrm{int}}$ 度量所有工作流框架分量表示之间在任务条件下的依赖性。在 $f_{\mathrm{ext}}$ 中对 $K_{\mathrm{hc}}^{X_i}$ 取平均,是为了防止实例数量更多的分量类型仅因条目更多而主导目标函数。

我们假设隐式目标由两个互补因素构成(图 12a)。第一个是外部可控因素 $f_{\mathrm{ext}}$,由 $L_{\mathrm{harness}}$ 的系统提示词指定。在我们的实现中,该提示词强调改进多智能体工作流,尤其是通信契约和跳转步骤(图 3;见附录 C 中 $L_{\mathrm{harness}}$ 的系统提示词)。因此,$f_{\mathrm{ext}}$ 引导 RHI 优化与信息流和编排相关的工作流框架分量。第二个是内部模型依赖因素 $f_{\mathrm{int}}$,源自 $L_{\mathrm{harness}}$ 底层的基础 LLM。该因素决定如何解释文本反馈并将其转化为对角色、指令、契约和跳转步骤的编辑。我们将 $f_{\mathrm{int}}$ 解释为提供功能专业化(functional specialization)引导:$f_{\mathrm{ext}}$ 促进任务特定的工作流精化,而 $f_{\mathrm{int}}$ 则通过降低任务条件冗余来鼓励工作流框架分量专业化为不同的功能角色(图 12b)。总体直觉在于,仅最大化任务信息会导致退化解——多个工作流框架分量重复相同的任务描述或协调规则。功能专业化引导则鼓励模块化分解:角色定义专业领域,指令定义智能体行为,契约定义需要传递的信息,跳转步骤定义控制流。

公式 (6) 中的目标函数将上述直觉形式化。具体而言,$f_{\mathrm{ext}}$ 度量提示词强调的工作流框架分量对任务信息的编码质量,这些信息有助于任务分解、通信和编排;在我们的实现中,这些分量为契约和跳转步骤。与此同时,$f_{\mathrm{int}}$ 并非在主要目标与模型复杂度之间进行权衡的经典正则化项,而是被我们解释为功能专业化引导[^1]:

[^1]: 该术语的灵感来源于无分类器扩散引导(classifier-free diffusion guidance),其中条件和无条件得分估计相结合,以引导生成朝向与条件相关的结构(Ho and Salimans, 2022)。此处的类比是概念层面而非代数层面:任务条件总相关项并不定义扩散得分,但它发挥着类似的引导作用,推动工作流框架远离通用冗余重叠,走向各具特色的分量功能。

This hypothesis provides one plausible characterization of how general harness optimization should behave. Specifically, we hypothesize that making harness components more task-specific alone may still yield a suboptimal harness if the components remain semantically redundant. In contrast, reducing redundancy while preserving task specificity can produce a more effective harness (Figure 12b). The empirical analyses in the Sections 6.3.3 and 6.3.4 instantiate this general hypothesis with the four component types used in our current RHI harness, C = {role,instr,cont,hop}. 该假设对通用工作流框架优化应有的行为提供了一种合理刻画。具体而言,我们假设:仅使工作流框架分量更具任务特异性,若分量在语义上仍存在冗余,仍可能产生次优工作流框架;相比之下,在保持任务特异性的同时减少冗余,可以产生更有效的工作流框架(图 12b)。第 6.3.3 节和第 6.3.4 节中的实证分析以我们当前 RHI 工作流框架中使用的四种分量类型 $C = \{\text{role}, \text{instr}, \text{cont}, \text{hop}\}$ 对这一一般假设进行了具体验证。对于每个 $H_x^{(i)}$,文本分量的总数为 $\sum_{\mathrm{hc} \in C} K_{\mathrm{hc}}^{x_i}$。在操作层面,我们的总相关(total correlation)估计器将这些分量归入上述四个类型块,而非对每个智能体特定分量单独估计协方差块。

### 6.3.3. $f_{\mathrm{ext}}$ 递增的证据

我们首先检验 $f_{\mathrm{ext}}$ 所强调的分量是否变得对任务更具信息量。对于每个任务 $x$、迭代轮次 $i$、分量类型 hc 和分量索引 $k$,我们估计分量表示与任务表示之间的互信息:

$$I\!\left(\mathrm{emb}(h_{\mathrm{hc},(i)}^{x_k});\, \mathrm{emb}(x)\right), \quad \mathrm{hc} \in \{\text{role}, \text{instr}, \text{cont}, \text{hop}\},\; k \in [K_{\mathrm{hc}}^{x_i}].$$

我们将该诊断量记为 $I(\mathrm{hc};\mathrm{task})$。嵌入使用 text-embedding-3-large 或 all-mpnet-base-v2 计算。我们使用全局拟合的白化 PCA 将嵌入降至 $d=10$ 维,然后计算高斯典型相关互信息:

$$I(\mathrm{hc};\mathrm{task}) = -\sum_r \log(1 - \rho_r^2),$$

其中 $\rho_r$ 为分量块与任务块之间的典型相关系数。由于有限样本互信息估计存在正偏差,我们同时报告原始插入估计值和通过打乱任务标签所得的置换去偏估计值。绝对值应理解为诊断统计量,而非原始文本的精确互信息。

We therefore estimate task-conditional total correlation by per-task centering: for each task, we subtract the within-task mean representation of each component and apply Equation (7) to the residuals. Overall, Tables 2 and 3 support our claim: RHI makes externally targeted coordination components, especially contracts and hops, more task-specific while reducing residual redundancy among harness components. The evidence is correlational and depends on embedding-based estimators, so it should not be read as a proof of the optimizer LLM’s true latent objective. Rather, it provides a compact and testable hypothesis for RHI’s implicit update objective: task-specific coordination 2The hop block is design-level: it provides one vector per (𝑥, 𝑖) and is therefore constant across agents for a fixed task and iteration. Per-task centering removes this block, so the per-agent task-conditional estimate effectively measures TC(role,instr,cont | task). Hop contributes to unconditional total correlation but not to this per-agent
conditional estimate.

综上,表 2 和表 3 支持我们的论断:RHI 使外部面向协调的组件——尤其是契约(contract)和跳转(hop)——更具任务针对性,同时减少了工作流框架组件间的残余冗余。这些证据属于相关性分析,依赖于基于嵌入的估计量,因此不应将其解读为优化器 LLM 真实潜在目标的证明。相反,它为 RHI 的隐式更新目标提供了一个简洁且可验证的假设:当工作流框架在契约和跳转中编码了与工作流相关的信息,且功能专化引导机制抑制了跨组件的信息重复时,任务特定的协调能力得以提升。

2跳转块属于设计层面:它为每个 (x, i) 提供一个向量,因此对于固定的任务和迭代轮次,其值在所有智能体间保持不变。逐任务中心化将消除该块,因而逐智能体的任务条件估计实际上度量的是 TC(role,instr,cont | task)。跳转对无条件全相关性有贡献,但不影响此逐智能体条件估计。
