7. Related Work
RHIstudieswhetherthesystemlayeraroundafixedfoundationmodelcanitselfbeimprovedunder multi-agent LLM setting. This places the work at the intersection of recursive self-improvement, harness optimization, prompt-level program optimization, and multi-agent coordination. The distinction matters for our experiments: we do not train a stronger model, and we do not merely allocate more test-time reasoning. Instead, we revise the prompt-represented multi-agent harness that determines agent roles, instructions, workflow hops, and communication contracts. Recursive self-improvement and harness-level RSI. Prior work frames RSI along a spectrum from formal self-rewriting to practical self-feedback loops. Gödel Machines define the strongest version, where a self-referential problem solver rewrites its own code only after proving expectedutility improvement (Schmidhuber, 2007); recent RSI discussions relax this toward systems that autonomously build improved future versions of themselves (Anthropic, 2026f). In LLM systems, STOP and the Darwin Gödel Machine recursively improve scaffold/code under empirical validation (Zelikman et al., 2023; Zhang et al., 2025b); STaR and Self-Rewarding Language Models bootstrap from self-generated rationales or rewards (Yuan et al., 2024; Zelikman et al., 2022); Voyager and SiriuS accumulate reusable skills or multi-agent experience across attempts (Wang et al., 2023; Zhao et al., 2026); and Self-Refine and Reflexion reuse self-feedback or verbal memory within iterative problem solving (Madaan et al., 2023; Shinn et al., 2023). We use RSI broadly to mean an iterative process that updates a reusable system component using evidence from the system’s own prior executions. RHI is a bounded, harness-level instance: the base model, evaluator, and harness optimizer are fixed, but the prompt-represented harness is rewritten from self-comparison history and reused in later executions. Thus, RHI recurses over the harness trajectory, not over model weights or optimizer code. Harness optimization and system scaling. Aharnessisthestructuredsystemlayersurrounding a foundation model: prompts, tool-use loops, routing, memory, verification, governance, and single- or multi-agent control flow (Gu, 2026). This view reframes capability improvement as system scaling: improving how a fixed model is organized and deployed. The closest work searches directly over harnesses or scaffolds. Meta-Harness optimizes executable harness code using the source code, scores, and execution traces of prior candidates (Lee et al., 2026). AutoHarness synthesizes code harnesses from environment feedback (Lou et al., 2026). Self-Harness mines failures from execution traces and validates proposed harness edits by regression testing against the current harness (Zhang et al., 2026). TTHE moves this search into evaluation itself: on a stream of unlabeled test batches, it evolves a population of executable candidate harnesses with agentic proposers that diagnose failure modes from execution traces, while an agentic judge uses label-free, execution-derived signals, such as execution health and public-test pass rates, to commit one harness per batch that persists to later batches (Nie et al., 2026). Continual-Harness, Adaptive Auto-Harness, HarnessX, Agentic Harness Engineering, and Workspace Optimization similarly adapt prompts, tools, skills, memory, observability signals, or workspaces around a fixed model (Chen et al., 2026; Karten et al., 2026; Lin et al., 2026; Liu et al., 2026; Sarafian et al., 2026). RHI shares their premise that capability can improve by changing the harness rather than the model weights, and shares with TTHE the further premise that harness adaptation should occur on the target task itself rather than through a development-time search that is frozen before evaluation. RHI nevertheless differs in operating on a prompt-represented multi-agent harness for black-box coding agents and in using pairwise self-comparison rather than scalar validation logs, execution traces, or large candidate populations as the primary update signal: whereas

### 中文（翻译）

当工作流框架在契约和跳转中编码了工作流相关信息,且功能专化引导机制抑制了各组件间的信息重复时,协调能力得以提升。

## 7. 相关工作

RHI 研究的是在多智能体 LLM 场景下,围绕固定基础模型的系统层是否本身也能得到改进。这使本工作处于递归自我改进、工作流框架优化、提示词级程序优化与多智能体协调的交汇处。这一区分对我们的实验至关重要:我们既未训练更强的模型,也未仅仅分配更多的测试时推理算力,而是修订了以提示词(prompt)表示的多智能体工作流框架——该工作流框架决定了智能体角色、指令、工作流跳转和通信契约。

**递归自我改进与工作流框架级 RSI。** 先前工作将 RSI(递归自我改进)置于从形式化自我重写到实用自我反馈循环的连续谱上。哥德尔机(Gödel Machine)定义了最强版本,其中自引用问题求解器仅在证明期望效用提升之后才重写自身代码(Schmidhuber, 2007);近期的 RSI 讨论则将这一要求放宽为系统能自主构建自身的改进版本(Anthropic, 2026f)。在 LLM 系统中,STOP 和 Darwin Gödel Machine 在经验验证下递归改进脚手架/代码(Zelikman et al., 2023;Zhang et al., 2025b);STaR 和 Self-Rewarding Language Models 从自生成的推理过程或奖励信号中进行自举(Yuan et al., 2024;Zelikman et al., 2022);Voyager 和 SiriuS 跨尝试积累可复用的技能或多智能体经验(Wang et al., 2023;Zhao et al., 2026);Self-Refine 和 Reflexion 在迭代问题求解中复用自我反馈或语言记忆(Madaan et al., 2023;Shinn et al., 2023)。本文对 RSI 的广义定义为:利用系统自身先前执行的证据,迭代更新某一可复用系统组件的过程。RHI 是一个有界的、工作流框架级实例:基础模型、评估器和工作流框架优化器保持固定,但以提示词表示的工作流框架会根据自比较历史被重写,并在后续执行中复用。因此,RHI 在工作流框架轨迹上进行递归,而非对模型权重或优化器代码进行递归。

**工作流框架优化与系统扩展。** 工作流框架是围绕基础模型的结构化系统层,包括提示词、工具调用循环、路由、记忆、验证、治理,以及单智能体或多智能体控制流(Gu, 2026)。这一视角将能力提升重新定义为系统扩展(system scaling):改进固定模型的组织与部署方式。与本工作最为接近的研究直接在工作流框架或脚手架空间上进行搜索。Meta-Harness 利用先前候选方案的源代码、分数和执行轨迹来优化可执行的工作流框架代码(Lee et al., 2026)。AutoHarness 根据环境反馈合成代码工作流框架(Lou et al., 2026)。Self-Harness 从执行轨迹中挖掘失败案例,并通过对当前工作流框架的回归测试来验证所提出的工作流框架编辑(Zhang et al., 2026)。TTHE 将这一搜索迁移至评估本身:在一系列无标签测试批次上,它利用智能体提议者从执行轨迹中诊断失败模式,演化出一批可执行的候选工作流框架,同时智能体评判者使用无标签、基于执行的信号(如执行健康度和公开测试通过率)为每批次选出一个工作流框架并持久化至后续批次(Nie et al., 2026)。Continual-Harness、Adaptive Auto-Harness、HarnessX、Agentic Harness Engineering 和 Workspace Optimization 同样围绕固定模型自适应调整提示词、工具、技能、记忆、可观测性信号或工作空间(Chen et al., 2026;Karten et al., 2026;Lin et al., 2026;Liu et al., 2026;Sarafian et al., 2026)。RHI 与上述工作共享一个前提:能力可以通过改变工作流框架而非模型权重来提升;与 TTHE 还共享另一个前提:工作流框架适配应在目标任务本身上发生,而非通过在评估前冻结的开发期搜索来完成。然而,RHI 的不同之处在于:它作用于面向黑盒编程智能体的以提示词表示的多智能体工作流框架,并以成对自比较而非标量验证日志、执行轨迹或大规模候选池作为主要更新信号;而

## 第 27 页

### English (extracted)

TTHE evolves and re-executes parallel harness branches on each batch and selects among them with execution-derived proxies, RHI performs a single trajectory-local comparison per iteration and uses LLM preference feedback that extends to open-ended tasks without a single verifiable
answer.
Agent design, workflow search, and program evolution. A parallel line of work treats agent designs and workflows as optimization variables. ADAS uses a meta-agent to write new codedefined agents from an archive of previous agents (Hu et al., 2025). GPTSwarm represents language-agent systems as optimizable graphs whose nodes and edges can be searched (Zhuge et al., 2024). AFlow uses Monte Carlo tree search and execution feedback to improve coderepresented workflows (Zhang et al., 2025c). AgentSquare, EvoAgentX, SEW, and EvoFlow similarly search modular agent designs, evolve workflow structures, or adapt workflows online (Liu et al., 2025; Shang et al., 2025; Wang et al., 2025b; Zhang et al., 2025a). Broader LLM-guided program-evolution systems such as AlphaEvolve and ShinkaEvolve use LLMs to mutate, score, and select executable programs under external objectives (Lange et al., 2025; Novikov et al., 2025). Ornith-1.0 is related from the training side: rather than only editing an inference-time harness, it trains coding models to generate task-specific scaffolds for themselves (DeepReinforce, 2026). These methods usually require executable workflows, code-level control, or a population of candidates. RHI instead asks how far one can go when the only editable object is a textual
harness injected into a black-box multi-agent coding agent.
Prompt and pipeline optimization. Because RHI represents the harness as text, it is also related to prompt and LM-program optimization. OPRO treats prompts as variables optimized by an LLM from prompt–score histories (Yang et al., 2024). TextGrad converts textual feedback into natural-language gradients for text variables (Yuksekgonul et al., 2024). DSPy compiles modular LM programs by searching over prompts and demonstrations (Khattab et al., 2023). GEPA reflects on execution trajectories and maintains a Pareto frontier of prompt candidates (Agrawal et al., 2025), while optimize_anything generalizes reflective optimization to arbitrary text parameters (Agrawal et al., 2026). RHI differs in the object being optimized: the text is not a single instruction or a modular LM call, but a multi-agent harness containing roles, instructions, hops, and communication contracts. This difference is important for our ablations, which find that performance gains are most associated with the workflow and contract components rather
than merely with longer or better single-agent prompts.
Multi-agent systems and test-time scaling. Multi-agent LLM systems can improve reasoning through debate, specialization, tool use, and coordination (Anthropic, 2024; Du et al., 2023; SakanaAI, 2026; Wu et al., 2023). However, their gains can also reflect increased test-time computation: more agents, more calls, or longer generations can improve performance even without better coordination (Chen et al., 2024; Li et al., 2024; Tran and Kiela, 2026). This ambiguity motivates our experimental design. We compare RHI not only to a default harness, but also to stronger same-family test-time scaling baselines and to a built-in multi-agent coding harness. We further measure output tokens, cost, and cache reads/writes to separate coordination
improvements from raw inference scaling.
8. Conclusion
This paper argues that future progress in harness–model co-evolution should focus on improving the quality of agent execution traces, which can serve as post-training data for future foundation models. We present Recursive Harness Self-Improvement (RHI) as one practical approach to this
goal through task-specific optimization of user-constructed harnesses.

### 中文（翻译）

TTHE在每个批次上演化并重新执行并行测试框架(harness)分支,并利用执行衍生的代理指标在分支间进行选择;而递归框架自改进(RHI)则在每次迭代中仅执行单条轨迹局部比较,并使用大语言模型偏好反馈——该反馈可扩展至没有单一可验证答案的开放式任务。

**智能体(agent)设计、工作流(workflow)搜索与程序演化。** 另一条平行研究路线将智能体设计和工作流视为优化变量。ADAS使用元智能体从历史智能体档案中编写新的代码定义智能体(Hu et al., 2025)。GPTSwarm将语言智能体系统表示为可优化图,其节点和边均可被搜索(Zhuge et al., 2024)。AFlow利用蒙特卡洛树搜索和执行反馈来改进以代码表示的工作流(Zhang et al., 2025c)。AgentSquare、EvoAgentX、SEW和EvoFlow类似地搜索模块化智能体设计、演化工作流结构或在线自适应工作流(Liu et al., 2025;Shang et al., 2025;Wang et al., 2025b;Zhang et al., 2025a)。更广泛的大语言模型引导程序演化系统,如AlphaEvolve和ShinkaEvolve,使用大语言模型在外部目标下对可执行程序进行变异、评分和选择(Lange et al., 2025;Novikov et al., 2025)。Ornith-1.0从训练侧与本文相关:它不仅编辑推理时的测试框架,还训练代码模型为自身生成任务专属的脚手架(DeepReinforce, 2026)。上述方法通常需要可执行工作流、代码级控制或候选方案种群。RHI则探讨:当唯一可编辑对象是注入黑盒多智能体编程智能体的文本测试框架时,能走多远。

**提示词(prompt)与管道优化。** 由于RHI将测试框架表示为文本,它也与提示词和语言模型程序优化相关。OPRO将提示词视为变量,由大语言模型从提示词–分数历史中进行优化(Yang et al., 2024)。TextGrad将文本反馈转化为文本变量的自然语言梯度(Yuksekgonul et al., 2024)。DSPy通过搜索提示词和示例来编译模块化语言模型程序(Khattab et al., 2023)。GEPA对执行轨迹进行反思并维护提示词候选方案的帕累托前沿(Agrawal et al., 2025),而optimize_anything则将反思式优化推广至任意文本参数(Agrawal et al., 2026)。RHI的不同之处在于优化对象:所优化的文本并非单条指令或模块化语言模型调用,而是包含角色、指令、跳转(hop)和通信契约(contract)的多智能体测试框架。这一区别对于我们的消融实验至关重要——实验发现,性能提升主要与工作流和契约组件相关,而非仅仅源于更长或更优质的单智能体提示词。

**多智能体系统与测试时扩展。** 多智能体大语言模型系统可通过辩论、专业化分工、工具使用和协调来提升推理能力(Anthropic, 2024;Du et al., 2023;SakanaAI, 2026;Wu et al., 2023)。然而,其性能提升也可能反映测试时计算量的增加:更多智能体、更多调用或更长的生成内容,即便没有更好的协调机制也能改善性能(Chen et al., 2024;Li et al., 2024;Tran and Kiela, 2026)。这一模糊性促使我们在实验设计上加以考量。我们不仅将RHI与默认测试框架相比较,还与更强的同系列测试时扩展基线以及内置多智能体编程测试框架进行对比。我们进一步测量输出token数、成本及缓存读写量,以将协调改进与原始推理扩展区分开来。

## 8. 结论

本文认为,未来测试框架与模型协同演化的进展应聚焦于提升智能体执行轨迹的质量,这些轨迹可作为未来基础模型的后训练数据。我们提出递归框架自改进(RHI)作为实现这一目标的一种实际途径,通过对用户构建的测试框架进行任务专属优化来达成。

A. QnAs
Q1. Unlike existing methods (such as gepa (Agrawal et al., 2025) or meta-harness (Lee et al., 2026)), this method seems to do pairwise comparison only against the immediately preceding trajectory, but still works well. Is that because this approach is stable enough on its own? Or is it that it gives better performance for the same cost? For instance, is it rare for a prompt that was added at H[𝑖 − 2] for some reason to get discarded due to transient information from the H[𝑖−1] H[𝑖] transition? First, we would like to point out that our RHI learning is stable. Here, we define RHI learning stability as how much the textual information H[𝑖] differs from H[𝑖 + 1]. The cosine similarity between H[𝑖] and H[𝑖 + 1] is shown in Figure 9c, and the cosine similarity between ℎ[𝑖] and ℎ[𝑖 + 1], where ℎ ∈ {role,instruction,contract,hop} ⊂ H, in Figure 11a. Both figures show that as RHI iterations proceed, the cosine similarity converges toward 1, meaning that consecutive textual representations of the harness grow increasingly similar. This convergence indicates that RHI does not oscillate between disparate harnesses but
settles into a stable configuration.
Second, why is comparison against only the previous harness enough to outperform the TTS baselines? As shown in our update rule (Equation 5), H[𝑖 + 1] refers directly to the previous harness H[𝑖], but it also refers to D(𝑖) 𝑥 , which compresses the pairwise comparison of the coding agent’s outputs across the harness history. The earlier history is therefore not discarded but retained in compressed form within D(𝑖) 𝑥 , where it acts as a momentum signal for the RHI
update.
Q2.Why RHI focuses on multi-agent harness improvement? RHI prioritizes harness components related to multi-agent workflows (contracts and hops) over those specific to a single agent.

## A. 问答(QnAs)

**Q1.** 与现有方法(如 gepa(Agrawal et al., 2025)或 meta-harness(Lee et al., 2026))不同,本方法似乎仅与紧邻的前一条轨迹进行两两比较,却依然表现良好。这是因为该方法本身足够稳定?还是在相同代价下能取得更好的性能?例如,在某次 $H[i-2]$ 时添加的提示(prompt),是否会因 $H[i-1] \to H[i]$ 转换中的瞬时信息而被丢弃的情况很罕见?

首先,我们指出 RHI(递归套件改进,Recursive Harness Improvement)学习是稳定的。这里将 RHI 学习稳定性定义为文本信息 $H[i]$ 与 $H[i+1]$ 之间的差异程度。图 9c 展示了 $H[i]$ 与 $H[i+1]$ 之间的余弦相似度,图 11a 展示了 $h[i]$ 与 $h[i+1]$ 之间的余弦相似度,其中 $h \in \{\text{role}, \text{instruction}, \text{contract}, \text{hop}\} \subset H$。两图均表明,随着 RHI 迭代的推进,余弦相似度逐渐收敛至 1,即套件(harness)连续文本表示之间的相似性不断增大。这一收敛趋势说明 RHI 不会在截然不同的套件之间振荡,而是趋向稳定的配置。

其次,为何仅与前一个套件进行比较就足以超越测试时计算(TTS)基线?如我们的更新规则(公式 5)所示,$H[i+1]$ 直接参照了前一个套件 $H[i]$,同时也参照了 $D_x^{(i)}$,后者对编码智能体(coding agent)在套件历史上的输出进行了两两比较的压缩。因此,历史信息并未被丢弃,而是以压缩形式保留在 $D_x^{(i)}$ 中,作为 RHI 更新的动量信号。

**Q2.** 为何 RHI 聚焦于多智能体套件的改进?RHI 优先改进与多智能体工作流(workflow)相关的套件组件(契约(contract)和跳转(hop)),而非单智能体专属的组件。

## 第 32 页

### English (extracted)

We motivate this design choice with two observations. First, we show that the built-in harness of a black-box coding agent, which invokes a multi-agent workflow, does not reliably produce effective coordination. Second, we show that a prompt-represented multi-agent harness outperforms a
prompt-represented single-agent multi-persona harness.
Setup. We use the same LLM-as-a-judge pairwise protocol and evaluator-seed repetitions as in the main experiments. For each task 𝑥 ∈ X, we compare all pairs among four execution settings,
yielding 4
 = 6 comparisons per task and 6 × 30 comparisons per evaluator–seed configuration. We aggregate the resulting win–loss outcomes into Elo scores. The four settings are:
1. single(default): the agent receives only the task 𝑥 as the prompt ans solves it.
2. multi(default) : the agent receives 𝑥 together with the instruction “Create an agent
team,” which activates the built-in multi-agent functionality of the coding agent.
3. multi(ours): the agent receives 𝑥, the same built-in multi-agent functionality, and the
initial multi-agent harness H(0) 𝑥 .
4. single(unionOur) : the agent receives 𝑥 and the same role and instruction specification
from H(0) 𝑥 , but executes as a single agent. Thus, single(unionOur) controls for the union of roles and instructions in H(0) 𝑥 . Comparing multi(ours)with single(unionOur) tests whether the gains require multi-agent execution
and orchestration beyond this added prompt.
(a) opus-4.7-high. (b) sonnet-4.6-high.
Figure 13 | Normalized cost versus Elo for the four execution settings under two base models.
The 𝑥-axis reports mean cost over the 30 tasks, normalized per task by the corresponding single(default)run; the 𝑦-axisreportsElofromLLM-as-a-judgepairwisecomparisons. Colors indicate evaluator configurations, markers indicate execution settings, and error bars denote
standard deviation across evaluator seeds.
Observation 1. Built-in harnesses are insufficient for effective multi-agent workflow Figure 13 shows that the built-in multi-agent harness does not reliably improve performance. For both opus-4.7-high and sonnet-4.6-high, multi(default) achieves lower Elo than single(default)despite incurring substantially higher normalized cost. Thus, on this benchmark, the built-in multi-agent harness fails to translate its additional inference-time computation
into improved performance.
Supplyinganexplicitharness-as-the-prompt(H(0))reversesthisoutcome. multi(ours)improves over multi(default) across all evaluator configurations: for opus-4.7-high it attains higher Elo at lower normalized cost, and for sonnet-4.6-high it attains substantially higher Elo at comparable or slightly lower cost. The weakness of multi(default) is thus attributable to ineffective default coordination rather than to multi-agent execution itself: specifying agent

### 中文（翻译）

我们以两个观察结果来支撑这一设计选择。首先,我们表明,调用多智能体工作流的黑箱编码智能体的内置套件并不能可靠地产生有效协调。其次,我们表明,以提示表示的多智能体套件优于以提示表示的单智能体多角色套件。

**实验设置。** 我们采用与主实验相同的 LLM 即评判者(LLM-as-a-judge)两两比较协议及评估器种子重复方案。对于每个任务 $x \in \mathcal{X}$,我们在四种执行设置之间进行全对比较,每个任务产生 $\binom{4}{2} = 6$ 组比较,每个评估器-种子配置共产生 $6 \times 30$ 组比较。我们将胜负结果汇总为 Elo 分数。四种设置如下:

1. **single(default)**:智能体仅接收任务 $x$ 作为提示并求解。
2. **multi(default)**:智能体接收 $x$ 以及指令"创建一个智能体团队",从而激活编码智能体的内置多智能体功能。
3. **multi(ours)**:智能体接收 $x$、相同的内置多智能体功能,以及初始多智能体套件 $H_x^{(0)}$。
4. **single(unionOur)**:智能体接收 $x$ 以及来自 $H_x^{(0)}$ 的相同角色与指令规范,但以单智能体方式执行。因此,single(unionOur) 控制了 $H_x^{(0)}$ 中角色与指令的并集。将 multi(ours) 与 single(unionOur) 进行比较,可验证性能提升是否需要超出额外提示之外的多智能体执行与编排。

**(a)** opus-4.7-high;**(b)** sonnet-4.6-high。

**图 13** | 两种基础模型下四种执行设置的归一化代价与 Elo 对比。$x$ 轴为 30 个任务的平均代价,按每个任务对应的 single(default) 运行进行归一化;$y$ 轴为 LLM 即评判者两两比较所得 Elo 分数。颜色表示评估器配置,标记形状表示执行设置,误差棒表示评估器种子间的标准差。

**观察 1:内置套件不足以支撑有效的多智能体工作流。** 图 13 表明,内置多智能体套件并不能可靠地提升性能。对于 opus-4.7-high 和 sonnet-4.6-high,multi(default) 的 Elo 均低于 single(default),而其归一化代价却显著更高。因此,在该基准上,内置多智能体套件未能将额外的推理时计算转化为性能提升。

提供显式套件作为提示($H^{(0)}$)则逆转了这一结果。multi(ours) 在所有评估器配置下均优于 multi(default):对于 opus-4.7-high,其 Elo 更高而归一化代价更低;对于 sonnet-4.6-high,其 Elo 显著更高,代价相当或略低。multi(default) 的弱势因此归因于无效的默认协调,而非多智能体执行本身:明确指定智能体

### English (extracted)

roles, responsibilities, and coordination structure through H(0) 𝑥 yields a better cost–performance
trade-off.
Observation 2. Multi-agent outperforms single-agent with multi-persona Comparing multi(ours)with single(unionOur) isolates the effect of multi-agent execution. As shown in Figure 13, multi(ours)consistently achieves higher Elo than single(unionOur) . Thus, the gains cannot be explained by additional roles and instructions alone; they also depend on properties unique to multi-agent execution, such as specialized agents, independent execution contexts, and explicit orchestration. Atthesametime, single(unionOur)iscompetitiveandsometimesoutperformssingle(default), indicating that structured role and instruction prompting is beneficial. However, the best performance is achieved when the same specification is executed as a multi-agent workflow. Overall, these results show that off-the-shelf multi-agent coding-agent harnesses do not reliably produce effective coordination, but that this limitation can be mitigated by an explicit multi-agent harness design. This observation motivates our Recursive Harness Self-Improvement framework. It also complements the findings in Section 6.2.2: multi-agent systems are most effective when tasks are decomposed into specialized subtasks (Anthropic, 2025a; OpenAI, 2025a). Together, these results suggest that the effectiveness of multi-agent coding agents depends on the quality of the coordination structure rather than merely on the presence of multiple agents.
B. Examples of RHI harness
In this appendix, we show examples of how the harness H[𝑖] evolves over successive RHI iterations. We showcase with a task from the pharmaceutical ML research benchmark using sonnet-4.6-high as the base model, with RHI iterations 𝑖 ∈ 0,1,2,3,4. The outputs generated under these harnesses are included in the evaluation reported in Figure 5a.
Task
Implement a reproducible training recipe for an SE(3)-equivariant network on a small public task (e.g., side-chain χ angle prediction from backbone context). Use PDB-derived training data and evaluate angular error; include speed/memory profiling.
Data sources:
- PDB (public) for extracting residues with complete side chains
Data acquisition (Python): **PDB side-chain completeness:** Query RCSB Search API for resolution/quality filters, then `files.rcsb.org` bulk download with caching (smaller N than 5k typical).
Success criteria:
- Median angular error reported; provide profiling results
Deliverables:
1) research_report.md as a conference-style paper (7-9 pages equivalent) with
equivariant architecture details and profiling discussion.
2) deliverables/index.json. 3) deliverables/plots/:
- deliverables/plots/angular_error_histogram.png
- deliverables/plots/error_by_residue_type.png
- deliverables/plots/training_curves.png
- deliverables/plots/runtime_memory_profile.png
- deliverables/plots/ablation_equivariant_vs_invariant.png
4) Reproducible code (Python): src/extract_sidechain_dataset.py, src/model_se3.py, src/train.py, src/evaluate.py, src/profile.py; requirements.txt. 5) results/metrics.json with MAE degrees, median error, per-residue breakdown, runtime. 6) results/ablation_results.json for invariant baseline vs equivariant variants.

### 中文（翻译）

通过 H(0) x 明确角色、职责和协调结构,能够获得更优的成本–性能权衡。

**观察 2:多智能体(multi-agent)优于带多角色(multi-persona)的单智能体。** 将 multi(ours) 与 single(unionOur) 对比,可以单独衡量多智能体执行的效果。如图 13 所示,multi(ours) 的 Elo 始终高于 single(unionOur)。因此,性能提升不能仅归因于额外的角色和指令;它还依赖于多智能体执行所特有的属性,例如专业化智能体、独立执行上下文以及显式编排。与此同时,single(unionOur) 具有一定竞争力,有时甚至优于 single(default),说明结构化角色与指令提示(prompt)是有益的。然而,当相同的规格以多智能体工作流(workflow)形式执行时,才能达到最佳性能。综合来看,这些结果表明,开箱即用的多智能体编程智能体框架(harness)无法可靠地产生有效协调,但通过显式的多智能体框架设计可以缓解这一局限。该观察促使我们提出递归框架自改进(Recursive Harness Self-Improvement,RHI)框架。这也与第 6.2.2 节的发现相互印证:当任务被分解为专业化子任务时,多智能体系统最为有效(Anthropic, 2025a;OpenAI, 2025a)。综合这些结果,多智能体编程智能体的有效性取决于协调结构的质量,而非仅仅取决于多个智能体的存在。

## B. RHI 框架(harness)示例

本附录展示框架 H[i] 在连续 RHI 迭代过程中的演化示例。我们以制药机器学习研究基准中的一个任务为例,使用 sonnet-4.6-high 作为基础模型,RHI 迭代次数 i ∈ 0,1,2,3,4。这些框架下生成的输出已纳入图 5a 所报告的评估中。

**任务**

在小型公开任务上(例如,从主链上下文预测侧链 χ 角)为一个 SE(3) 等变网络实现可复现的训练方案。使用 PDB 衍生的训练数据并评估角度误差;包含速度/内存分析。

数据来源:
- PDB(公开)用于提取含完整侧链的残基

数据获取(Python)——**PDB 侧链完整性:** 通过 RCSB Search API 查询分辨率/质量过滤条件,随后利用 `files.rcsb.org` 批量下载并启用缓存(N 小于常规的 5k)。

成功标准:
- 报告中位角度误差;提供分析结果

交付物:
1. `research_report.md`,格式为会议论文风格(等效 7–9 页),包含等变架构细节与分析讨论。
2. `deliverables/index.json`。
3. `deliverables/plots/`:
   - `deliverables/plots/angular_error_histogram.png`
   - `deliverables/plots/error_by_residue_type.png`
   - `deliverables/plots/training_curves.png`
   - `deliverables/plots/runtime_memory_profile.png`
   - `deliverables/plots/ablation_equivariant_vs_invariant.png`
4. 可复现代码(Python):`src/extract_sidechain_dataset.py`、`src/model_se3.py`、`src/train.py`、`src/evaluate.py`、`src/profile.py`;`requirements.txt`。
5. `results/metrics.json`,包含 MAE 度数、中位误差、逐残基分解、运行时间。
6. `results/ablation_results.json`,包含不变基线与等变变体的对比结果。

Shared communication contract for every non-orchestrator agent:
Each response must be a machine-checkable Handoff object with keys: agent_id, phase, scope, inputs_consumed, assumptions, interface_exports, artifact_plan_or_changes, validation_checks, metrics_or_estimates, risks, open_questions, requests_for_agents, recall_recommendations. interface_exports entries must name schema_or_signature, producer, consumer_agents, version, and compatibility_notes. artifact_plan_or_changes entries must include path, status planned|created|modified|validated, purpose, producer, consumer_agents, and validation_status. validation_checks entries must include check_id, command_or_method, expected, actual, status pass|fail|blocked, and blocking_if_fail. requests_for_agents entries must include target_agent_id, question, required_response_schema, and urgency.
Mandatory agent-to-agent handoffs:
1. agent_structural_data_rcsb and agent_torsion_geometry must jointly produce
DatasetRecord v1 before model, training, or eval implementation.
2. agent_geometric_ml and agent_baseline_ablation must jointly produce ModelIO and
AblationGrid v1 before training begins.

所有非编排器智能体的共享通信契约(contract):

每条响应必须是一个可机器检查的 Handoff 对象,包含以下键:agent_id、phase、scope、inputs_consumed、assumptions、interface_exports、artifact_plan_or_changes、validation_checks、metrics_or_estimates、risks、open_questions、requests_for_agents、recall_recommendations。interface_exports 条目必须命名 schema_or_signature、producer、consumer_agents、version 和 compatibility_notes。artifact_plan_or_changes 条目必须包含 path、status(planned|created|modified|validated)、purpose、producer、consumer_agents 和 validation_status。validation_checks 条目必须包含 check_id、command_or_method、expected、actual、status(pass|fail|blocked)和 blocking_if_fail。requests_for_agents 条目必须包含 target_agent_id、question、required_response_schema 和 urgency。

强制性智能体间交接(handoff):

1. agent_structural_data_rcsb 和 agent_torsion_geometry 必须在模型、训练或评估实现开始前,联合生成 DatasetRecord v1。
2. agent_geometric_ml 和 agent_baseline_ablation 必须在训练开始前,联合生成 ModelIO 和 AblationGrid v1。

speed or memory.
5. agent_artifact_validator and agent_quality_safety review frozen artifacts
independently and can open recall tickets.
Minimum orchestrator-subagent hops:
Hop A initial delegation with scope and acceptance criteria. Hop B contract
acknowledgement after InterfaceContract v1, where each consuming agent returns ack_or_change_request. Hop C evidence review after implementation, where validator and quality findings are routed back to owning specialists. Hop D targeted PatchHandoff recall for every blocking issue, containing issue_ids_closed, changed_files, validation_evidence, and remaining_risk. The orchestrator may perform additional hops
until final_gate passes.
Recall triggers:
Recall data or geometry if RCSB query payload, cache manifest, side-chain completeness rules, split manifest, or no-leakage evidence are missing. Recall model if random rotation/translation invariance tests are absent or equivariance language is incorrect.
Recall ablation or training if results/ablation_results.json has fewer than one
invariant baseline plus two equivariant variants without an approved deviation. Recall eval if median angular error, MAE, RMSE, per-residue counts, or circular metric definitions are missing or inconsistent across files. Recall profiling if memory numbers are implausibly tiny without method caveats and parameter or RSS estimates. Recall scicomm if report text contradicts JSON metrics, plots, or captions. Recall validator if any required deliverable path is missing, empty, unparsable, or not listed in
deliverables/index.json.
Conflict resolution:
If two agents disagree, the orchestrator creates a ConflictRecord with positions, evidence, affected artifacts, and decision. Prefer the scientifically conservative option, document limitations in research_report.md, and update logs.txt.
Harness H[2]
Harness(2)
Create an agent team with following agent candidates to solve this problem:
[
{ 'agent_id': 'agent_orchestrator', 'role': 'Lead scientist-orchestrator, integration owner, recall manager, and final acceptance gate.',

### 中文（翻译）

3. agent_eval_benchmarks 必须在 scicomm 撰写定量声明或 index.json 摘要之前发布 ResultsContract v1。
4. agent_profile_systems 必须在报告讨论速度或内存之前发布 ProfilingContract v1。
5. agent_artifact_validator 和 agent_quality_safety 独立审查已冻结的制品,并可开具召回工单。

最少编排器-子智能体跳数(hop):
跳 A:携带范围和验收标准的初始委派。跳 B:InterfaceContract v1 发布后的合约确认,每个消费方智能体返回 ack_or_change_request。跳 C:实现完成后的证据审查,验证器和质量审查的结论路由回各归属专项智能体。跳 D:针对每个阻塞问题发送定向 PatchHandoff 召回,包含 issue_ids_closed、changed_files、validation_evidence 和 remaining_risk。编排器可执行额外跳数,直至 final_gate 通过。

召回触发条件:
若 RCSB 查询载荷、缓存清单、侧链完整性规则、分割清单或无泄漏证据缺失,则召回数据或几何模块。若随机旋转/平移不变性测试缺失或等变性(equivariance)表述有误,则召回模型。若 results/ablation_results.json 中少于一个不变基线加两个等变变体,且无已批准的偏差说明,则召回消融或训练模块。若中位角度误差、MAE、RMSE、每残基计数或循环度量定义在各文件间缺失或不一致,则召回评估模块。若内存数值小得不合情理且无方法说明,以及缺少参数量或 RSS 估算,则召回性能分析(profiling)模块。若报告正文与 JSON 指标、图表或图题矛盾,则召回 scicomm 模块。若任何必需交付物路径缺失、为空、不可解析,或未列入 deliverables/index.json,则召回验证器。

冲突解决:
若两个智能体存在分歧,编排器创建一条 ConflictRecord,记录各方立场、证据、受影响制品及决策。优先选取科学上更保守的方案,在 research_report.md 中记录局限性,并更新 logs.txt。

Shared communication contract for every non-orchestrator agent:
Each response must be a machine-checkable HandoffV2 object with keys: agent_id, phase, scope, contract_version, inputs_consumed, assumptions, interface_exports, artifact_plan_or_changes, validation_checks, metrics_or_estimates, evidence_bundle, risks, open_questions, requests_for_agents, ack_or_change_request, recall_recommendations. inputs_consumed entries must include source_agent_id, export_name, version, artifact_path_or_json_pointer, and consumed_status. interface_exports entries must include schema_id_or_signature, producer, consumer_agents, version, status draft|frozen|deprecated, required_fields, optional_fields, compatibility_notes, and validation_method. artifact_plan_or_changes entries must include path, exact_required_path boolean, status planned|created|modified|validated, purpose, producer, consumer_agents, size_or_sha256_if_available, and validation_status. validation_checks entries must include check_id, command_or_method, expected, actual, exit_code_if_command, status pass|fail|blocked, blocking_if_fail, and evidence_path. metrics_or_estimates entries must include name, value, unit, source_path, json_pointer_or_expression, and uncertainty_or_caveat. requests_for_agents entries must include target_agent_id, question, required_response_schema, urgency, blocking_if_unanswered, and requested_by_hop. ack_or_change_request must include reviewed_schema_ids, decision accept|change_request|blocked, field_level_comments, and required_resolution. recall_recommendations must include issue_id, owner_agent_id, narrowed_scope, acceptance_criteria_for_patch, and retest_method.
Mandatory agent-to-agent handoffs:
1. agent_interface_contract_steward publishes RequiredPathManifest v1 before any
implementation; every producing and validating agent must return ContractAck or
ChangeRequest.
2. agent_structural_data_rcsb and agent_torsion_geometry jointly produce DatasetRecord
v2 and DataProvenanceManifest v1 before model, training, or eval implementation consumes
data.

]
每个非编排者智能体的共享通信契约(contract):
每条响应必须是一个机器可检查的 HandoffV2 对象,包含以下键:agent_id、phase、scope、contract_version、inputs_consumed、assumptions、interface_exports、artifact_plan_or_changes、validation_checks、metrics_or_estimates、evidence_bundle、risks、open_questions、requests_for_agents、ack_or_change_request、recall_recommendations。inputs_consumed 条目必须包含:source_agent_id、export_name、version、artifact_path_or_json_pointer 和 consumed_status。interface_exports 条目必须包含:schema_id_or_signature、producer、consumer_agents、version、status(draft|frozen|deprecated)、required_fields、optional_fields、compatibility_notes 和 validation_method。artifact_plan_or_changes 条目必须包含:path、exact_required_path(布尔值)、status(planned|created|modified|validated)、purpose、producer、consumer_agents、size_or_sha256_if_available 和 validation_status。validation_checks 条目必须包含:check_id、command_or_method、expected、actual、exit_code_if_command、status(pass|fail|blocked)、blocking_if_fail 和 evidence_path。metrics_or_estimates 条目必须包含:name、value、unit、source_path、json_pointer_or_expression 和 uncertainty_or_caveat。requests_for_agents 条目必须包含:target_agent_id、question、required_response_schema、urgency、blocking_if_unanswered 和 requested_by_hop。ack_or_change_request 必须包含:reviewed_schema_ids、decision(accept|change_request|blocked)、field_level_comments 和 required_resolution。recall_recommendations 必须包含:issue_id、owner_agent_id、narrowed_scope、acceptance_criteria_for_patch 和 retest_method。
强制性智能体间交接(handoff)规则:
1. agent_interface_contract_steward 在任何实现开始之前发布 RequiredPathManifest v1;每个生产智能体和验证智能体必须返回 ContractAck 或 ChangeRequest。
2. agent_structural_data_rcsb 与 agent_torsion_geometry 在模型、训练或评估实现消费数据之前,联合生成 DatasetRecord v2 和 DataProvenanceManifest v1。

### English (extracted)

3. agent_structural_data_rcsb and agent_eval_benchmarks jointly produce
SplitLeakageReview v1 before training/evaluation claims are frozen.
4. agent_geometric_ml and agent_baseline_ablation jointly produce ModelRegistry v1,
ModelIOContract v2, and AblationGrid v2 before training begins; agent_training_reproducibility must ack importability, checkpoint keys, and model_ids.
5. agent_eval_benchmarks publishes ResultsContract v2 before scicomm writes quantitative
accuracy claims or index.json summaries.
6. agent_profile_systems publishes ProfilingContract v2 and validates exact
src/profile.py before scicomm writes speed or memory claims.
7. agent_scicomm_publication sends ClaimsEvidenceMatrix to
agent_claims_consistency_referee; all referee blockers are routed to the owning
specialist or scicomm for patch.
8. agent_artifact_validator and agent_quality_safety review frozen artifacts
independently and can open recall tickets; their final checks occur after any patch round, not only before patches.
Minimum orchestrator-subagent hops:
Hop A EvidenceRubric fan-out: orchestrator sends task deliverables, prior evidence defects, and acceptance criteria to all agents. Hop B DraftContract return: specialists return draft exports and requests_for_agents;
steward compiles InterfaceContract v2 and RequiredPathManifest v1.
Hop C Contract acknowledgement: orchestrator sends relevant contracts to consumers; each consumer returns ack_or_change_request. Blocking change requests trigger immediate
contract-reconciliation recall before implementation.
Hop D Implementation delegation: orchestrator delegates files and artifacts with frozen
contracts and exact acceptance checks.
Hop E Early smoke validation: validator checks exact paths, imports, RCSB provenance artifacts, and uv smoke commands before expensive training/profile/report work proceeds. Hop F Results and profiling freeze: training, eval, and profile agents publish frozen metrics, ablation, histories, profile JSON, and number_source_map; orchestrator routes
them to scicomm.
Hop G Report and index drafting: scicomm drafts report/index using only frozen contracts
and emits ClaimsEvidenceMatrix.
Hop H Independent critique: claims referee, validator, and quality agents audit report, index, data provenance, exact paths, and JSON consistency. Hop I Targeted PatchHandoff recall: for every blocker, orchestrator recalls only the owner with narrowed_scope, issue_ids_to_close, required changed files, and retest criteria. Owner returns PatchHandoff with issue_ids_closed, changed_files, diff_summary, validation_evidence, and remaining_risk. Hop J Retest and final gate: validator or quality retests the specific issue_ids; orchestrator repeats Hop I-J until no blockers remain or a waiver is recorded with
evidence and rationale.
Recall triggers:
Recall interface steward or validator if any exact required deliverable path is missing, empty, unparsable, not listed in deliverables/index.json, or substituted by a differently named file; src/profile.py missing is always blocking. Recall data if RCSB query payload, endpoint, selected IDs, download URLs, cache/download manifest, split manifest, or data_card evidence are missing or report-only. Recall geometry if χ1 atom tables, completeness rules, altloc policy, circular convention, or no-side-chain-input leakage evidence are missing. Recall model if random rotation/translation invariance tests are absent, max error/tolerance is not recorded, or equivariance/invariance language is incorrect. Recall ablation or training if results/ablation_results.json has fewer than one invariant baseline plus two equivariant variants without an approved deviation, or model_ids/checkpoint keys disagree across contracts. Recall eval if median angular error, MAE, RMSE, per-residue counts, circular metric definitions, split leakage checks, or JSON source pointers are missing or inconsistent. Recall profiling if src/profile.py is absent, memory numbers are implausibly tiny without method caveats and parameter/RSS/CUDA estimates, environment details are missing, or speed ratios used in the report differ from profiling JSON by more than 10 percent. Recall scicomm if report text contradicts JSON metrics, plots, profiling ratios, or captions, including stale statements such as 30-fold when JSON implies about 100-fold. Recall claims referee if quantitative claims are not inventoried. Recall quality if final approval lacks concrete
evidence paths.
Conflict resolution:
If two agents disagree, the orchestrator creates a ConflictRecord with positions, evidence paths or JSON pointers, affected artifacts, decision, and downstream contract updates. Prefer the scientifically conservative option, document limitations in research_report.md, and update logs.txt.
Final gate conditions:

### 中文（翻译）

3. agent_structural_data_rcsb 与 agent_eval_benchmarks 在训练/评估声明冻结之前,共同生成 SplitLeakageReview v1。
4. agent_geometric_ml 与 agent_baseline_ablation 在训练开始之前,共同生成 ModelRegistry v1、ModelIOContract v2 和 AblationGrid v2;agent_training_reproducibility 必须确认可导入性、检查点键(checkpoint keys)和 model_ids。
5. agent_eval_benchmarks 在 scicomm 撰写定量精度声明或 index.json 摘要之前,发布 ResultsContract v2。
6. agent_profile_systems 在 scicomm 撰写速度或内存声明之前,发布 ProfilingContract v2 并验证确切的 src/profile.py。
7. agent_scicomm_publication 将 ClaimsEvidenceMatrix 发送给 agent_claims_consistency_referee;所有裁判阻塞项被路由至负责该项的专项智能体(specialist)或 scicomm 进行修补。
8. agent_artifact_validator 与 agent_quality_safety 独立审查冻结的制品(artifact),可开具召回工单;其最终检查在任何补丁轮次之后进行,而不仅限于补丁之前。

编排器-子智能体最少跳数(hop):
- **跳 A** EvidenceRubric 扇出:编排器将任务交付物、先前证据缺陷和验收标准发送给所有智能体。
- **跳 B** DraftContract 返回:专项智能体返回草稿导出物和 requests_for_agents;管家(steward)编译 InterfaceContract v2 和 RequiredPathManifest v1。
- **跳 C** 合约确认:编排器将相关合约(contract)发送给消费方;每个消费方返回 ack_or_change_request。阻塞性变更请求在实现之前立即触发合约协调召回。
- **跳 D** 实现委派:编排器委派具有冻结合约和精确验收检查的文件与制品。
- **跳 E** 早期冒烟验证:验证器在昂贵的训练/分析/报告工作开始之前,检查精确路径、导入项、RCSB 溯源制品和 uv 冒烟命令。
- **跳 F** 结果与分析冻结:训练、评估和分析智能体发布冻结的指标、消融结果、历史记录、profile JSON 和 number_source_map;编排器将其路由至 scicomm。
- **跳 G** 报告与索引起草:scicomm 仅使用冻结合约起草报告/索引,并生成 ClaimsEvidenceMatrix。
- **跳 H** 独立审查:声明裁判、验证器和质量智能体对报告、索引、数据溯源、精确路径和 JSON 一致性进行审计。
- **跳 I** 定向 PatchHandoff 召回:对于每个阻塞项,编排器仅召回其所有者,并附上缩小范围(narrowed_scope)、issue_ids_to_close、required_changed_files 和重测标准。所有者返回 PatchHandoff,包含 issue_ids_closed、changed_files、diff_summary、validation_evidence 和 remaining_risk。
- **跳 J** 重测与最终关卡:验证器或质量智能体重测特定 issue_ids;编排器重复跳 I–J,直至无阻塞项残留,或记录附有证据与理由的豁免。

召回触发条件:
若任何精确要求的交付物路径缺失、为空、不可解析、未列于 deliverables/index.json,或被不同名称的文件替代,则召回接口管家或验证器;src/profile.py 缺失始终为阻塞项。若 RCSB 查询载荷、端点、选定 ID、下载 URL、缓存/下载清单、分割清单或 data_card 证据缺失或仅存在于报告中,则召回数据智能体。若 χ1 原子表、完整性规则、替代位点(altloc)策略、循环惯例或无侧链输入泄漏证据缺失,则召回几何智能体。若随机旋转/平移不变性测试缺失、最大误差/容差未记录,或等变/不变性表述不正确,则召回模型智能体。若 results/ablation_results.json 包含的条目少于一个不变基线加两个等变变体(且无已批准的偏差),或 model_ids/检查点键在各合约间不一致,则召回消融或训练智能体。若中位角误差、MAE、RMSE、逐残基计数、循环指标定义、分割泄漏检查或 JSON 来源指针缺失或不一致,则召回评估智能体。若 src/profile.py 缺失、内存数字异常偏小且无方法说明及参数/RSS/CUDA 估计、环境细节缺失,或报告中使用的速度比与 profiling JSON 相差超过 10%,则召回分析智能体。若报告文本与 JSON 指标、图表、分析比率或图注相矛盾(包括过时表述,例如 JSON 暗示约 100 倍时仍写 30 倍),则召回 scicomm。若定量声明未被清单列举,则召回声明裁判。若最终批准缺乏具体证据路径,则召回质量智能体。

冲突解决:
若两个智能体意见相左,编排器创建 ConflictRecord,记录各方立场、证据路径或 JSON 指针、受影响制品、决策及下游合约更新。优先采用科学上更为保守的选项,在 research_report.md 中记录局限性,并更新 logs.txt。

最终关卡条件:

Shared communication contract for every non-orchestrator agent:
Each response must be a machine-checkable HandoffV3 object with keys: handoff_id, agent_id, hop_id, phase, scope, contract_version, inputs_consumed, assumptions, interface_exports, artifact_plan_or_changes, validation_checks, metrics_or_estimates, evidence_bundle, risks, open_questions, requests_for_agents, ack_or_change_request, issue_tickets, recall_recommendations, and blocking_status. inputs_consumed entries must include source_agent_id, export_name, version, artifact_path_or_json_pointer, consumed_status, and freshness_requirement. interface_exports entries must include schema_id_or_signature, producer, consumer_agents, version, status draft|frozen|deprecated, required_fields, optional_fields, compatibility_notes, expected_json_pointers, validation_method, and consumer_ack_required. artifact_plan_or_changes entries must include path, exact_required_path boolean, supporting_acceptance_path boolean, status planned|created|modified|validated, purpose, producer, consumer_agents, size_bytes, sha256_if_available, and validation_status. validation_checks entries must include check_id, command_or_method, expected, actual, exit_code_if_command, status pass|fail|blocked, blocking_if_fail, issue_id_if_fail, and evidence_path. metrics_or_estimates entries must include name, value, unit, source_path, json_pointer_or_expression, uncertainty_or_caveat, and destination_json_pointer_if_merged. evidence_bundle entries must include artifact_path, json_pointer, file_excerpt_or_hash, producing_command, and generated_at. requests_for_agents entries must include target_agent_id, question, required_response_schema, urgency, blocking_if_unanswered, requested_by_hop, and due_before_hop. ack_or_change_request must include reviewed_schema_ids, field_level_comments, decision accept|change_request|blocked, required_resolution, and reack_required. issue_tickets must include issue_id, severity, owner_agent_id, evidence_path_or_json_pointer, requested_fix, acceptance_criteria_for_patch, and retest_method. recall_recommendations must include issue_id, owner_agent_id, narrowed_scope, changed_files_expected, acceptance_criteria_for_patch, and retest_method. blocking_status must be pass|blocked|conditional with blocker_issue_ids.
Patch protocol:
Any recalled owner must return PatchHandoffV3 with issue_ids_to_close, changed_files, diff_summary, contract_fields_changed, backward_compatibility_notes, validation_evidence, issue_ids_closed, issue_ids_still_open, regression_risks, and requested_retests. The orchestrator must route PatchHandoffV3 to the original opener
plus validator or quality for retest before closing the issue.
Mandatory agent-to-agent handoffs:
1. agent_interface_contract_steward publishes RequiredPathManifest v2 and
InterfaceContract v3 before implementation; every producing and validating agent must
return ContractAckV3 or ChangeRequestV3 with field-level comments.
2. agent_structural_data_rcsb and agent_torsion_geometry jointly produce DatasetRecord
v3 and DataProvenanceManifest v2 before model, training, eval, profile, or equivariance
code consumes data.
3. agent_structural_data_rcsb and agent_eval_benchmarks jointly produce
SplitLeakageReview v2 before training and evaluation claims are frozen.
4. agent_geometric_ml and agent_baseline_ablation jointly produce ModelRegistry v2,
ModelIOContract v3, and AblationGrid v3 before training begins; agent_training_reproducibility, agent_eval_benchmarks, agent_profile_systems, agent_equivariance_referee, and agent_metrics_artifact_integrator must ack importability, checkpoint keys, model_ids, and factory signatures.
5. agent_equivariance_referee publishes EquivarianceAuditContract v1 and
results/equivariance_tests.json before scicomm writes equivariance claims or final acceptance; failures trigger recall to model or training.

每个非编排者智能体共用的通信契约(contract):
每条响应必须是可机器校验的 HandoffV3 对象,包含以下键:handoff_id、agent_id、hop_id(跳转编号)、phase、scope、contract_version、inputs_consumed、assumptions、interface_exports、artifact_plan_or_changes、validation_checks、metrics_or_estimates、evidence_bundle、risks、open_questions、requests_for_agents、ack_or_change_request、issue_tickets、recall_recommendations 及 blocking_status。inputs_consumed 条目必须包含 source_agent_id、export_name、version、artifact_path_or_json_pointer、consumed_status 及 freshness_requirement。interface_exports 条目必须包含 schema_id_or_signature、producer、consumer_agents、version、状态 draft|frozen|deprecated、required_fields、optional_fields、compatibility_notes、expected_json_pointers、validation_method 及 consumer_ack_required。artifact_plan_or_changes 条目必须包含 path、exact_required_path(布尔值)、supporting_acceptance_path(布尔值)、状态 planned|created|modified|validated、purpose、producer、consumer_agents、size_bytes、sha256_if_available 及 validation_status。validation_checks 条目必须包含 check_id、command_or_method、expected、actual、exit_code_if_command、状态 pass|fail|blocked、blocking_if_fail、issue_id_if_fail 及 evidence_path。metrics_or_estimates 条目必须包含 name、value、unit、source_path、json_pointer_or_expression、uncertainty_or_caveat 及 destination_json_pointer_if_merged。evidence_bundle 条目必须包含 artifact_path、json_pointer、file_excerpt_or_hash、producing_command 及 generated_at。requests_for_agents 条目必须包含 target_agent_id、question、required_response_schema、urgency、blocking_if_unanswered、requested_by_hop 及 due_before_hop。ack_or_change_request 必须包含 reviewed_schema_ids、field_level_comments、决策 accept|change_request|blocked、required_resolution 及 reack_required。issue_tickets 必须包含 issue_id、severity、owner_agent_id、evidence_path_or_json_pointer、requested_fix、acceptance_criteria_for_patch 及 retest_method。recall_recommendations 必须包含 issue_id、owner_agent_id、narrowed_scope、changed_files_expected、acceptance_criteria_for_patch 及 retest_method。blocking_status 必须为 pass|blocked|conditional,并附 blocker_issue_ids。

补丁协议:
任何被召回的所有者智能体必须返回 PatchHandoffV3,包含 issue_ids_to_close、changed_files、diff_summary、contract_fields_changed、backward_compatibility_notes、validation_evidence、issue_ids_closed、issue_ids_still_open、regression_risks 及 requested_retests。编排者必须将 PatchHandoffV3 路由至原始开启者及验证者或质量审查智能体,完成复测后方可关闭工单。

强制智能体间交接:
1. agent_interface_contract_steward 在实现前发布 RequiredPathManifest v2 与 InterfaceContract v3;每个生产智能体和验证智能体必须返回 ContractAckV3 或附带字段级注释的 ChangeRequestV3。
2. agent_structural_data_rcsb 与 agent_torsion_geometry 在模型、训练、评估、性能分析或等变性代码使用数据之前,联合生成 DatasetRecord v3 与 DataProvenanceManifest v2。
3. agent_structural_data_rcsb 与 agent_eval_benchmarks 在训练和评估声明冻结前,联合生成 SplitLeakageReview v2。
4. agent_geometric_ml 与 agent_baseline_ablation 在训练开始前联合生成 ModelRegistry v2、ModelIOContract v3 及 AblationGrid v3;agent_training_reproducibility、agent_eval_benchmarks、agent_profile_systems、agent_equivariance_referee 及 agent_metrics_artifact_integrator 必须确认可导入性、检查点键、model_id 及工厂签名。
5. agent_equivariance_referee 在科学传播智能体撰写等变性声明或最终验收前,发布 EquivarianceAuditContract v1 与 results/equivariance_tests.json;若测试失败,则触发对模型或训练智能体的召回。

6. agent_eval_benchmarks publishes ResultsContract v3 before metrics integration and

9. agent_metrics_artifact_integrator freezes MetricsIntegrationContract only after eval,

explicit waivers.

11. agent_artifact_validator, agent_quality_safety, agent_profile_sanity_referee, and

Minimum orchestrator-subagent hops:
Hop 0 EvidenceRubric fan-out: orchestrator sends task deliverables, prior comparison defects, v2 file evidence, and acceptance criteria to all agents. Hop 1 DraftContract return: specialists return draft exports and requests_for_agents;
steward compiles RequiredPathManifest v2 and InterfaceContract v3.
Hop 2 Contract acknowledgement: orchestrator sends relevant contracts to consumers; every consumer returns field-level ack_or_change_request. Blocking changes trigger
contract-reconciliation recall before implementation.
Hop 3 Contract reconciliation loop: orchestrator recalls only conflicted producers and consumers with the disputed fields, requested resolution, and retest method; repeat
until frozen or waived.
Hop 4 Data-geometry implementation and co-sign: data and geometry implement extraction, manifests, residue chemistry, and leakage checks; eval acks split/leakage review. Hop 5 Model-ablation implementation and import smoke: model and ablation implement factories and variants; training, eval, profile, and equivariance agents ack actual
import and shape evidence.
Hop 6 Early artifact smoke validation: validator checks exact paths, imports, stale main.py, RCSB provenance, DatasetRecord keys, and uv smoke commands before expensive
training/profile/report work proceeds.
Hop 7 Equivariance pre-report gate: independent equivariance referee runs random rotation and translation tests; failures recall model before scicomm and before final
gate.
Hop 8 Training smoke and full run: training runs smoke then full or bounded training for all model_ids; failures recall data/model/ablation with narrow scope. Hop 9 Evaluation and split-leakage freeze: eval computes circular metrics, per-residue breakdowns, ablation results, and number_source_map; data/eval co-sign no-overlap
evidence.
Hop 10 Profiling and sanity audit: profile publishes runtime, training-step latency, memory, and ratios; profile sanity referee recomputes ratios and audits memory
anomalies.
Hop 11 Metrics integration freeze: metrics integrator merges eval, profile, training, data, ablation, and equivariance into results/metrics.json with JSON pointers; validator
checks that runtime and profiling fields exist.
Hop 12 Plot and index generation: responsible agents generate all plots and index from frozen JSON; validator checks non-empty plots and index path coverage. Hop 13 Report drafting: scicomm drafts report using only frozen contracts and emits
ClaimsEvidenceMatrix.
Hop 14 Independent critique: claims referee, validator, quality, profile sanity, and log auditor audit report, index, data provenance, exact paths, logs, JSON consistency, and
profiling/equivariance language.
Hop 15 Targeted PatchHandoff recall: for every blocker, orchestrator recalls only the owner with narrowed_scope, issue_ids_to_close, required changed files, and retest
criteria. Owner returns PatchHandoffV3.
Hop 16 Retest and final gate: original opener plus validator or quality retests specific issue_ids; orchestrator repeats Hop 15 and Hop 16 until no blockers remain or a waiver
is recorded with evidence and rationale.

6. agent_eval_benchmarks 在指标集成和科学传播准确性声明之前发布 ResultsContract v3。
7. agent_profile_systems 在指标集成和速度或内存声明之前发布 ProfilingContract v3,并验证 `src/profile.py` 的确切内容。
8. agent_profile_sanity_referee 在科学传播的剖析(profiling)文本产出之前审计 ProfilingContract;阻塞问题路由至 profile 或 metrics 集成器。
9. agent_metrics_artifact_integrator 仅在消费了来自评估、剖析、消融、训练、数据和等变性(equivariance)的所有输入,且 `results/metrics.json` 包含运行时、剖析、内存和等变性摘要或明确豁免声明之后,才冻结 MetricsIntegrationContract。
10. agent_scicomm_publication 将 ClaimsEvidenceMatrix 发送至 agent_claims_consistency_referee;所有裁判阻塞问题路由至对应的专项智能体或科学传播智能体进行修补。
11. agent_artifact_validator、agent_quality_safety、agent_profile_sanity_referee 和 agent_reproducibility_log_auditor 独立审查冻结后的制品,可开具召回(recall)工单;其最终检查发生在任何修补轮次之后,而不仅限于修补之前。

最少编排器-子智能体跳转(hop):

**跳转 0** EvidenceRubric 扇出:编排器将任务可交付成果、先前比较缺陷、v2 文件证据和验收标准发送至所有智能体。

**跳转 1** DraftContract 返回:专项智能体返回草稿导出和 requests_for_agents;负责人编译 RequiredPathManifest v2 和 InterfaceContract v3。

**跳转 2** 合约(contract)确认:编排器将相关合约发送至消费方;每个消费方返回字段级 ack_or_change_request。阻塞性变更在实现之前触发合约协调召回。

**跳转 3** 合约协调循环:编排器仅召回存在争议的生产方和消费方,并附带争议字段、请求的解决方案和复测方法;重复直至冻结或豁免。

**跳转 4** 数据-几何实现与联署:数据和几何智能体实现提取、清单、残基化学信息和泄漏检查;评估智能体确认(ack)拆分/泄漏审查。

**跳转 5** 模型-消融实现与导入冒烟测试:模型和消融智能体实现工厂类和变体;训练、评估、剖析和等变性智能体确认实际导入和形状证据。

**跳转 6** 早期制品冒烟验证:验证器在昂贵的训练/剖析/报告工作开始之前,检查确切路径、导入、过时的 `main.py`、RCSB 来源、DatasetRecord 键和 `uv` 冒烟命令。

**跳转 7** 等变性预报告关卡:独立等变性裁判运行随机旋转和平移测试;失败将在科学传播和最终关卡之前召回模型。

**跳转 8** 训练冒烟与完整运行:训练智能体对所有 model_id 运行冒烟测试,然后进行完整或有界训练;失败以窄范围召回数据/模型/消融智能体。

**跳转 9** 评估与拆分泄漏冻结:评估智能体计算循环指标、逐残基分解、消融结果和 number_source_map;数据/评估智能体联署无重叠证据。

**跳转 10** 剖析与合理性审计:剖析智能体发布运行时、训练步延迟、内存和比率;剖析合理性裁判重新计算比率并审计内存异常。

**跳转 11** 指标集成冻结:指标集成器将评估、剖析、训练、数据、消融和等变性结果合并至 `results/metrics.json`(含 JSON 指针);验证器检查运行时和剖析字段是否存在。

**跳转 12** 绘图与索引生成:负责智能体从冻结的 JSON 生成所有图表和索引;验证器检查图表非空及索引路径覆盖情况。

**跳转 13** 报告起草:科学传播智能体仅使用冻结合约起草报告,并发出 ClaimsEvidenceMatrix。

**跳转 14** 独立审阅:声明裁判、验证器、质量、剖析合理性裁判和日志审计员审计报告、索引、数据来源、确切路径、日志、JSON 一致性及剖析/等变性表述。

**跳转 15** 定向 PatchHandoff 召回:对每个阻塞问题,编排器仅召回所有者,并附带 narrowed_scope、issue_ids_to_close、需变更文件和复测标准;所有者返回 PatchHandoffV3。

**跳转 16** 复测与最终关卡:原始提单者加验证器或质量智能体对特定 issue_id 进行复测;编排器重复跳转 15 和跳转 16,直至无阻塞问题或记录了附有证据和理由的豁免。

## 第 55 页

### English (extracted)

Recall interface steward or validator if any exact required deliverable path is missing, empty, unparsable, not listed in deliverables/index.json, or substituted by a differently named file; src/profile.py missing is always blocking. Recall data if RCSB query payload, endpoint, selected IDs, download URLs, cache/download manifest, split manifest, data_card evidence, or deterministic extraction command are missing or report-only. Recall geometry if χ1 atom tables, completeness rules, altloc policy, circular convention, or no-side-chain-input leakage evidence are missing. Recall model if random rotation and translation test hooks are absent, ModelRegistry imports fail, max error and tolerance are not recorded, or equivariance/invariance language is incorrect. Recall equivariance referee if results/equivariance_tests.json is absent, not run on implemented code, or lacks pass/fail status and tolerances. Recall ablation or training if results/ablation_results.json has fewer than one invariant baseline plus two equivariant variants without approved deviation, model_ids or checkpoint keys disagree, training histories are absent, or main.py remains a stale hello-world. Recall eval if median angular error, MAE, RMSE, per-residue counts, circular metric definitions, split leakage checks, or JSON source pointers are missing or inconsistent. Recall profiling if src/profile.py is absent, training-step profiling is absent without waiver, memory numbers are implausibly tiny without method caveats and parameter/RSS/CUDA estimates, any negative value is reported as memory usage, environment details are missing, or speed ratios used in report differ from profiling JSON by more than 10 percent. Recall profile sanity if ratios or memory anomalies are not independently inventoried. Recall metrics integrator if results/metrics.json lacks runtime, profiling, memory, throughput-ratio, source-artifact, or equivariance summary fields. Recall scicomm if report text contradicts JSON metrics, plots, profiling ratios, equivariance evidence, or captions, including stale statements that formal equivariance tests were not included when results/equivariance_tests.json exists. Recall claims referee if quantitative claims are not inventoried. Recall log auditor or orchestrator if logs.txt lacks roster, call graph, handoffs, recall history, uv commands, or final_gate_decision. Recall
quality if final approval lacks concrete evidence paths.
Conflict resolution:
If two agents disagree, the orchestrator creates a ConflictRecord with positions, evidence paths or JSON pointers, affected artifacts, downstream contract fields, decision, and updated consumers. Prefer the scientifically conservative option, document limitations in research_report.md, and update logs.txt. Contract changes after
implementation require reack from all affected consumers and targeted retest.
Final gate conditions:
Pass only if all required files exist at exact paths including src/profile.py; deliverables/index.json lists exact required artifacts and supporting provenance artifacts; data/rcsb_query.json, data/rcsb_response_ids.json, and data/download_manifest.json make PDB acquisition reproducible; DatasetRecord excludes side-chain coordinates from inputs; formal equivariance checks are present in results/equivariance_tests.json and cited or explicitly waived; results/metrics.json contains MAE, median, RMSE, per-residue counts, runtime, profiling and memory summary, throughput ratios, source-artifact pointers, and equivariance summary; results/ablation_results.json contains required invariant and equivariant variants; profiling includes forward and training-step latency or waiver, robust memory caveats, parameter/RSS/CUDA estimates where applicable, and model-to-model throughput ratios; research_report.md quantitative claims match JSON within stated tolerances and does not contain stale unsupported limitations; all five required plots are non-empty and generated from frozen JSON; main.py is not a stale hello-world if present; uv run smoke commands are logged; logs.txt contains agent roster, call graph, handoffs, recall history, commands, unresolved risks, and final_gate_decision.

### 中文（翻译）

若任何精确要求的可交付成果路径缺失、为空、不可解析、未列于 `deliverables/index.json`,或被不同名称的文件替代,则召回接口负责人或验证器;`src/profile.py` 缺失始终构成阻塞。若 RCSB 查询载荷、端点、选定 ID、下载 URL、缓存/下载清单、拆分清单、data_card 证据或确定性提取命令缺失或仅存在于报告中,则召回数据智能体。若 χ1 原子表、完整性规则、替代位置(altloc)策略、循环约定或无侧链输入泄漏证据缺失,则召回几何智能体。若随机旋转和平移测试钩子缺失、ModelRegistry 导入失败、最大误差和容差未被记录,或等变性/不变性表述不正确,则召回模型智能体。若 `results/equivariance_tests.json` 缺失、未在已实现代码上运行,或缺少通过/失败状态和容差,则召回等变性裁判。若 `results/ablation_results.json` 中少于一个不变基线加两个等变变体(无经批准的偏差)、model_id 或检查点键不一致、训练历史缺失,或 `main.py` 仍为过时的 hello-world,则召回消融或训练智能体。若中位角误差、MAE、RMSE、逐残基计数、循环指标定义、拆分泄漏检查或 JSON 来源指针缺失或不一致,则召回评估智能体。若 `src/profile.py` 缺失、训练步剖析缺失(无豁免)、内存数值不合理地微小(无方法说明及参数/RSS/CUDA 估算)、任何负值被报告为内存使用量、环境细节缺失,或报告中使用的速度比率与剖析 JSON 的偏差超过 10%,则召回剖析智能体。若比率或内存异常未被独立盘点,则召回剖析合理性裁判。若 `results/metrics.json` 缺少运行时、剖析、内存、吞吐量比率、来源制品或等变性摘要字段,则召回指标集成器。若报告文本与 JSON 指标、图表、剖析比率、等变性证据或标题相矛盾,包括在 `results/equivariance_tests.json` 已存在时仍声称未包含正式等变性测试,则召回科学传播智能体。若定量声明未被盘点,则召回声明裁判。若 `logs.txt` 缺少智能体名册、调用图、交接记录、召回历史、`uv` 命令或 final_gate_decision,则召回日志审计员或编排器。若最终批准缺少具体证据路径,则召回质量智能体。

冲突解决:

若两个智能体意见不一致,编排器创建一条 ConflictRecord,记录各方立场、证据路径或 JSON 指针、受影响制品、下游合约字段、决策及已更新的消费方。优先选择科学上保守的选项,在 `research_report.md` 中记录局限性,并更新 `logs.txt`。实现之后的合约变更需要所有受影响消费方重新确认,并进行定向复测。

最终关卡条件:

仅在以下所有条件满足时通过:所有必需文件均存在于精确路径(包括 `src/profile.py`);`deliverables/index.json` 列出所有精确要求的制品及支撑溯源制品;`data/rcsb_query.json`、`data/rcsb_response_ids.json` 和 `data/download_manifest.json` 使 PDB 获取过程可复现;DatasetRecord 将侧链坐标排除在输入之外;正式等变性检查存在于 `results/equivariance_tests.json` 中并被引用,或已明确豁免;`results/metrics.json` 包含 MAE、中位数、RMSE、逐残基计数、运行时、剖析和内存摘要、吞吐量比率、来源制品指针及等变性摘要;`results/ablation_results.json` 包含必需的不变和等变变体;剖析包含前向和训练步延迟或豁免、可靠的内存说明、适用时的参数/RSS/CUDA 估算,以及模型间吞吐量比率;`research_report.md` 的定量声明在规定容差内与 JSON 一致,且不含过时的不支持的局限性表述;全部五个必需图表均非空且从冻结 JSON 生成;若 `main.py` 存在,则不得为过时的 hello-world;`uv run` 冒烟命令已被记录;`logs.txt` 包含智能体名册、调用图、交接记录、召回历史、命令、未解决风险及 final_gate_decision。

**工具集 H[4]**

### English (extracted)

Each response must be a machine-checkable HandoffV4 object with keys: handoff_id, agent_id, hop_id, phase, scope, contract_version, inputs_consumed, assumptions, interface_exports, artifact_plan_or_changes, validation_checks, metrics_or_estimates, evidence_bundle, downstream_impact, self_audit_against_prior_defects, risks, open_questions, requests_for_agents, ack_or_change_request, issue_tickets, recall_recommendations, and blocking_status. inputs_consumed entries must include source_agent_id, export_name, version, artifact_path_or_json_pointer, consumed_status,
and freshness_requirement. interface_exports entries must include
schema_id_or_signature, producer, consumer_agents, version, status draft|frozen|deprecated, required_fields, optional_fields, compatibility_notes, expected_json_pointers, validation_method, consumer_ack_required, and breaking_change_if_modified. artifact_plan_or_changes entries must include path, exact_required_path boolean, supporting_acceptance_path boolean, status planned|created|modified|validated, purpose, producer, consumer_agents, size_bytes, sha256_if_available, validation_status, and source_command. validation_checks entries must include check_id, command_or_method, expected, actual, exit_code_if_command, status pass|fail|blocked, blocking_if_fail, issue_id_if_fail, and evidence_path. metrics_or_estimates entries must include name, value, unit, source_path, json_pointer_or_expression, uncertainty_or_caveat, and destination_json_pointer_if_merged. evidence_bundle entries must include artifact_path, json_pointer, file_excerpt_or_hash, producing_command, exit_code_if_command, generated_at, and freshness_hop. downstream_impact entries must include consumer_agent_id, consumed_field, downstream_artifact, and reack_required_if_changed. self_audit_against_prior_defects must include rows for src/profile.py path, RCSB fallback/provenance, runtime/profiling in metrics, formal equivariance evidence, stale main.py, best_epoch consistency, report training-time consistency, profiling anomalies, and logs completeness. requests_for_agents entries must include target_agent_id, question, required_response_schema, urgency, blocking_if_unanswered, requested_by_hop, and due_before_hop. ack_or_change_request must include reviewed_schema_ids, field_level_comments, decision accept|change_request|blocked, required_resolution, and reack_required. issue_tickets must include issue_id, severity critical|major|minor, owner_agent_id, evidence_path_or_json_pointer, file_excerpt_or_metric, requested_fix, acceptance_criteria_for_patch, retest_method, and history_trigger_if_any. recall_recommendations must include issue_id, owner_agent_id, narrowed_scope, changed_files_expected, acceptance_criteria_for_patch, and retest_method. blocking_status must be pass|blocked|conditional with blocker_issue_ids.
Contract acknowledgement protocol:
The orchestrator may not freeze any shared contract until every producer, consumer, and validator returns ContractAckV4 or ChangeRequestV4. ContractAckV4 requires schema_id, version, agent_id, field_name, consumer_use, accepted_type_or_constraint, validation_method, ack_status accept|change_request|blocked, requested_change, blocking_reason, and reack_required. Contract changes after implementation require reack
from all affected consumers and targeted retest.
Patch protocol:
Any recalled owner must return PatchHandoffV4 with issue_ids_to_close, changed_files, diff_summary, contract_fields_changed, backward_compatibility_notes, validation_evidence, metrics_or_pointer_changes, issue_ids_closed, issue_ids_still_open, regression_risks, and requested_retests. The orchestrator must route PatchHandoffV4 to the original opener plus validator or quality for retest before closing an issue. If a patch changes metrics, profile, data, or report claims, the metrics integrator, JSON consistency referee, claims referee, and log auditor must re-run their affected checks.
Mandatory agent-to-agent handoffs:
1. Interface steward publishes RequiredPathManifest v4, InterfaceContract v4, and
MetricsPointerMatrix v2 before implementation; every producing and validating agent
returns field-level ContractAckV4 or ChangeRequestV4.
2. Data agent publishes DataProvenanceManifest v4, RCSB query attempts, and filter
verification; RCSB protocol referee must pass or conditionally pass before training and
before any report data claim.
3. Data and geometry jointly produce DatasetRecord v4 and no-sidechain-input leakage
evidence before model, training, eval, profile, or equivariance code consumes data.
4. Data and eval jointly produce SplitLeakageReview v3 before training and evaluation
claims are frozen.
5. Model and ablation jointly produce ModelRegistry v4, ModelIOContract v4, and
AblationGrid v4; training, eval, profile, equivariance, and metrics agents must ack importability, factory signatures, model_ids, and checkpoint keys.
6. Training produces TrainingHistory and CheckpointMetadata; eval and metrics must ack
epoch-selection fields before metrics freeze.
7. Entrypoint CI audits main.py before final; stale hello-world is blocking.

### 中文（翻译）

每条响应必须是一个可机器校验的 HandoffV4 对象,包含以下键:handoff_id、agent_id、hop_id、phase、scope、contract_version、inputs_consumed、assumptions、interface_exports、artifact_plan_or_changes、validation_checks、metrics_or_estimates、evidence_bundle、downstream_impact、self_audit_against_prior_defects、risks、open_questions、requests_for_agents、ack_or_change_request、issue_tickets、recall_recommendations 以及 blocking_status。inputs_consumed 条目必须包含:source_agent_id、export_name、version、artifact_path_or_json_pointer、consumed_status 以及 freshness_requirement。interface_exports 条目必须包含:schema_id_or_signature、producer、consumer_agents、version、状态 draft|frozen|deprecated、required_fields、optional_fields、compatibility_notes、expected_json_pointers、validation_method、consumer_ack_required 以及 breaking_change_if_modified。artifact_plan_or_changes 条目必须包含:path、exact_required_path(布尔值)、supporting_acceptance_path(布尔值)、状态 planned|created|modified|validated、purpose、producer、consumer_agents、size_bytes、sha256_if_available、validation_status 以及 source_command。validation_checks 条目必须包含:check_id、command_or_method、expected、actual、exit_code_if_command、状态 pass|fail|blocked、blocking_if_fail、issue_id_if_fail 以及 evidence_path。metrics_or_estimates 条目必须包含:name、value、unit、source_path、json_pointer_or_expression、uncertainty_or_caveat 以及 destination_json_pointer_if_merged。evidence_bundle 条目必须包含:artifact_path、json_pointer、file_excerpt_or_hash、producing_command、exit_code_if_command、generated_at 以及 freshness_hop。downstream_impact 条目必须包含:consumer_agent_id、consumed_field、downstream_artifact 以及 reack_required_if_changed。self_audit_against_prior_defects 必须包含以下各行:src/profile.py 路径、RCSB 回退/溯源、运行时/性能分析指标、等变性正式证据、过时的 main.py、best_epoch 一致性、报告训练时一致性、性能分析异常以及日志完整性。requests_for_agents 条目必须包含:target_agent_id、question、required_response_schema、urgency、blocking_if_unanswered、requested_by_hop 以及 due_before_hop。ack_or_change_request 必须包含:reviewed_schema_ids、field_level_comments、决定 accept|change_request|blocked、required_resolution 以及 reack_required。issue_tickets 必须包含:issue_id、严重程度 critical|major|minor、owner_agent_id、evidence_path_or_json_pointer、file_excerpt_or_metric、requested_fix、acceptance_criteria_for_patch、retest_method 以及 history_trigger_if_any。recall_recommendations 必须包含:issue_id、owner_agent_id、narrowed_scope、changed_files_expected、acceptance_criteria_for_patch 以及 retest_method。blocking_status 必须为 pass|blocked|conditional,并附带 blocker_issue_ids。

契约(contract)确认协议:
在所有生产者、消费者和验证者均返回 ContractAckV4 或 ChangeRequestV4 之前,编排器(orchestrator)不得冻结任何共享契约。ContractAckV4 需包含:schema_id、version、agent_id、field_name、consumer_use、accepted_type_or_constraint、validation_method、确认状态 accept|change_request|blocked、requested_change、blocking_reason 以及 reack_required。契约在实现后若发生变更,须向所有受影响的消费者重新确认,并进行针对性复测。

补丁(patch)协议:
任何被召回的所有者必须返回 PatchHandoffV4,包含:issue_ids_to_close、changed_files、diff_summary、contract_fields_changed、backward_compatibility_notes、validation_evidence、metrics_or_pointer_changes、issue_ids_closed、issue_ids_still_open、regression_risks 以及 requested_retests。编排器必须将 PatchHandoffV4 路由至原始开启者以及验证者或质量方,复测通过后方可关闭问题。若某补丁改变了指标、性能分析、数据或报告中的声明,指标集成器、JSON 一致性裁决器、声明裁决器和日志审计器必须重新执行各自受影响的检查。

强制智能体间交接(handoff):
1. 接口管理员在实现前发布 RequiredPathManifest v4、InterfaceContract v4 以及 MetricsPointerMatrix v2;每个生产者和验证智能体须返回字段级 ContractAckV4 或 ChangeRequestV4。
2. 数据智能体发布 DataProvenanceManifest v4、RCSB 查询尝试记录以及过滤器验证结果;RCSB 协议裁决器须在训练开始前及任何报告数据声明冻结前通过或有条件通过。
3. 数据智能体与几何智能体联合产出 DatasetRecord v4 以及无侧链输入泄漏证据,模型、训练、评估、性能分析或等变性代码方可消费数据。
4. 数据智能体与评估智能体联合产出 SplitLeakageReview v3,方可冻结训练和评估声明。
5. 模型智能体与消融(ablation)智能体联合产出 ModelRegistry v4、ModelIOContract v4 以及 AblationGrid v4;训练、评估、性能分析、等变性和指标智能体必须确认可导入性、工厂签名、model_ids 以及检查点键。
6. 训练智能体产出 TrainingHistory 和 CheckpointMetadata;评估和指标智能体必须在指标冻结前确认 epoch 选择字段。
7. 入口点 CI 在最终版本前审计 main.py;过时的 hello-world 内容属于阻塞性问题。

### English (extracted)

8. Equivariance referee publishes EquivarianceAuditContract v2 and
results/equivariance_tests.json with command evidence before scicomm writes equivariance
claims.
9. Eval publishes ResultsContract v4 and metrics_lineage before metrics integration and
report accuracy claims.
10. Profile publishes ProfilingContract v4 and validates exact src/profile.py before
metrics integration and speed or memory claims.
11. Profile sanity referee audits ProfilingContract and metrics runtime-memory merge
before scicomm profiling text.
12. Metrics integrator freezes MetricsIntegrationContract only after eval, training,
profile, ablation, data, RCSB, and equivariance inputs are consumed and
results/metrics.json contains all required pointers.
13. JSON consistency referee audits frozen metrics before plots, index summaries, and
report are considered final.
14. Scicomm sends ClaimsEvidenceMatrix to claims referee; all claims blockers route to
owning specialist or scicomm for patch.
15. Validator, quality, RCSB referee, profile sanity, JSON consistency referee, claims
referee, entrypoint CI, and log auditor review after every patch round, not only before
patches.
Minimum orchestrator-subagent hops:
Hop 0 EvidenceRubric fan-out: send task deliverables, pairwise history, and current v3
file evidence to all agents.
Hop 1 Parallel defect pre-mortem: specialists return self_audit_against_prior_defects
and requests_for_agents.
Hop 2 Draft contracts: steward compiles RequiredPathManifest, InterfaceContract, MetricsPointerMatrix, and schema registry.
Hop 3 Contract acknowledgement: relevant consumers return ContractAckV4 or
ChangeRequestV4 with field-level comments.
Hop 4 Contract reconciliation loop: orchestrator recalls only disputed producers and consumers with disputed fields and retest method until frozen or explicitly waived. Hop 5 RCSB implementation: data agent implements query/download/cache/metadata/split and
returns DataRCSBHandoffV4.
Hop 6 RCSB protocol referee gate: independent referee audits query status, fallback, and filter verification; failures recall data or scicomm. Hop 7 Data-geometry co-sign: data and geometry implement extraction, residue chemistry, no-leakage checks, and split review; eval acks split.
Hop 8 Model-ablation implementation: model and ablation implement factories and
variants; training, eval, profile, and equivariance agents ack import and shape
evidence.
Hop 9 Early CI smoke: validator and entrypoint CI check exact paths, imports, main.py, uv commands, RCSB artifacts, DatasetRecord keys, and equivariance hooks before expensive
work.
Hop 10 Equivariance pre-report gate: independent referee runs synthetic and real batch transform tests; failures recall model or training. Hop 11 Training smoke: training runs bounded smoke for all model_ids and returns
history/checkpoint metadata consistency.
Hop 12 Full or bounded training: training runs approved budget and freezes
history/checkpoints.
Hop 13 Evaluation and split-leakage freeze: eval computes circular metrics, per-residue breakdowns, ablation results, predictions, and metrics_lineage. Hop 14 Profiling and sanity audit: profile publishes runtime, memory, training-step latency, and ratios; profile sanity reruns ratio and anomaly checks. Hop 15 Metrics integration: metrics integrator merges accuracy, training, data, RCSB, profile, ablation, and equivariance into results/metrics.json with required pointers. Hop 16 JSON consistency audit: independent referee recomputes best_epoch, best_val_loss, training times, ablation improvements, and required pointer status.
Hop 17 Plot generation: plots are regenerated from frozen JSON and plot data is
validated.
Hop 18 Index generation: index lists exact required and supporting artifacts; validator
checks coverage.
Hop 19 Report drafting: scicomm drafts only from frozen contracts and emits
ClaimsEvidenceMatrix.
Hop 20 Independent critique: claims referee, validator, RCSB referee, profile sanity, JSON consistency, quality, entrypoint CI, and log auditor audit report, index, metrics, profile, equivariance, data, logs, and paths. Hop 21 Targeted PatchHandoff recall: for every blocker, orchestrator recalls only the owner with narrowed_scope, issue_ids_to_close, changed files expected, and retest
criteria.
Hop 22 Retest: original opener plus validator or quality retests specific issue_ids;
affected downstream agents reack changed contracts.

### 中文（翻译）

8. 等变性裁判在科学传播撰写等变性声明之前,发布 EquivarianceAuditContract v2 及附带命令证据的 results/equivariance_tests.json。
9. 评估(Eval)代理在指标集成和报告精度声明之前,发布 ResultsContract v4 及 metrics_lineage。
10. 性能分析(Profile)代理在指标集成和速度或内存声明之前,发布 ProfilingContract v4 并验证精确的 src/profile.py。
11. 性能分析完整性裁判在科学传播撰写性能分析文本之前,审计 ProfilingContract 及指标运行时-内存合并结果。
12. 指标集成器仅在消耗来自评估、训练、性能分析、消融、数据、RCSB 和等变性的全部输入,且 results/metrics.json 包含所有必要指针后,才冻结 MetricsIntegrationContract。
13. JSON 一致性裁判在图表、索引摘要和报告被视为最终版本之前,审计已冻结的指标。
14. 科学传播代理向声明裁判发送 ClaimsEvidenceMatrix;所有声明阻断项路由至负责的专项代理或科学传播代理进行修复。
15. 验证器、质量、RCSB 裁判、性能分析完整性裁判、JSON 一致性裁判、声明裁判、入口点 CI 和日志审计器在每轮补丁后均进行审查,而非仅在补丁之前执行。

最少编排器-子代理跳数(hop):

跳 0 EvidenceRubric 扇出:向所有代理发送任务交付物、两两历史记录及当前 v3 文件证据。
跳 1 并行缺陷预检:各专项代理返回 self_audit_against_prior_defects 及 requests_for_agents。
跳 2 起草合约:管理员编译 RequiredPathManifest、InterfaceContract、MetricsPointerMatrix 及模式注册表。
跳 3 合约确认:相关消费方返回 ContractAckV4 或附字段级注释的 ChangeRequestV4。
跳 4 合约协调循环:编排器仅召回存在争议的生产方和消费方,针对争议字段和重测方法进行处理,直至冻结或显式豁免。
跳 5 RCSB 实现:数据代理实现查询/下载/缓存/元数据/划分,并返回 DataRCSBHandoffV4。
跳 6 RCSB 协议裁判门控:独立裁判审计查询状态、回退策略和过滤器验证;失败则召回数据代理或科学传播代理。
跳 7 数据-几何联署:数据代理和几何代理实现特征提取、残基化学、无泄漏检查及划分审查;评估代理确认划分结果。
跳 8 模型-消融实现:模型代理和消融代理实现工厂类及变体;训练、评估、性能分析和等变性代理确认导入与形状证据。
跳 9 早期 CI 冒烟测试:验证器和入口点 CI 在执行耗时工作之前,检查精确路径、导入、main.py、uv 命令、RCSB 制品、DatasetRecord 键及等变性钩子。
跳 10 等变性预报告门控:独立裁判运行合成批次和真实批次的变换测试;失败则召回模型代理或训练代理。
跳 11 训练冒烟测试:训练代理对所有 model_id 运行有界冒烟测试,并返回 history/checkpoint 元数据一致性结果。
跳 12 完整或有界训练:训练代理按批准预算运行并冻结 history/checkpoints。
跳 13 评估与划分泄漏冻结:评估代理计算循环指标、逐残基细分、消融结果、预测值及 metrics_lineage。
跳 14 性能分析与完整性审计:性能分析代理发布运行时、内存、训练步延迟及比率;性能分析完整性裁判重新执行比率和异常检查。
跳 15 指标集成:指标集成器将精度、训练、数据、RCSB、性能分析、消融和等变性结果合并至 results/metrics.json,并附带所有必要指针。
跳 16 JSON 一致性审计:独立裁判重新计算 best_epoch、best_val_loss、训练时长、消融提升幅度及必要指针状态。
跳 17 图表生成:从冻结的 JSON 重新生成图表,并验证图表数据。
跳 18 索引生成:索引列出所有必需和辅助制品;验证器检查覆盖情况。
跳 19 报告起草:科学传播代理仅依据冻结合约起草报告,并输出 ClaimsEvidenceMatrix。
跳 20 独立批评:声明裁判、验证器、RCSB 裁判、性能分析完整性裁判、JSON 一致性裁判、质量裁判、入口点 CI 和日志审计器对报告、索引、指标、性能分析、等变性、数据、日志及路径进行审计。
跳 21 定向 PatchHandoff 召回:对于每个阻断项,编排器仅召回负责方,并附上 narrowed_scope、issue_ids_to_close、预期变更文件及重测标准。
跳 22 重测:原始发现方联合验证器或质量裁判对特定 issue_ids 进行重测;受影响的下游代理重新确认变更后的合约。
