Hop 23 Final gate: repeat Hop 21 and Hop 22 until no blockers remain or waiver is
recorded with evidence and conservative report wording.
Recall triggers:
Recall interface steward or validator if any exact required deliverable path is missing, empty, unparsable, not listed in deliverables/index.json, or substituted by a differently named file; src/profile.py missing is always blocking. Recall data if RCSB query payload, endpoint, selected IDs, query attempts, metadata verification, fallback policy, download manifest, split manifest, data_card evidence, or deterministic extraction command are missing or report-only. Recall RCSB referee if query/fallback filter verification is absent or not independently audited. Recall geometry if χ1 atom tables, completeness rules, altloc policy, circular convention, or no-side-chain-input leakage evidence are missing. Recall model if transform test hooks, ModelRegistry imports, factory signatures, checkpoint keys, or equivariance/invariance language are
wrong. Recall equivariance referee if results/equivariance_tests.json lacks
command_results, synthetic and real batch cases, tolerances, pass/fail status, or evidence paths. Recall ablation or training if ablation has fewer than one invariant plus two equivariant variants without waiver, model_ids or checkpoint keys disagree, training histories are absent, best_epoch or best_val_loss disagree across artifacts, or main.py remains stale. Recall entrypoint CI if main.py was not checked after patches. Recall eval if median angular error, MAE, RMSE, per-residue counts, split leakage checks, prediction lengths, or metric_lineage pointers are missing or inconsistent. Recall profile if src/profile.py is absent, training-step profiling is absent without waiver, memory numbers are implausible without caveats, negative values are reported as usage, environment details are missing, timing CV is high without rerun/caveat, or speed ratios differ from profile JSON by more than 10 percent. Recall profile sanity if ratios, high-CV timings, or memory anomalies are not independently inventoried. Recall metrics integrator if results/metrics.json lacks runtime, profiling, memory, training_step_latency, throughput_ratio, source_artifact, consistency_checks, or equivariance summary fields. Recall JSON consistency referee if best_epoch, best_val_loss, training_time, ablation, profile, or data consistency is not audited. Recall scicomm if report text contradicts JSON metrics, histories, RCSB protocol, profile ratios, equivariance evidence, or plot captions. Recall claims referee if
quantitative or provenance claims are not inventoried. Recall log auditor or
orchestrator if logs.txt lacks roster, call graph, contract acks, handoffs, recall history, uv commands, patch retests, or final_gate_decision. Recall quality if final
approval lacks concrete evidence paths.
Conflict resolution:
If two agents disagree, the orchestrator creates a ConflictRecord with positions, evidence paths or JSON pointers, affected artifacts, downstream contract fields, decision, updated consumers, and reack requirements. Prefer scientifically conservative choices: do not claim unverified RCSB filters, do not hide fallback, do not report unstable profiling ratios without caveats, and do not publish metrics that disagree across artifacts. Document decisions in logs.txt and limitations in research_report.md.
Final gate conditions:
Pass only if all required files exist at exact paths including src/profile.py; deliverables/index.json lists exact required artifacts and supporting provenance artifacts; RCSB acquisition is reproducible through successful query-derived IDs or explicitly verified fallback with per-ID filter evidence and correct report wording; DatasetRecord excludes side-chain coordinates from inputs; formal equivariance checks are present in results/equivariance_tests.json with command_results, synthetic and real batch evidence, model_ids, tolerances, and pass/fail status or an explicit waiver; results/metrics.json contains MAE, median, RMSE, per-residue counts, best_epoch and best_val_loss consistent with histories/checkpoints, total_training_time_s, runtime, profiling, RSS/CUDA/parameter memory summary, training_step_latency, throughput ratios, source-artifact pointers, consistency_checks, and equivariance summary; results/ablation_results.json contains required invariant and equivariant variants and matches metrics; profiling includes forward and training-step latency, robust memory caveats, stability flags, parameter/RSS/CUDA estimates where applicable, and model-to-model ratios; research_report.md quantitative claims match JSON within tolerance, training-time claims match histories, RCSB wording matches protocol audit, speed claims match profile ratios, and no stale unsupported limitations remain; all five required plots are non-empty and generated from frozen JSON; main.py is not stale hello-world if present; uv run smoke commands are logged; logs.txt contains agent roster, call graph, contract acks, handoffs, recall history, patch retests, commands, unresolved risks, and final_gate_decision.

### 中文（翻译）

跳转节点(Hop)23 最终关卡:重复 Hop 21 和 Hop 22,直至无阻塞项,或阻塞项已记录豁免并附有证据及保守措辞的报告。

召回触发条件:
若有任何必需交付物路径缺失、为空、无法解析、未列入 deliverables/index.json,或被同名不符的文件替代,则召回接口管理员(interface steward)或验证员(validator);src/profile.py 缺失始终为阻塞项。若 RCSB 查询载荷、端点、已选 ID、查询尝试记录、元数据验证、回退策略、下载清单、分割清单、data_card 证据或确定性提取命令缺失或仅出现在报告中,则召回数据智能体(data agent)。若查询/回退过滤器验证缺失或未经独立审计,则召回 RCSB 裁判员(RCSB referee)。若 χ1 原子表、完整性规则、替代位点(altloc)策略、环形约定或无侧链输入泄漏证据缺失,则召回几何智能体(geometry agent)。若变换测试钩子、ModelRegistry 导入、工厂签名、检查点键,或等变性/不变性表述有误,则召回模型智能体(model agent)。若 results/equivariance_tests.json 缺少 command_results、合成与真实批次案例、容差、通过/失败状态或证据路径,则召回等变性裁判员(equivariance referee)。若消融实验少于一个不变变体加两个等变变体且无豁免,model_ids 或检查点键不一致,训练历史缺失,best_epoch 或 best_val_loss 在各制品间不一致,或 main.py 仍为陈旧版本,则召回消融(ablation)或训练智能体。若 main.py 在补丁后未重新检查,则召回入口点 CI 智能体(entrypoint CI agent)。若中位角误差、MAE、RMSE、每残基计数、分割泄漏检查、预测长度或 metric_lineage 指针缺失或不一致,则召回评估智能体(eval agent)。若 src/profile.py 缺失、训练步骤性能分析缺失且无豁免、内存数值不合理且无注意事项、负值被报告为使用量、环境详情缺失、时间变异系数(CV)偏高且无重跑/注意事项,或速度比率与 profile JSON 相差超过 10%,则召回 profile 智能体。若比率、高 CV 时间或内存异常未经独立盘点,则召回 profile 健全性检查员(profile sanity agent)。若 results/metrics.json 缺少 runtime、profiling、memory、training_step_latency、throughput_ratio、source_artifact、consistency_checks 或等变性汇总字段,则召回指标集成员(metrics integrator)。若 best_epoch、best_val_loss、training_time、消融、profile 或数据一致性未经审计,则召回 JSON 一致性裁判员(JSON consistency referee)。若报告正文与 JSON 指标、训练历史、RCSB 协议、profile 比率、等变性证据或图表标题相矛盾,则召回科学传播智能体(scicomm agent)。若定量或溯源声明未经盘点,则召回声明裁判员(claims referee)。若 logs.txt 缺少智能体名单、调用图、合约确认(contract acks)、交接记录、召回历史、uv 命令、补丁重测或 final_gate_decision,则召回日志审计员(log auditor)或编排器(orchestrator)。若最终批准缺少具体证据路径,则召回质量智能体(quality agent)。

冲突解决:
若两个智能体意见不一致,编排器创建一条 ConflictRecord,记录各方立场、证据路径或 JSON 指针、受影响制品、下游合约字段、决策、更新的消费方及重新确认要求。优先选择科学保守的选项:不声明未验证的 RCSB 过滤器,不隐藏回退情况,不在无注意事项的情况下报告不稳定的 profiling 比率,不发布在各制品间不一致的指标。将决策记录于 logs.txt,将局限性记录于 research_report.md。

最终关卡条件:
仅在满足以下所有条件时方可通过:所有必需文件均位于精确路径(包括 src/profile.py);deliverables/index.json 列出了确切的必需制品及其溯源支持制品;RCSB 数据获取可通过成功的查询衍生 ID 复现,或通过带有每 ID 过滤器证据及正确报告措辞的显式验证回退复现;DatasetRecord 从输入中排除侧链坐标;results/equivariance_tests.json 中存在正式等变性检查,包含 command_results、合成与真实批次证据、model_ids、容差以及通过/失败状态,或附有明确豁免;results/metrics.json 包含 MAE、中位数、RMSE、每残基计数、与训练历史/检查点一致的 best_epoch 和 best_val_loss、total_training_time_s、runtime、profiling、RSS/CUDA/参数内存摘要、training_step_latency、吞吐量比率、来源制品指针、consistency_checks 及等变性摘要;results/ablation_results.json 包含必需的不变变体和等变变体并与指标一致;profiling 包含前向与训练步骤延迟、稳健的内存注意事项、稳定性标志、参数/RSS/CUDA 估计(适用时)以及模型间比率;research_report.md 中的定量声明在容差范围内与 JSON 一致,训练时间声明与历史记录一致,RCSB 措辞与协议审计一致,速度声明与 profile 比率一致,且不存在陈旧的无依据局限性表述;所有五个必需图表均非空且由冻结 JSON 生成;若 main.py 存在则不应为陈旧的 hello-world;uv run 冒烟命令已记录;logs.txt 包含智能体名单、调用图、合约确认、交接记录、召回历史、补丁重测、命令、未解决风险及 final_gate_decision。

  - [1x] 验证/测试薄弱

4. 指令

将多智能体(multi-agent)设计从 v1 改进至 v2,通过构建相对于单智能体执行的真正多智能体优势,提升查询可交付成果的质量。保留原始意图和所要求的可交付成果,但需针对证据揭示的弱点进行改进。

- 显式强化"输出给协调器(orchestrator)"的契约(contract),使下游智能体能够消费结构化输出。
- 显式增加协调器与子智能体之间的反馈循环(feedback loop):允许协调器以更窄的后续范围和更新的验收标准重新调用已调用过的子智能体。最重要的是,优先推进能够创造真正多智能体优势的改进(例如,专家并行、跨智能体验证、智能体间通信以及冲突解决循环)。

反馈质量要求:
- 具体且有证据支撑。
- 引用所提供证据中的具体文件/指标/历史信号。

D.2. ML Research Task (Quantitative)
Quantitative Research Task
Task. Investigate whether alternative data from Wikipedia pageviews can improve short-term return
prediction for large-cap equities.
Universe. Dow 30 constituents (from Wikipedia) and SPY as benchmark.
Data sources.
• Wikimedia REST API for daily Wikipedia pageviews
• Yahoo Finance for prices and trading volume
• FRED for optional macroeconomic controls
Feature construction. Build features including:
• Abnormal pageview surge (z-score relative to trailing baseline)
• Attention momentum
• Day-of-week adjustments
• Interactions with earnings dates
Use a public earnings-calendar source (e.g., Nasdaq earnings calendar scrape). If scraping is unstable,
document fallback procedures.
Modeling and evaluation. Train a leakage-safe model to predict next-day and next-week returns and/or volatility. Evaluate using purged time-series cross-validation. Backtest a market-neutral long/short attention strategy with transaction costs. Include a causal-style placebo test by shifting
pageviews by +7 days and verifying that the predictive signal disappears.
Deliverables.
1. research_report.md as a conference-style paper (7–10 pages equivalent).
2. deliverables/index.json.
3. deliverables/plots/ containing experimental visualizations and backtest figures.
4. Reproducible Python code for data collection, feature engineering, modeling, and evaluation.
5. results/metrics.json including:
• IC / RankIC
• Predictive 𝑅2
• Strategy Sharpe ratio
• Beta
• Maximum drawdown
• Turnover
• Transaction costs
• Placebo-test metrics
6. results/feature_mapping.json documenting page-to-ticker mappings and confi-
dence scores, and results/model_comparison.json comparing baseline and attention-
augmented models.

### 中文（翻译）

### D.2. 机器学习研究任务(定量研究)

**定量研究任务**

**任务。** 研究来自维基百科页面浏览量的替代数据能否改善大盘股的短期收益预测。

**研究范围。** 道琼斯 30 指数成分股(来自维基百科)及 SPY 作为基准。

**数据来源。**
- Wikimedia REST API,用于获取每日维基百科页面浏览量
- Yahoo Finance,用于获取价格和交易量
- FRED,用于可选的宏观经济控制变量

**特征构建。** 构建以下特征:
- 异常页面浏览量激增(相对于滞后基准的 z-score)
- 注意力动量(attention momentum)
- 星期效应调整
- 与财报日期的交互项

使用公开的财报日历来源(例如,抓取纳斯达克财报日历)。若抓取不稳定,记录备用方案。

**建模与评估。** 训练一个无泄露的模型,预测次日和次周收益及/或波动率。使用净化时间序列交叉验证(purged time-series cross-validation)进行评估。在考虑交易成本的情况下,对市场中性多空注意力策略进行回测。加入因果式安慰剂测试(causal-style placebo test),将页面浏览量向后平移 +7 天,并验证预测信号消失。

**可交付成果。**

1. research_report.md,以会议论文格式(相当于 7–10 页)。
2. deliverables/index.json。
3. deliverables/plots/,包含实验可视化图表和回测图表。
4. 可重复的 Python 代码,用于数据收集、特征工程、建模和评估。
5. results/metrics.json,包含:
   - IC / RankIC
   - 预测 $R^2$
   - 策略夏普比率(Sharpe ratio)
   - Beta
   - 最大回撤(Maximum drawdown)
   - 换手率(Turnover)
   - 交易成本
   - 安慰剂测试指标
6. results/feature_mapping.json,记录页面到股票代码的映射及置信度分数;results/model_comparison.json,对基线模型与注意力增强模型进行比较。

E. Evaluator Prompt
We provide a system and user prompt of LLM evaluator Leval to do a pairwise comparison between
the two outputs:
Pairwise Comparison: system prompt
You are a {senior quantitative researcher} comparing two completed submissions for the same assignment. {Judge with a quant lens: empirical rigor, OOS and leakage discipline, uncertainty and baselines, reproducibility, and consistency between narrative and numeric artifacts.} You are strict, practical, and evidence-driven, matching the spirit of an internal review between two
junior/mid deliverables.
Rules:
• The # Task block is the only contract for what must be produced. Workspace sections are
evidence only; do not invent file contents.
• Howtasksarewritteninthisrepo’scatalogs(multi_agent_design/*/queries.json).
It is usually one document with the assignment first, then sections such as Data sources, Data acquisition, Success criteria, and almost always a Deliverables: block (sometimes Deliverable: singular, or inline Deliverables: (1) ...). Use every explicitly required artifact named there (and any equally explicit requirement in the narrative above it) as the checklist—not folder names or assumptions.
• Prefer concrete citations (paths, JSON keys, report sections) over generic praise.
• Choose A or B when one submission clearly better satisfies the task across deliverables, rigor,
reproducibility, and alignment. Use tie only when they are genuinely comparable overall (not merely different).
• Return ONLY valid JSON with the keys specified in the user message (no markdown fences,
no prose outside JSON).

E. 评估器提示词(Prompt)
我们提供大语言模型评估器 $L_\text{eval}$ 的系统提示词和用户提示词,用于对两个输出进行两两比较(pairwise comparison):

两两比较:系统提示词
你是一名{资深量化研究员},正在比较同一任务的两份已完成提交。{以量化视角进行评判:经验严谨性、样本外(OOS)测试与数据泄露规范、不确定性与基线设置、可复现性,以及叙述与数值产出之间的一致性。}你作风严格、务实、以证据为导向,契合对两名初中级研究员交付物进行内部评审的精神。

规则:
• # Task 块是唯一规定必须产出内容的合约(contract)。工作区各节仅作为证据,不得凭空捏造文件内容。
• 任务在本仓库目录(multi_agent_design/*/queries.json)中的编写方式:通常为一份文档,先给出任务说明,然后包含"数据来源"、"数据获取"、"成功标准"等节,以及几乎必有的 Deliverables: 块(有时写作单数 Deliverable:,或行内写法 Deliverables: (1) ...)。以其中明确要求的所有产出物(以及其上方叙述中同等明确的要求)作为核查清单,而非以文件夹名称或假设推断。
• 优先引用具体出处(路径、JSON 键、报告章节),而非泛泛称赞。
• 当某份提交在交付物、严谨性、可复现性和任务契合度方面整体上明显更优时,选择 A 或 B。仅当两份提交整体上确实旗鼓相当(而非仅仅各有侧重)时,才使用平局(tie)。
• 仅返回符合用户消息中指定键的合法 JSON(无 Markdown 围栏,JSON 外无正文)。

### File: ‘requirements.txt‘ (contents) ### File: ‘main.py‘ (contents) ## Comparison rubric (relative) Judge which submission better fulfills the same task. Consider, as in the single-submission senior
review:
1. Deliverable coverage — map the task’s Deliverables / Deliverable: / inline deliverables
list (and any other explicit output requirements in ‘# Task‘) to evidence; mark gaps or
placeholders.
2. Numerical/empirical rigor — appropriate methodology, baselines, honest limitations; con-
sistency between report and metrics when applicable.
3. Reproducibility — dependencies, entry points, seeds, documented data or generation.
4. Presentation — report structure, clarity, figure integration (infer from paths and excerpts).
5. Engineering — layout, modularity, readability from excerpts and tree.
6. Task alignment — penalize solving the wrong problem or drifting from the stated objective.
## Output JSON schema (exact keys) { "winner": "A" | "B" | "tie", "rationale": "<string, cite concrete evidence from both workspaces>" }
Return JSON only.
F. Distribution of normalized cost, output tokens, and cache read/write

### 中文（翻译）

### 文件：'requirements.txt'（内容） ### 文件：'main.py'（内容） ## 对比评分标准（相对） 判断哪份提交更好地完成了同一任务。评估维度与单份提交的资深审阅相同：
1. 可交付物覆盖度——将任务中 Deliverables / Deliverable: / 内联可交付物列表（以及"# Task"中其他明确的输出要求）逐一映射到证据；标注缺失或占位内容。
2. 数值/实证严谨性——方法论是否合理、基线是否充分、局限性是否诚实说明；报告与指标之间的一致性。
3. 可复现性——依赖项、入口点、随机种子、数据文档或生成方式。
4. 呈现质量——报告结构、清晰度、图表整合情况（从路径和摘录推断）。
5. 工程质量——目录布局、模块化程度、代码可读性（从摘录和目录树判断）。
6. 任务对齐度——若解决了错误问题或偏离了既定目标则予以扣分。
## 输出 JSON 模式（精确键名） { "winner": "A" | "B" | "tie", "rationale": "<字符串，引用两个工作区的具体证据>" }
仅返回 JSON。
F. 归一化成本、输出 token 数及缓存读写量的分布
